<!doctype html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover">
<title>Channel Manager API · Fourvenues</title>

<style>
/* ==========================================================================
   Fourvenues — design tokens + base components (dark)
   Pega este bloque entero al principio del <style> de cualquier página.
   Valores verificados contra fourvenues.com y el design system de producto.
   ========================================================================== */

:root{
  color-scheme: dark;

  /* superficies */
  --bg:#000000;
  --surface:#121214;
  --surface-2:#1c1c20;
  --line:rgba(255,255,255,.08);
  --line-soft:rgba(255,255,255,.055);

  /* texto */
  --ink:#fafafa;
  --ink-2:#d4d4d8;
  --muted:#9c9d9f;

  /* marca */
  --brand:#8493f5;          /* primario sobre negro */
  --brand-strong:#6770ed;
  --brand-deep:#4f4de1;     /* índigo núcleo de marca */
  --brand-pale:#4f4de1;     /* segunda serie en gráficos */
  --brand-fill:rgba(132,147,245,.22);

  /* acento (importes negativos, comisiones, avisos) */
  --accent:#f59e0b;

  --focus:#8493f5;
  --beam:#6770ed;

  --maxw:1020px;
}

html{overflow-x:hidden}

body{
  margin:0;
  background:var(--bg);
  color:var(--ink);
  font-family:Inter,ui-sans-serif,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI",Roboto,Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif;
  font-size:17px;
  line-height:1.5;
  letter-spacing:-.011em;
  -webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased;
  overflow-x:hidden;
}
*{box-sizing:border-box}
img{max-width:100%}

/* fuente display: solo para EL dato grande de una sección, nunca para texto corrido */


/* --------------------------------------------------------------- layout */

.wrap{max-width:var(--maxw);margin:0 auto;padding:0 22px;position:relative;isolation:isolate}
main{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:clamp(56px,8vw,104px);padding-top:clamp(56px,8vw,104px)}
section{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:22px;position:relative}

/* halo morado de cabecera: va dentro de .wrap, con z-index:-1 */
.backdrop{
  position:absolute;top:0;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);
  width:100vw;max-width:1800px;height:840px;z-index:-1;pointer-events:none;overflow:hidden;
  background:
    radial-gradient(46% 34% at 50% -8%, rgba(225,232,254,.20), transparent 64%),
    radial-gradient(120% 74% at 50% -12%, rgba(79,77,225,.42), transparent 62%),
    radial-gradient(64% 48% at 88% 8%, rgba(103,112,237,.26), transparent 72%);
  -webkit-mask-image:linear-gradient(to bottom,#000 0%,#000 48%,transparent 100%);
  mask-image:linear-gradient(to bottom,#000 0%,#000 48%,transparent 100%);
}
/* haces de luz diagonales, al ángulo de la itálica del logo */
.backdrop .beams{position:absolute;top:0;left:-70px;width:calc(100% + 140px);height:100%;opacity:.26;filter:blur(28px)}
.backdrop .beams.back{opacity:.13}
.beams stop{stop-color:var(--beam)}
@keyframes swell{from{transform:translateX(-26px)}to{transform:translateX(26px)}}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion:no-preference){
  .backdrop .beams.front{animation:swell 86s ease-in-out infinite alternate}
  .backdrop .beams.back{animation:swell 132s ease-in-out infinite alternate-reverse}
}
/* halo suelto detrás de una sección */
.glow{
  position:absolute;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);width:min(1200px,100vw);height:700px;
  z-index:-1;pointer-events:none;filter:blur(10px);
  background:radial-gradient(50% 50% at 50% 50%, rgba(79,77,225,.30), transparent 70%);
}

/* ----------------------------------------------------------- tipografía */

.eyebrow{
  font-size:11.5px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.17em;text-transform:uppercase;
  color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:20px;
}
.eyebrow b{color:var(--brand);font-weight:600}

h1{
  font-weight:600;font-size:clamp(36px,6.2vw,66px);line-height:1.04;letter-spacing:-.032em;
  margin:0 auto;max-width:19ch;text-wrap:balance;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;
}
/* la cifra protagonista del titular, en degradado de marca */
h1 .out{
  background:linear-gradient(92deg,#e1e8fe 0%,#a8bafa 26%,#8493f5 62%,#6770ed 100%);
  -webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;color:transparent;
}
h2{font-weight:600;font-size:clamp(26px,3.7vw,42px);line-height:1.1;letter-spacing:-.03em;margin:0;text-wrap:balance}
h2 .soft{color:var(--muted)}   /* titulares a dos tonos: blanco + gris */

.lede{max-width:40ch;margin:22px auto 0;color:var(--muted);font-size:clamp(16.5px,2vw,20px);line-height:1.45;letter-spacing:-.012em}
.sec-head{text-align:center;max-width:36ch;margin:0 auto;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px}
.sec-note{margin:0;color:var(--muted);font-size:16px;line-height:1.45}

/* dato grande de sección */
.bigstat-v{
  font-family:Moderniz,Inter,sans-serif;font-weight:400;
  font-size:clamp(42px,6vw,66px);letter-spacing:-.01em;line-height:1;
  background:linear-gradient(92deg,#e1e8fe,#a8bafa 30%,#8493f5 65%,#6770ed);
  -webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;color:transparent;
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------- componentes */

.card{background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:22px;padding:clamp(22px,3.2vw,38px)}

.chip{background:rgba(255,255,255,.04);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:980px;
      padding:7px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:var(--ink-2);white-space:nowrap}
.chip b{font-weight:600;color:var(--ink)}

.pill{appearance:none;border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--surface-2);color:var(--ink);
      border-radius:980px;padding:7px 16px;font:inherit;font-size:13.5px;letter-spacing:-.01em;cursor:pointer}
.pill[aria-pressed="true"]{background:linear-gradient(90deg,#4f4de1,#6770ed);border-color:transparent;color:#fff;font-weight:600}
.pill:focus-visible{outline:3px solid var(--focus);outline-offset:3px}

.swatch{width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:3px;flex:none}
.sw-brand{background:var(--brand)}
.sw-pale{background:var(--brand-pale)}
.sw-accent{background:var(--accent)}

/* barras de datos */
.bar{height:8px;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#6770ed,#8493f5);border-radius:0 4px 4px 0}
.bar.rest{background:var(--brand-fill)}

/* tablas */
table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse}
th,td{padding:13px 10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-soft);text-align:right;
      font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;font-size:15.5px;letter-spacing:-.01em}
th{font-size:12px;font-weight:600;color:var(--muted);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);
   white-space:nowrap;letter-spacing:.04em;text-transform:uppercase}
th:first-child,td:first-child,th[colspan],td.left{text-align:left}
tbody tr:last-child td{border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
tfoot td{border-bottom:0;padding-top:16px;font-weight:600}
.t-scroll{overflow-x:auto;margin:0 -6px}   /* envuelve SIEMPRE las tablas anchas */

/* marco de foto: velo a negro por abajo + capa índigo en diagonal */
.frame{position:relative;border-radius:22px;overflow:hidden;border:1px solid var(--line);
       box-shadow:0 40px 120px -40px rgba(79,77,225,.65), 0 2px 0 0 rgba(255,255,255,.05) inset}
.frame img{display:block;width:100%;height:auto}
.frame::after{
  content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;
  background:
    linear-gradient(to bottom, rgba(0,0,0,.34) 0%, rgba(0,0,0,.05) 28%, rgba(0,0,0,.42) 68%, rgba(0,0,0,.97) 100%),
    linear-gradient(200deg, rgba(79,77,225,.32), transparent 58%);
}
/* cualquier texto sobre la foto necesita z-index:2, el velo es un ::after y pinta por encima */
.frame .overlay{z-index:2;position:absolute;inset:auto 0 0 0;padding:clamp(24px,4vw,46px)}

/* panel de cierre: bloom morado desde abajo, sin borde */
.closing{
  position:relative;border-radius:28px;overflow:hidden;text-align:center;
  padding:clamp(40px,6vw,74px) 24px clamp(34px,5vw,54px);
  background:
    radial-gradient(78% 128% at 50% 122%, rgba(225,232,254,.34) 0%, rgba(168,186,250,.46) 13%,
                    rgba(103,112,237,.88) 38%, rgba(56,54,140,.52) 70%, rgba(0,0,0,0) 100%),
    #08080c;
}

/* logos */
.fv-logo{height:clamp(17px,2.4vw,21px);width:auto}      /* lockup, ratio 246x28 */
.fv-inline{height:.82em;width:auto;vertical-align:-.03em;margin:0 .14em}
.partner-logo{height:clamp(30px,4.6vw,40px);width:auto;opacity:.96}
.brand-x{color:var(--muted);font-size:17px;font-weight:400}

/* ---------------- página: Channel Manager API ---------------- */
:root{--maxw:1200px;--top:60px;box-sizing:border-box;padding-top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px);padding-bottom:env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)}
html{scroll-padding-top:calc(var(--top) + 64px + env(safe-area-inset-top,0px));scroll-behavior:smooth}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){html{scroll-behavior:auto}}
body{background:#000000}
html[lang="es"] .l-en,html[lang="en"] .l-es{display:none!important}
a{color:var(--brand)}
.muted{color:var(--muted);margin:0 0 18px;font-size:15px}

/* barra superior */
.top{position:sticky;top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px);z-index:40;background:rgba(0,0,0,.72);
     -webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(14px);backdrop-filter:blur(14px);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-soft)}
.top-in{max-width:var(--maxw);margin:0 auto;height:var(--top);padding:0 22px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px}
.top-logo{display:flex}
.top-sep{width:1px;height:18px;background:var(--line)}
.top-name{font-size:14px;color:var(--ink-2);font-weight:500;white-space:nowrap}
.grow{flex:1}
.search-btn{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--muted);
  border-radius:980px;padding:7px 10px 7px 13px;font:inherit;font-size:13.5px;cursor:pointer;min-width:160px}
.search-btn:hover{color:var(--ink);border-color:rgba(255,255,255,.16)}
.search-btn .sb-t{flex:1;text-align:left}
kbd{font-family:inherit;font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:6px;padding:1px 6px;background:var(--surface-2)}
.lang{display:flex;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:980px;padding:3px}
.lang button{appearance:none;border:0;background:transparent;color:var(--muted);font:inherit;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;
  padding:5px 11px;border-radius:980px;cursor:pointer;letter-spacing:.02em}
.lang button[aria-pressed="true"]{background:linear-gradient(90deg,#4f4de1,#6770ed);color:#ffffff}
button:focus-visible,a:focus-visible,input:focus-visible{outline:3px solid var(--focus);outline-offset:2px}
@media (max-width:640px){.top-sep,.top-name{display:none}.search-btn{min-width:0}.search-btn .sb-t,.search-btn kbd{display:none}}

/* hero */
.hero{text-align:center;padding:clamp(52px,8vw,96px) 0 clamp(30px,4vw,44px)}
.hero h1{max-width:20ch}
.hero h1 .soft{color:var(--muted)}
.chips{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:center;gap:8px;margin-top:30px}

/* tabs */
.tabs{position:sticky;top:calc(var(--top) + env(safe-area-inset-top,0px));z-index:30;display:flex;gap:4px;overflow-x:auto;scrollbar-width:none;
  padding:10px 0;margin:0 -22px;padding-left:22px;padding-right:22px;background:linear-gradient(#000000 72%,rgba(0,0,0,0))}
.tabs::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none}
.tabs button{appearance:none;flex:none;border:1px solid transparent;background:transparent;color:var(--muted);font:inherit;font-size:14.5px;
  font-weight:500;padding:9px 16px;border-radius:980px;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;letter-spacing:-.01em}
.tabs button:hover{color:var(--ink);background:rgba(255,255,255,.04)}
.tabs button[aria-selected="true"]{color:#ffffff;background:var(--surface-2);border-color:var(--line)}
.tabs button[aria-selected="true"] span::before{content:"";display:inline-block;width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--brand);margin-right:8px;vertical-align:2px}

/* paneles */
main.docs{display:block;padding-top:26px;gap:0}
.panel{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:22px}
.panel.has-side{display:grid;grid-template-columns:230px minmax(0,1fr);gap:40px;align-items:start}
.panel[hidden]{display:none!important}
.panel-main{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:22px;min-width:0}
.side{position:sticky;top:calc(var(--top) + 76px + env(safe-area-inset-top,0px))}
.side-nav{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:2px;border-left:1px solid var(--line)}
.side-nav a{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:baseline;color:var(--muted);text-decoration:none;font-size:14px;line-height:1.35;
  padding:8px 12px;margin-left:-1px;border-left:2px solid transparent}
.side-nav a:hover{color:var(--ink)}
.side-nav a.on{color:var(--ink);border-left-color:var(--brand)}
.num{font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;color:var(--brand);font-weight:600;font-size:12.5px;flex:none}
@media (max-width:900px){
  .panel.has-side{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:18px;align-items:stretch}
  .side{position:static}
  .side-nav{flex-direction:row;flex-wrap:wrap;border-left:0;gap:6px}
  .side-nav a{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:980px;padding:6px 12px;margin:0;font-size:13px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.03)}
  .side-nav a.on{border-color:var(--brand)}
}

.tab-head{padding:6px 0 4px}
.tab-head .eyebrow{margin-bottom:10px}
.tab-h{font-size:clamp(24px,3.2vw,34px)}

/* contenido de flujo */
.card{scroll-margin-top:calc(var(--top) + 70px)}
.card .eyebrow{margin-bottom:12px}
.flow h2{font-size:clamp(23px,3vw,32px);margin-bottom:6px}
.flow-body,.card{font-size:16px;line-height:1.6;color:var(--ink-2)}
.card p{margin:14px 0}
.card strong{color:var(--ink);font-weight:600}
h3.sub{font-size:18px;font-weight:600;color:var(--ink);letter-spacing:-.015em;margin:34px 0 4px;padding-top:26px;border-top:1px solid var(--line-soft)}
.card ul,.card ol{margin:12px 0;padding-left:22px}
.card li{margin:7px 0}
.card li::marker{color:var(--brand)}
.card ol li::marker{font-weight:600;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
code{font-family:monospace;font-size:.86em;background:rgba(132,147,245,.12);color:#c9d5fc;border-radius:6px;padding:.12em .42em;word-break:break-word}
code.ep{display:inline-flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;white-space:nowrap;word-break:normal}
.m{font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.04em;border-radius:4px;padding:1px 5px;font-family:Inter,sans-serif}
.m-get{background:rgba(13,148,136,.22);color:#e4e4e7}
.m-post{background:rgba(79,77,225,.45);color:#ffffff}
.m-put,.m-patch{background:rgba(245,158,11,.22);color:#f59e0b}
.m-delete{background:rgba(225,29,72,.22);color:#e4e4e7}
a.xref{text-decoration:none;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(132,147,245,.35)}
a.xref:hover{border-bottom-color:var(--brand)}

/* tablas de documentación: todo a la izquierda */
.card table{margin:16px 0;min-width:520px}
.card th,.card td{text-align:left;font-size:14.5px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.5;white-space:normal}
.card th{white-space:nowrap}
.card td:first-child{color:var(--ink);white-space:nowrap}
.card td:first-child code{white-space:nowrap}
@media (max-width:640px){.card td:first-child{white-space:normal}}
.t-scroll{overflow-x:auto;margin:0}
tbody tr:hover td{background:rgba(255,255,255,.015)}

/* árbol de catálogo */
.tree{list-style:none;padding:0!important;margin:16px 0!important}
.tree li{position:relative;margin:0!important;padding:7px 0 7px calc(var(--d) * 26px + 20px);color:var(--ink-2)}
.tree li::before{content:"";position:absolute;left:calc(var(--d) * 26px + 4px);top:50%;width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:2px;
  background:var(--brand);transform:translateY(-50%);opacity:calc(1 - var(--d) * .28)}

/* código */
.code{background:#0b0b0b;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;margin:18px 0;overflow:hidden}
.code-bar{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;padding:8px 10px 8px 16px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-soft);background:rgba(255,255,255,.02)}
.code-lang{font-size:11.5px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--muted)}
.copy{appearance:none;border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--surface-2);color:var(--ink-2);font:inherit;font-size:12.5px;border-radius:8px;padding:4px 10px;cursor:pointer}
.copy:hover{color:#ffffff}
.copy.ok{color:#ffffff;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#4f4de1,#6770ed);border-color:transparent}
.code pre{margin:0;padding:16px 18px;overflow-x:auto;font-family:monospace;font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.65;color:#d4d4d8;tab-size:2}
.code pre code{background:none;padding:0;color:inherit;font-size:inherit;word-break:normal}
.t-k{color:#8493f5}.t-s{color:#c9d5fc}.t-n{color:#f59e0b}.t-kw{color:#a8bafa;font-weight:600}.t-f{color:#71717a}.t-c{color:#71717a;font-style:italic}
.seg{display:flex;gap:2px;background:var(--surface-2);border-radius:8px;padding:2px}
.seg button{appearance:none;border:0;background:transparent;color:var(--muted);font:inherit;font-size:12.5px;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:6px;cursor:pointer}
.seg button.on{background:#27272a;color:#ffffff}

/* avisos */
.note{display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;border-radius:14px;padding:14px 16px;margin:18px 0;border:1px solid var(--line);background:rgba(132,147,245,.07)}
.note p{margin:0!important}
.note-i{flex:none;width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:50%;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;background:rgba(132,147,245,.22);color:var(--brand);margin-top:1px}
.note.warn{background:rgba(245,158,11,.07);border-color:rgba(245,158,11,.28)}
.note.warn .note-i{background:rgba(245,158,11,.2);color:var(--accent)}

/* checklist */
.check{margin-top:34px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:16px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.02);padding:6px 18px 12px}
.check-h{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.17em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--muted);padding:12px 0 6px}
.check-n{letter-spacing:0;text-transform:none;font-size:13px;color:var(--brand);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.check ul{list-style:none;padding:0!important;margin:0!important}
.check li{margin:0!important;border-top:1px solid var(--line-soft)}
.check li:first-child{border-top:0}
.check label{display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;padding:10px 0;cursor:pointer}
.check input{position:absolute;opacity:0;pointer-events:none}
.check .box{flex:none;width:19px;height:19px;border-radius:6px;border:1.5px solid #52525b;margin-top:3px;display:grid;place-items:center;transition:background .15s}
.check input:focus-visible + .box{outline:3px solid var(--focus);outline-offset:2px}
.check input:checked + .box{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#4f4de1,#8493f5);border-color:transparent}
.check input:checked + .box::after{content:"";width:9px;height:5px;border:2px solid #ffffff;border-top:0;border-right:0;transform:rotate(-45deg) translate(1px,-1px)}
.check input:checked ~ span:not(.box){color:var(--muted);text-decoration:line-through;text-decoration-color:rgba(156,157,159,.5)}

/* ruta en Empezar */
.route-pick{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin:4px 0 20px}
.route-q{color:var(--ink);font-weight:600;margin-right:6px;font-size:15.5px}
.route{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(250px,1fr));gap:12px;margin-bottom:6px}
.route-g{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:16px;padding:14px 14px 10px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.02);transition:opacity .2s,border-color .2s}
.route-h{font-size:12px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.08em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:6px}
.route-note{font-size:13px!important;margin:0 0 6px!important;color:var(--accent)}
.route-list{display:flex;flex-direction:column}
.route-f{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:baseline;text-decoration:none;color:var(--ink);padding:6px 0;font-size:14.5px;line-height:1.35}
.route-f:hover{color:var(--brand)}
.route.filter .route-g{opacity:.28}
.route.filter .route-g.hit{opacity:1;border-color:rgba(132,147,245,.45)}
.pill.ghost{margin-top:18px;background:transparent}

/* progreso */
.prog{display:flex;flex-direction:column}
.prog-row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:28px minmax(0,1fr) 120px 44px;gap:12px;align-items:center;padding:9px 0;border-top:1px solid var(--line-soft);text-decoration:none;color:var(--ink-2);font-size:14.5px}
.prog-row:first-child{border-top:0}
.prog-row:hover{color:var(--ink)}
.prog-bar{height:6px;border-radius:4px;background:var(--brand-fill);overflow:hidden}
.prog-bar i{display:block;height:100%;width:0;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#6770ed,#8493f5);transition:width .3s}
.prog-n{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--muted);text-align:right;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
@media (max-width:560px){.prog-row{grid-template-columns:28px minmax(0,1fr) 44px}.prog-bar{display:none}}
.chipref{display:inline-block;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;border:1px solid var(--line);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)!important;border-radius:980px;padding:1px 8px;margin:0 2px 2px 0}

/* cierre */
.closing{margin-top:clamp(64px,9vw,120px)}
.closing h2{margin:22px auto 0;max-width:22ch}
.close-p{color:var(--ink-2);margin:14px 0 22px}
.cta{display:inline-block;color:#ffffff;text-decoration:none;font-weight:600;background:rgba(0,0,0,.35);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.18);border-radius:980px;padding:11px 22px}
.cta:hover{background:rgba(0,0,0,.55)}
.foot{text-align:center;color:#52525b;font-size:13px;padding:30px 0 40px}

/* búsqueda */
.search{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:80;background:rgba(0,0,0,.66);-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(6px);backdrop-filter:blur(6px);
  display:flex;justify-content:center;align-items:flex-start;padding:calc(10vh + env(safe-area-inset-top,0px)) 16px 16px}
.search[hidden]{display:none}
.search-box{width:min(640px,100%);background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:18px;overflow:hidden;
  box-shadow:0 40px 120px -40px rgba(79,77,225,.65)}
.search-in{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:14px 16px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--muted)}
.search-in input{flex:1;background:transparent;border:0;color:var(--ink);font:inherit;font-size:16px;outline:none}
.results{max-height:60vh;overflow-y:auto;padding:6px}
.res{display:block;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:10px;text-decoration:none;color:var(--ink-2)}
.res.on,.res:hover{background:var(--surface-2)}
.res-t{color:var(--ink);font-weight:600;font-size:14.5px}
.res-t .num{margin-right:8px}
.res-s{font-size:13px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:2px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap}
.res-s mark{background:rgba(132,147,245,.25);color:var(--ink);border-radius:3px;padding:0 1px}
.res-empty{padding:18px 12px;color:var(--muted);font-size:14px}

@media (max-width:760px){.card{border-radius:18px}}

.panel-main section.card{display:block}
.card p,.card li{max-width:82ch}
.card .note p{max-width:none}
html,body{overflow-x:clip}
.card h2,.tab-h{color:var(--ink)}

</style>
</head>
<body>
<header class="top">
  <div class="top-in">
    <a class="top-logo" href="#empezar"><img class="fv-logo"   <header class="hero">
    <div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Guía de venta con Fourvenues</span><span class="l-en">Selling with Fourvenues guide</span> &middot; <b>Channel Manager API</b></div>
    <h1><span class="l-es">Vende entradas, reservas y listas <span class="soft">desde tu propia plataforma</span></span><span class="l-en">Sell tickets, bookings and lists <span class="soft">from your own platform</span></span></h1>
    <p class="lede"><span class="l-es">Para marketplaces, ticketeras, agregadores y operadores multi-venue.</span><span class="l-en">For marketplaces, ticketing platforms, aggregators and multi-venue operators.</span></p>
    <div class="chips">
      <span class="chip"><b>16</b> <span class="l-es">flujos</span><span class="l-en">flows</span></span>
      <span class="chip"><b>Alpha</b> + <span class="l-es">producción</span><span class="l-en">production</span></span>
      <span class="chip"><span class="l-es">Actualizada el <b>30 sep 2026</b></span><span class="l-en">Updated <b>30 Sep 2026</b></span></span>
    </div>
  </header>

  <nav class="tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Secciones"><button type="button" role="tab" data-tab="empezar" aria-selected="false"><span class="l-es">Empezar</span><span class="l-en">Start</span></button><button type="button" role="tab" data-tab="base" aria-selected="false"><span class="l-es">Base</span><span class="l-en">Foundation</span></button><button type="button" role="tab" data-tab="entradas" aria-selected="false"><span class="l-es">Entradas</span><span class="l-en">Tickets</span></button><button type="button" role="tab" data-tab="reservas" aria-selected="false"><span class="l-es">Reservas</span><span class="l-en">Bookings</span></button><button type="button" role="tab" data-tab="listas" aria-selected="false"><span class="l-es">Listas y preregistro</span><span class="l-en">Lists &amp; pre-registration</span></button><button type="button" role="tab" data-tab="extras" aria-selected="false"><span class="l-es">Extras</span><span class="l-en">Extras</span></button><button type="button" role="tab" data-tab="postventa" aria-selected="false"><span class="l-es">Postventa y webhooks</span><span class="l-en">Post-sale &amp; webhooks</span></button><button type="button" role="tab" data-tab="referencia" aria-selected="false"><span class="l-es">Referencia</span><span class="l-en">Reference</span></button></nav>

  <main class="docs"><div class="panel" role="tabpanel" id="tab-empezar" data-tab="empezar" hidden><div class="panel-main">
    <section class="card">
      <div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Introducción</span><span class="l-en">Introduction</span></div>
      <p><span class="l-es">Con el Channel Manager API puedes vender entradas, reservas y listas de los venues de Fourvenues desde tu propia plataforma. Esta guía reúne las recetas paso a paso para integrarlo.</span><span class="l-en">With the Channel Manager API you can sell tickets, bookings and guest lists from Fourvenues venues on your own platform. This guide brings together the step-by-step recipes to integrate it.</span></p><p><span class="l-es">Está pensada para canales de venta: marketplaces, ticketeras, agregadores y operadores multi-venue. Para trabajar como canal, cada venue (host) tiene que añadirte como partner. Tu API key solo ve los eventos de los venues que lo hayan hecho.</span><span class="l-en">It is built for sales channels: marketplaces, ticketing platforms, aggregators and multi-venue operators. To work as a channel, each venue (host) has to add you as a partner. Your API key only sees events from venues that have done so.</span></p>
    </section>
    <section class="card" id="ruta">
      <div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Toda integración sigue el mismo orden</span><span class="l-en">Every integration follows the same order</span></div>
      <div class="route-pick" role="group">
        <span class="route-q"><span class="l-es">¿Qué quieres vender?</span><span class="l-en">What do you want to sell?</span></span>
        <button class="pill" type="button" aria-pressed="false" data-route="tickets"><span class="l-es">Entradas</span><span class="l-en">Tickets</span></button>
        <button class="pill" type="button" aria-pressed="false" data-route="bookings"><span class="l-es">Reservas</span><span class="l-en">Bookings</span></button>
        <button class="pill" type="button" aria-pressed="false" data-route="lists"><span class="l-es">Listas</span><span class="l-en">Lists</span></button>
      </div>
      <div class="route"><div class="route-g" data-routes="tickets bookings lists"><div class="route-h"><span class="l-es">Base (se implementa una vez)</span><span class="l-en">Foundation (implemented once)</span></div><div class="route-list"><a class="route-f" href="#flujo-1"><span class="num">01</span><span class="l-es">Autenticación y permisos</span><span class="l-en">Authentication and permissions</span></a><a class="route-f" href="#flujo-2"><span class="num">02</span><span class="l-es">Sincronizar el catálogo</span><span class="l-en">Sync the catalog</span></a><a class="route-f" href="#flujo-3"><span class="num">03</span><span class="l-es">Precios</span><span class="l-en">Pricing</span></a></div></div><div class="route-g" data-routes="tickets"><div class="route-h"><span class="l-es">Venta de tickets (elige uno o los dos)</span><span class="l-en">Ticket sales (choose one or both)</span></div><div class="route-list"><a class="route-f" href="#flujo-4"><span class="num">04</span><span class="l-es">Venta de tickets con pasarela de Fourvenues</span><span class="l-en">Ticket sales with the Fourvenues gateway</span></a><a class="route-f" href="#flujo-5"><span class="num">05</span><span class="l-es">Venta de tickets con pasarela externa</span><span class="l-en">Ticket sales with an external gateway</span></a></div></div><div class="route-g" data-routes="bookings"><div class="route-h"><span class="l-es">Reservas (elige uno o los dos)</span><span class="l-en">Bookings (choose one or both)</span></div><div class="route-list"><a class="route-f" href="#flujo-6"><span class="num">06</span><span class="l-es">Reserva con pasarela de Fourvenues</span><span class="l-en">Booking with the Fourvenues gateway</span></a><a class="route-f" href="#flujo-7"><span class="num">07</span><span class="l-es">Solicitud de reserva sin pago</span><span class="l-en">Booking request without payment</span></a></div></div><div class="route-g" data-routes="lists"><div class="route-h"><span class="l-es">Listas y preregistro</span><span class="l-en">Lists and pre-registration</span></div><div class="route-list"><a class="route-f" href="#flujo-8"><span class="num">08</span><span class="l-es">Listas de invitados</span><span class="l-en">Guest lists</span></a><a class="route-f" href="#flujo-9"><span class="num">09</span><span class="l-es">Preregistro</span><span class="l-en">Pre-registration</span></a></div></div><div class="route-g" data-routes="tickets bookings"><div class="route-h"><span class="l-es">Extras</span><span class="l-en">Extras</span></div><div class="route-list"><a class="route-f" href="#flujo-10"><span class="num">10</span><span class="l-es">Complementos al comprar</span><span class="l-en">Add-ons at purchase</span></a><a class="route-f" href="#flujo-11"><span class="num">11</span><span class="l-es">Upselling después de la compra</span><span class="l-en">Upselling after purchase</span></a><a class="route-f" href="#flujo-12"><span class="num">12</span><span class="l-es">Descuentos</span><span class="l-en">Discounts</span></a><a class="route-f" href="#flujo-13"><span class="num">13</span><span class="l-es">Garantía de cancelación y propinas</span><span class="l-en">Cancellation guarantee and tips</span></a></div></div><div class="route-g" data-routes="tickets bookings lists"><div class="route-h"><span class="l-es">Postventa</span><span class="l-en">Post-sale</span></div><div class="route-list"><a class="route-f" href="#flujo-14"><span class="num">14</span><span class="l-es">Reembolsos</span><span class="l-en">Refunds</span></a><a class="route-f" href="#flujo-15"><span class="num">15</span><span class="l-es">Consulta y conciliación</span><span class="l-en">Lookup and reconciliation</span></a></div></div><div class="route-g" data-routes="tickets bookings"><div class="route-h"><span class="l-es">Notificaciones</span><span class="l-en">Notifications</span></div><p class="route-note"><span class="l-es">Imprescindible: confirman los pagos con la pasarela de Fourvenues y avisan de los reembolsos que debes procesar con pasarela externa.</span><span class="l-en">Required: they confirm payments with the Fourvenues payment gateway and notify you of refunds you must process with an external gateway.</span></p><div class="route-list"><a class="route-f" href="#flujo-16"><span class="num">16</span><span class="l-es">Webhooks</span><span class="l-en">Webhooks</span></a></div></div></div>
      <p><span class="l-es">Los flujos 1 a 3 son la base y se implementan una sola vez. Los flujos de venta se apoyan en ellos y en los webhooks (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-16">flujo 16</a>).</span><span class="l-en">Flows 1 to 3 are the foundation and are implemented only once. The sales flows build on them and on webhooks (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-16">flow 16</a>).</span></p><p><span class="l-es">Cada receta explica qué consigue el flujo, qué endpoints usa, los pasos en orden, los errores habituales y una checklist para darlo por terminado. La referencia completa de cada endpoint está en la <a href="https://docs.fourvenues.com/channel-manager/introduction/overview" target="_blank" rel="noopener">documentación del Channel Manager API</a>.</span><span class="l-en">Each recipe explains what the flow achieves, which endpoints it uses, the steps in order, common errors and a checklist to sign it off. The full reference for every endpoint is in the <a href="https://docs.fourvenues.com/channel-manager/introduction/overview" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Channel Manager API documentation</a>.</span></p>
    </section>
    <section class="card">
      <div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Entornos</span><span class="l-en">Environments</span></div>
      <div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Entorno</span><span class="l-en">Environment</span></th><th><span class="l-es">URL base</span><span class="l-en">Base URL</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Uso</span><span class="l-en">Use</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="l-es">Alpha</span><span class="l-en">Alpha</span></td><td><code>https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Desarrollo y pruebas. Datos de test, sin pagos reales.</span><span class="l-en">Development and testing. Test data, no real payments.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Producción</span><span class="l-en">Production</span></td><td><code>https://channels-service.fourvenues.com</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Datos y pagos reales. Úsalo solo cuando la integración esté validada en alpha.</span><span class="l-en">Real data and payments. Use it only once the integration is validated in alpha.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div>
    </section></div></div><div class="panel has-side" role="tabpanel" id="tab-base" data-tab="base" hidden><aside class="side"><nav class="side-nav"><a href="#flujo-1"><span class="num">01</span><span class="l-es">Autenticación y permisos</span><span class="l-en">Authentication and permissions</span></a><a href="#flujo-2"><span class="num">02</span><span class="l-es">Sincronizar el catálogo</span><span class="l-en">Sync the catalog</span></a><a href="#flujo-3"><span class="num">03</span><span class="l-es">Precios</span><span class="l-en">Pricing</span></a></nav></aside><div class="panel-main"><div class="tab-head"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Se implementa una vez</span><span class="l-en">Implemented once</span></div><h2 class="tab-h"><span class="l-es">Autenticación, catálogo y precios</span><span class="l-en">Authentication, catalog and pricing</span></h2></div><article class="card flow" id="flujo-1" data-flow="1"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow</span> <b>01</b></div><h2><span class="l-es">Autenticación y permisos</span><span class="l-en">Authentication and permissions</span></h2><div class="flow-body"><p><span class="l-es">La API key se obtiene en autoservicio desde FV Pro, una vez que tu organización está activada. Si eres una organización nueva que solo quiere integrarse por API, antes nos envías un business case breve. Todas las peticiones se autentican con la key en la cabecera <code>X-Api-Key</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You get the API key through self-service in FV Pro, once your organization is activated. If you are a new organization that only wants to integrate via API, send us a short business case first. Every request is authenticated with the key in the <code>X-Api-Key</code> header.</span></p><p><span class="l-es">El proceso completo: business case (solo organizaciones nuevas) → organización en FV Pro → datos fiscales → activación → solicitud de la clave → prueba con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/auth</code>.</span><span class="l-en">The full process: business case (new organizations only) → organization in FV Pro → tax details → activation → key request → test with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/auth</code>.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-1-s1" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 1. Envía tu business case (solo organizaciones nuevas)</span><span class="l-en">Step 1. Send your business case (new organizations only)</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Si aún no trabajas con Fourvenues y quieres integrarte solo por API, envía una propuesta breve a <a href="mailto:integrations@fourvenues.com">integrations@fourvenues.com</a>. Con una página basta:</span><span class="l-en">If you don't work with Fourvenues yet and want to integrate via API only, send a short proposal to <a href="mailto:integrations@fourvenues.com">integrations@fourvenues.com</a>. One page is enough:</span></p><ul><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Venues:</strong> con qué venues trabajas o quieres trabajar.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Venues:</strong> which venues you work with or want to work with.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Volumen:</strong> cuánto vendes al año (entradas, reservas o facturación aproximada).</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Volume:</strong> how much you sell per year (tickets, bookings or approximate revenue).</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Canales:</strong> en qué otras plataformas o ticketeras vendes hoy.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Channels:</strong> which other platforms or ticketing providers you sell on today.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Propuesta de valor:</strong> qué aporta la colaboración a los venues y a Fourvenues. Por ejemplo, nuevos públicos, mercados o tipos de cliente a los que llegas.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Value proposition:</strong> what the partnership brings to the venues and to Fourvenues. For example, new audiences, markets or customer types you reach.</span></li></ul><h3 id="flujo-1-s2" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 2. Crea tu organización en FV Pro</span><span class="l-en">Step 2. Create your organization in FV Pro</span></h3><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Entorno</span><span class="l-en">Environment</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Dónde</span><span class="l-en">Where</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Cuándo</span><span class="l-en">When</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="l-es">Alpha</span><span class="l-en">Alpha</span></td><td><a href="https://alpha.pro.fourvenues.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">alpha.pro.fourvenues.com</a></td><td><span class="l-es">La primera vez, para desarrollar y probar la integración.</span><span class="l-en">The first time, to develop and test the integration.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Producción</span><span class="l-en">Production</span></td><td><a href="https://pro.fourvenues.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">pro.fourvenues.com</a></td><td><span class="l-es">Cuando la integración funciona en alpha y quieres pasar a venta real.</span><span class="l-en">When the integration works in alpha and you want to move to real sales.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">Cada entorno es independiente: tendrás una organización y una API key en alpha, y otra organización y otra key en producción. Repite los pasos 2 a 6 en cada entorno, incluidos los datos fiscales y la activación.</span><span class="l-en">Each environment is independent: you will have one organization and API key in alpha, and another organization and key in production. Repeat steps 2 to 6 in each environment, including tax details and activation.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-1-s3" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 3. Rellena los datos fiscales</span><span class="l-en">Step 3. Fill in your tax details</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Completa los datos fiscales de la organización en FV Pro. Sin ellos no se puede activar la cuenta.</span><span class="l-en">Complete your organization's tax details in FV Pro. Without them the account cannot be activated.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-1-s4" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 4. Solicita la activación de la cuenta</span><span class="l-en">Step 4. Request account activation</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Pide la activación por email a <a href="mailto:integrations@fourvenues.com">integrations@fourvenues.com</a> o al equipo de soporte. Fourvenues revisa la solicitud y activa la organización.</span><span class="l-en">Request activation by email at <a href="mailto:integrations@fourvenues.com">integrations@fourvenues.com</a> or through the support team. Fourvenues reviews the request and activates the organization.</span></p><p><span class="l-es">Indica en la solicitud si necesitas alta en la pasarela de pagos de Fourvenues. La necesitas en dos casos:</span><span class="l-en">State in your request whether you need to be onboarded onto the Fourvenues payment gateway. You need it in two cases:</span></p><ul><li><span class="l-es">Vas a vender con la pasarela de pagos de Fourvenues.</span><span class="l-en">You will sell using the Fourvenues payment gateway.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Quieres comprar entradas de prueba con la pasarela de Fourvenues.</span><span class="l-en">You want to buy test tickets with the Fourvenues gateway.</span></li></ul><h3 id="flujo-1-s5" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 5. Solicita tu API key</span><span class="l-en">Step 5. Request your API key</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Con la cuenta activa, entra en <strong>Ajustes &gt; Developer Portal &gt; Solicitar clave</strong>. Fourvenues aprueba la solicitud y recibes la key automáticamente por email.</span><span class="l-en">Once the account is active, go to <strong>Settings &gt; Developer Portal &gt; Request key</strong>. Fourvenues approves the request and you receive the key automatically by email.</span></p><div class="note warn"><span class="note-i" aria-hidden="true">!</span><p><span class="l-es"><strong>Importante:</strong> el enlace del email solo se puede abrir una vez. Al abrirlo verás la key por única vez. Cópiala en ese momento y guárdala en tu gestor de secretos antes de cerrar la página. La key no se puede volver a consultar.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Important:</strong> the link in the email can only be opened once. When you open it you will see the key for the only time. Copy it right then and store it in your secrets manager before closing the page. The key cannot be viewed again.</span></p></div><p><span class="l-es">Si pierdes la key, desde el Developer Portal puedes:</span><span class="l-en">If you lose the key, from the Developer Portal you can:</span></p><ul><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Reenviarla:</strong> recibes una key nueva y la anterior queda cancelada.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Resend it:</strong> you get a new key and the previous one is cancelled.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Solicitar una nueva.</span><span class="l-en">Request a new one.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Eliminar las que ya no uses.</span><span class="l-en">Delete the ones you no longer use.</span></li></ul><h3 id="flujo-1-s6" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 6. Comprueba que funciona</span><span class="l-en">Step 6. Check that it works</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Llama siempre desde tu backend. Nunca expongas la key en el navegador ni en una app móvil. Solo se acepta HTTPS.</span><span class="l-en">Always call from your backend. Never expose the key in the browser or in a mobile app. Only HTTPS is accepted.</span></p><div class="code pair" data-pair="1"><div class="code-bar"><div class="seg" role="tablist"><button type="button" class="on" data-show="req"><span class="l-es">Petición</span><span class="l-en">Request</span></button><button type="button" data-show="res"><span class="l-es">Respuesta</span><span class="l-en">Response</span></button></div><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre data-pane="req"><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> GET \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/auth'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span></code></pre><pre data-pane="res" hidden><code>{
  <span class="t-k">"success"</span>: <span class="t-kw">true</span>,
  <span class="t-k">"data"</span>: {
    <span class="t-k">"message"</span>: <span class="t-s">"Authed"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"channel"</span>: {
      <span class="t-k">"_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"ch_abc123"</span>,
      <span class="t-k">"name"</span>: <span class="t-s">"Mi Marketplace"</span>,
      <span class="t-k">"slug"</span>: <span class="t-s">"mi-marketplace"</span>,
      <span class="t-k">"currency"</span>: <span class="t-s">"EUR"</span>,
      <span class="t-k">"hosts"</span>: [
        { <span class="t-k">"_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"org_xyz789"</span>, <span class="t-k">"name"</span>: <span class="t-s">"Club Ejemplo"</span>, <span class="t-k">"slug"</span>: <span class="t-s">"club-ejemplo"</span>, <span class="t-k">"created_at"</span>: <span class="t-s">"2023-06-01T00:00:00.000Z"</span> }
      ],
      <span class="t-k">"created_at"</span>: <span class="t-s">"2024-10-01T00:00:00.000Z"</span>
    }
  }
}</code></pre></div><ul><li><span class="l-es"><code>hosts</code> son los venues que te han añadido como partner. Usa su <code>_id</code> como <code>organization_id</code> en el resto de llamadas. Si la lista sale vacía, ningún venue te ha añadido todavía.</span><span class="l-en"><code>hosts</code> are the venues that have added you as a partner. Use their <code>_id</code> as <code>organization_id</code> in every other call. If the list is empty, no venue has added you yet.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><code>anfitrions</code> es un campo antiguo con el mismo contenido y está deprecado. Usa <code>hosts</code>.</span><span class="l-en"><code>anfitrions</code> is a legacy field with the same content and is deprecated. Use <code>hosts</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Si un venue te quita como partner, desaparece de <code>hosts</code> y sus eventos dejan de aparecer. Tu catálogo debe reflejarlo (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-2">flujo 2</a>).</span><span class="l-en">If a venue removes you as a partner, it disappears from <code>hosts</code> and its events stop appearing. Your catalog must reflect this (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-2">flow 2</a>).</span></li></ul><h3 id="flujo-1-s7" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Consigue tus venues partner</span><span class="l-en">Get your partner venues</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Tener una API key no te da acceso a ningún venue. Cada venue tiene que aceptar la colaboración contigo.</span><span class="l-en">Having an API key does not give you access to any venue. Each venue has to accept the partnership with you.</span></p><ol><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Tu equipo contacta con los venues.</strong> Fourvenues no puede facilitar los contactos directos de los venues. La relación comercial es tuya.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Your team contacts the venues.</strong> Fourvenues cannot share venues' direct contacts. The commercial relationship is yours.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><strong>El venue activa la colaboración.</strong> Lo hace desde su panel de configuración en FV Pro o a través de su Account Manager en Fourvenues.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>The venue activates the partnership.</strong> It does so from its settings panel in FV Pro or through its Account Manager at Fourvenues.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Compruébalo con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/auth</code>.</strong> En cuanto la colaboración está activa, el venue aparece en <code>hosts</code>.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Check it with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/auth</code>.</strong> As soon as the partnership is active, the venue appears in <code>hosts</code>.</span></li></ol><h3 id="flujo-1-s8" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Permisos y ciclo de vida de la key</span><span class="l-en">Key permissions and lifecycle</span></h3><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Aspecto</span><span class="l-en">Aspect</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué significa para ti</span><span class="l-en">What it means for you</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="l-es">Alcance</span><span class="l-en">Scope</span></td><td><span class="l-es">La key pertenece a tu organización y a un entorno. Solo ve los venues que te tienen como partner.</span><span class="l-en">The key belongs to your organization and to one environment. It only sees venues that have you as a partner.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Capabilities</span><span class="l-en">Capabilities</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Cada key tiene habilitados solo los endpoints que necesitas (p. ej. <code>preregister:write</code> para el preregistro, o <code>all:write</code>). Si falta una, la llamada devuelve <code>403</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Each key only has the endpoints you need enabled (e.g. <code>preregister:write</code> for pre-registration, or <code>all:write</code>). If one is missing, the call returns <code>403</code>.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Caducidad</span><span class="l-en">Expiry</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Las keys caducan. Solicita la nueva desde el Developer Portal antes de la fecha de expiración.</span><span class="l-en">Keys expire. Request the new one from the Developer Portal before the expiry date.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Gestión</span><span class="l-en">Management</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Desde el Developer Portal reenvías una key (cancela la anterior), solicitas nuevas o eliminas las antiguas.</span><span class="l-en">From the Developer Portal you resend a key (cancels the previous one), request new ones or delete old ones.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Revocación</span><span class="l-en">Revocation</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Fourvenues puede revocar una key de inmediato si se usa mal.</span><span class="l-en">Fourvenues can revoke a key immediately if it is misused.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><h3 id="flujo-1-s9" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Límites y errores</span><span class="l-en">Limits and errors</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">No pases de 10 peticiones por segundo por key. Si recibes <code>429</code>, reintenta con backoff exponencial.</span><span class="l-en">Do not exceed 10 requests per second per key. If you get <code>429</code>, retry with exponential backoff.</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Código</span><span class="l-en">Code</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Causa</span><span class="l-en">Cause</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué hacer</span><span class="l-en">What to do</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Falta la cabecera o la key no es válida</span><span class="l-en">The header is missing or the key is invalid</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Revisa el nombre <code>X-Api-Key</code>, el valor y que usas la key del entorno correcto.</span><span class="l-en">Check the <code>X-Api-Key</code> name, the value, and that you are using the key for the right environment.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>403</code></td><td><span class="l-es">La key no tiene permiso para ese endpoint o ese venue</span><span class="l-en">The key has no permission for that endpoint or venue</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Pide ampliar las capabilities o confirma que el venue te tiene como partner.</span><span class="l-en">Ask for extra capabilities or confirm that the venue has you as a partner.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span class="l-es">El recurso no existe o no es visible para tu canal</span><span class="l-en">The resource does not exist or is not visible to your channel</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Revisa el <code>_id</code> o el <code>slug</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Check the <code>_id</code> or <code>slug</code>.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>429</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Has superado el límite de peticiones</span><span class="l-en">You exceeded the request limit</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Backoff exponencial.</span><span class="l-en">Exponential backoff.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>500</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Error inesperado</span><span class="l-en">Unexpected error</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Reintenta una vez. Si se repite, contacta con soporte.</span><span class="l-en">Retry once. If it repeats, contact support.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="check" data-flow="1"><div class="check-h"><span class="l-es">Checklist</span><span class="l-en">Checklist</span><span class="check-n" data-count="1"></span></div><ul><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="1-0"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Organización creada en alpha, con datos fiscales y cuenta activada.</span><span class="l-en">Organization created in alpha, with tax details and account activated.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="1-1"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Key copiada del enlace de un solo uso y guardada en tu gestor de secretos.</span><span class="l-en">Key copied from the single-use link and stored in your secrets manager.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="1-2"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es"><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/auth</code> devuelve <code>&quot;message&quot;: &quot;Authed&quot;</code> y los <code>hosts</code> esperados.</span><span class="l-en"><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/auth</code> returns <code>&quot;message&quot;: &quot;Authed&quot;</code> and the expected <code>hosts</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="1-3"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">La key solo se usa en el backend.</span><span class="l-en">The key is only used on the backend.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="1-4"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Gestionas <code>401</code>, <code>403</code> y <code>429</code> sin romper la experiencia del comprador.</span><span class="l-en">You handle <code>401</code>, <code>403</code> and <code>429</code> without breaking the buyer experience.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="1-5"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Tienes anotada la fecha de caducidad de la key.</span><span class="l-en">You have noted the key's expiry date.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="1-6"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Antes de salir a producción, repites los pasos 2 a 6 en <a href="https://pro.fourvenues.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">pro.fourvenues.com</a>.</span><span class="l-en">Before going to production, you repeat steps 2 to 6 on <a href="https://pro.fourvenues.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">pro.fourvenues.com</a>.</span></label></li></ul></div></div></article><article class="card flow" id="flujo-2" data-flow="2"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow</span> <b>02</b></div><h2><span class="l-es">Sincronizar el catálogo</span><span class="l-en">Sync the catalog</span></h2><div class="flow-body"><p><span class="l-es">El catálogo se lee de arriba abajo: venue, ubicación, evento y, dentro del evento, lo que se puede vender. Todo es de solo lectura: los venues crean y editan sus eventos en FV Pro.</span><span class="l-en">The catalog is read top-down: venue, location, event and, inside the event, what can be sold. Everything is read-only: venues create and edit their events in FV Pro.</span></p><ul class="tree"><li style="--d:0"><span class="l-es">Organización (venue o host)</span><span class="l-en">Organization (venue or host)</span></li><li style="--d:1"><span class="l-es">Ubicaciones</span><span class="l-en">Locations</span></li><li style="--d:1"><span class="l-es">Grupos de eventos (opcional, agrupan varios eventos)</span><span class="l-en">Event groups (optional, group several events)</span></li><li style="--d:1"><span class="l-es">Eventos, cada uno en una ubicación:</span><span class="l-en">Events, each at a location:</span></li><li style="--d:2"><span class="l-es">Tarifas de entrada</span><span class="l-en">Ticket rates</span></li><li style="--d:2"><span class="l-es">Tarifas de lista</span><span class="l-en">List rates</span></li><li style="--d:2"><span class="l-es">Zonas de reserva, con sus mesas y tarifas de mesa</span><span class="l-en">Booking zones, with their tables and table rates</span></li></ul><p><span class="l-es">Un evento puede pertenecer a un grupo de eventos, que agrupa varias fechas o eventos relacionados.</span><span class="l-en">An event can belong to an event group, which groups several dates or related events.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-2-s1" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Endpoints</span><span class="l-en">Endpoints</span></h3><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Qué obtienes</span><span class="l-en">What you get</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Endpoint</span><span class="l-en">Endpoint</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Filtros útiles</span><span class="l-en">Useful filters</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="l-es">Venues partner</span><span class="l-en">Partner venues</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/organizations</code> · <code>/organizations/{id}</code> · <code>/organizations/by-slug/{slug}</code></td><td><code>search</code></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Ubicaciones</span><span class="l-en">Locations</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/locations</code> · <code>/locations/{id}</code></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>organization_id</code>, <code>lat</code> + <code>lng</code> + <code>radius</code> (km, máx. 500), <code>limit</code>, <code>offset</code></span><span class="l-en"><code>organization_id</code>, <code>lat</code> + <code>lng</code> + <code>radius</code> (km, max. 500), <code>limit</code>, <code>offset</code></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Eventos</span><span class="l-en">Events</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/events</code> · <code>/events/{id}</code> · <code>/events/by-slug/{slug}</code></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>organization_id</code>, <code>location_id</code>, <code>start_date</code>, <code>end_date</code>, <code>search</code>, <code>lat</code> + <code>lng</code> + <code>radius</code>, <code>limit</code> (50 por defecto), <code>offset</code>, <code>populate</code></span><span class="l-en"><code>organization_id</code>, <code>location_id</code>, <code>start_date</code>, <code>end_date</code>, <code>search</code>, <code>lat</code> + <code>lng</code> + <code>radius</code>, <code>limit</code> (50 by default), <code>offset</code>, <code>populate</code></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Grupos de eventos</span><span class="l-en">Event groups</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/event-groups</code> · <code>/event-groups/{id}</code></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>organization_id</code> (obligatorio), <code>limit</code>, <code>offset</code></span><span class="l-en"><code>organization_id</code> (required), <code>limit</code>, <code>offset</code></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Tarifas de entrada</span><span class="l-en">Ticket rates</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/ticket-rates</code> · <code>/ticket-rates/{id}</code> · <code>/ticket-rates/by-slug/{slug}</code></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>event_id</code> (obligatorio, admite varios separados por coma)</span><span class="l-en"><code>event_id</code> (required, accepts several comma-separated)</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Tarifas de lista</span><span class="l-en">List rates</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/list-rates</code> · <code>/list-rates/{id}</code> · <code>/list-rates/by-slug/{slug}</code></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>event_id</code> (obligatorio, admite varios separados por coma)</span><span class="l-en"><code>event_id</code> (required, accepts several comma-separated)</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Zonas de reserva</span><span class="l-en">Booking zones</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/bookings/zones</code></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>event_id</code> (obligatorio)</span><span class="l-en"><code>event_id</code> (required)</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Disponibilidad de reservas</span><span class="l-en">Booking availability</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/bookings/availability</code></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>event_id</code> (obligatorio), <code>quantity</code> (nº de personas)</span><span class="l-en"><code>event_id</code> (required), <code>quantity</code> (number of people)</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es"><code>populate</code> añade información a cada evento en la misma llamada: <code>ticket-rates</code>, <code>list-rates</code>, <code>event-groups</code>, <code>preregister</code> o <code>*</code> para todo. Sin <code>populate</code>, la respuesta ya trae la ubicación, los artistas y la configuración de preregistro.</span><span class="l-en"><code>populate</code> adds information to each event in the same call: <code>ticket-rates</code>, <code>list-rates</code>, <code>event-groups</code>, <code>preregister</code> or <code>*</code> for everything. Without <code>populate</code>, the response already includes the location, artists and pre-registration settings.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-2-s2" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 1. Carga los eventos de cada venue</span><span class="l-en">Step 1. Load each venue's events</span></h3><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">cURL</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> GET \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/events?organization_id=org_xyz789&amp;start_date=2026-10-01&amp;end_date=2026-10-31&amp;populate=ticket-rates&amp;limit=50&amp;offset=0'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span></code></pre></div><p><span class="l-es">Pagina con <code>offset</code> (0, 50, 100…) hasta que la respuesta traiga menos elementos que <code>limit</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Paginate with <code>offset</code> (0, 50, 100…) until the response returns fewer items than <code>limit</code>.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-2-s3" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 2. Guarda los campos que necesitas para mostrar el evento</span><span class="l-en">Step 2. Store the fields you need to display the event</span></h3><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Para qué sirve</span><span class="l-en">What it's for</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>display_date</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Fecha que ve el comprador. Para eventos nocturnos no coincide con <code>start_date</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Date shown to the buyer. For night events it does not match <code>start_date</code>.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>start_date</code>, <code>end_date</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Horario real del evento.</span><span class="l-en">Actual event schedule.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>location.timezone</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Zona horaria para mostrar las horas.</span><span class="l-en">Time zone for displaying times.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>age</code>, <code>outfit</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Edad mínima y dress code.</span><span class="l-en">Minimum age and dress code.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>currency</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Moneda de los importes del evento.</span><span class="l-en">Currency of the event's amounts.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>image_url</code>, <code>description</code>, <code>artists</code>, <code>music_genres</code>, <code>ambiences</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Ficha del evento.</span><span class="l-en">Event listing.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>is_upselling_enabled</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si se pueden añadir complementos después de la compra.</span><span class="l-en">Whether add-ons can be added after purchase.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>preregister</code>, <code>is_preregistered</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Configuración de preregistro (<code>null</code> si no hay).</span><span class="l-en">Pre-registration settings (<code>null</code> if none).</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><h3 id="flujo-2-s4" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 3. Lee qué se puede vender en cada evento</span><span class="l-en">Step 3. Read what can be sold at each event</span></h3><p><span class="l-es"><strong>Tarifas de entrada.</strong> Estos son los campos que deciden si se puede comprar y cómo:</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Ticket rates.</strong> These are the fields that decide whether and how a purchase can be made:</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Significado</span><span class="l-en">Meaning</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>available</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si la tarifa está a la venta ahora. Si es <code>false</code>, no ofrezcas la compra.</span><span class="l-en">Whether the rate is on sale now. If <code>false</code>, do not offer the purchase.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>valid_from</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Fecha a partir de la que se puede comprar.</span><span class="l-en">Date from which it can be bought.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>availability.available</code>, <code>availability.sold</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Entradas disponibles y vendidas.</span><span class="l-en">Tickets available and sold.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>min</code>, <code>max</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Mínimo y máximo de entradas por compra.</span><span class="l-en">Minimum and maximum tickets per purchase.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>current_price</code>, <code>prices</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Precio activo y todos los tramos (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flujo 3</a>).</span><span class="l-en">Active price and all price tiers (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flow 3</a>).</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>supplements</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Complementos que se pueden añadir.</span><span class="l-en">Add-ons that can be added.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>warranty</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Garantía de cancelación opcional.</span><span class="l-en">Optional cancellation guarantee.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>questions</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Preguntas al comprador. Si tienen <code>required: true</code>, son obligatorias.</span><span class="l-en">Questions for the buyer. If <code>required: true</code>, they are mandatory.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>has_discount_codes_enabled</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si hay que mostrar el campo de código de descuento.</span><span class="l-en">Whether to show the discount code field.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es"><strong>Zonas de reserva.</strong> Cada zona indica si está a la venta (<code>available</code>), si está llena (<code>is_full</code>) y si el comprador puede elegir mesa (<code>can_select_client</code>). Si tiene mesas, vienen en <code>spaces</code>, cada una con <code>capacity</code>, <code>available</code> y sus tarifas. Si no tiene mesas, las tarifas vienen directamente en <code>rates</code>. <code>background_image</code> es el plano de la zona.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Booking zones.</strong> Each zone shows whether it is on sale (<code>available</code>), whether it is full (<code>is_full</code>) and whether the buyer can choose a table (<code>can_select_client</code>). If it has tables, they come in <code>spaces</code>, each with <code>capacity</code>, <code>available</code> and its rates. If it has no tables, rates come directly in <code>rates</code>. <code>background_image</code> is the zone's floor plan.</span></p><p><span class="l-es">Usa <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/bookings/availability?event_id=...&amp;quantity=N</code> para mostrar solo las zonas y mesas con sitio para N personas.</span><span class="l-en">Use <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/bookings/availability?event_id=...&amp;quantity=N</code> to show only zones and tables with room for N people.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-2-s5" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 4. Mantén el catálogo al día</span><span class="l-en">Step 4. Keep the catalog up to date</span></h3><div class="note warn"><span class="note-i" aria-hidden="true">!</span><p><span class="l-es"><strong>Importante:</strong> las tarifas pueden cambiar en cualquier momento. El venue puede editarlas o cerrarlas, un tramo de precio puede agotarse y la disponibilidad baja con cada venta. Que una tarifa fuera válida hace un minuto no garantiza que lo siga siendo.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Important:</strong> rates can change at any time. The venue can edit or close them, a price tier can sell out and availability drops with every sale. A rate being valid a minute ago does not guarantee it still is.</span></p></div><p><span class="l-es">Comprueba la tarifa (<code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/ticket-rates/{id}</code>) o la zona de reserva en tres momentos:</span><span class="l-en">Check the rate (<code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/ticket-rates/{id}</code>) or the booking zone at three moments:</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Momento</span><span class="l-en">Moment</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué comprobar</span><span class="l-en">What to check</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="l-es">Al mostrarla</span><span class="l-en">When displaying it</span></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>available</code>, <code>current_price</code> y <code>availability.available</code>. No muestres tarifas desde una caché antigua.</span><span class="l-en"><code>available</code>, <code>current_price</code> and <code>availability.available</code>. Do not display rates from an old cache.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Al iniciar la compra</span><span class="l-en">When starting the purchase</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Que sigue a la venta, que el precio no ha cambiado y que hay stock para la cantidad elegida.</span><span class="l-en">That it is still on sale, the price hasn't changed and there is stock for the chosen quantity.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Justo antes de enviar la compra</span><span class="l-en">Right before submitting the purchase</span></td><td><span class="l-es">La última lectura antes de lanzar el checkout o la creación de entradas. Envía el <code>price_id</code> y gestiona <code>conditions_changed</code>.</span><span class="l-en">The last read before launching checkout or ticket creation. Send the <code>price_id</code> and handle <code>conditions_changed</code>.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">Si algo ha cambiado, enseña al comprador las condiciones nuevas antes de continuar.</span><span class="l-en">If anything has changed, show the buyer the new conditions before continuing.</span></p><p><span class="l-es">Además:</span><span class="l-en">Also:</span></p><ul><li><span class="l-es">Sincroniza periódicamente los eventos de cada host, sin pasar de 10 peticiones por segundo.</span><span class="l-en">Periodically sync each host's events, without exceeding 10 requests per second.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Si un evento o un venue deja de aparecer, retira el evento de la venta en tu plataforma.</span><span class="l-en">If an event or venue stops appearing, take the event off sale on your platform.</span></li></ul><div class="check" data-flow="2"><div class="check-h"><span class="l-es">Checklist</span><span class="l-en">Checklist</span><span class="check-n" data-count="2"></span></div><ul><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="2-0"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Recorres todos los hosts y paginas <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/events</code> hasta el final.</span><span class="l-en">You go through every host and paginate <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/events</code> to the end.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="2-1"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Muestras <code>display_date</code> en la zona horaria de la ubicación.</span><span class="l-en">You show <code>display_date</code> in the location's time zone.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="2-2"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Solo ofreces tarifas con <code>available: true</code> y respetas <code>min</code> y <code>max</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You only offer rates with <code>available: true</code> and respect <code>min</code> and <code>max</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="2-3"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Validas la tarifa al mostrarla, al iniciar la compra y justo antes de enviarla.</span><span class="l-en">You validate the rate when displaying it, when starting the purchase and right before submitting.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="2-4"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Retiras de tu catálogo los eventos que ya no devuelve la API.</span><span class="l-en">You remove from your catalog the events the API no longer returns.</span></label></li></ul></div></div></article><article class="card flow" id="flujo-3" data-flow="3"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow</span> <b>03</b></div><h2><span class="l-es">Precios</span><span class="l-en">Pricing</span></h2><div class="flow-body"><p><span class="l-es">El precio que ve el comprador es el precio del tramo activo más los gastos de gestión. Si compra varias entradas, cada una puede caer en un tramo distinto. <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/ticket-rates/{id}/pricing-info</code> te da el desglose exacto.</span><span class="l-en">The price the buyer sees is the active tier price plus service fees. If they buy several tickets, each can fall into a different tier. <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/ticket-rates/{id}/pricing-info</code> gives you the exact breakdown.</span></p><div class="note info"><span class="note-i" aria-hidden="true">i</span><p><span class="l-es">Todos los importes vienen en unidades de la moneda del evento (<code>currency</code>), no en céntimos: <code>12</code> son 12,00 €.</span><span class="l-en">All amounts come in units of the event currency (<code>currency</code>), not cents: <code>12</code> means €12.00.</span></p></div><p><span class="l-es">El número de decimales depende de la moneda. El euro tiene 2 decimales (<code>12.5</code> son 12,50 €), pero otras monedas no tienen decimales (por ejemplo, el yen japonés o el peso chileno). Valida y redondea cada importe según los decimales de su <code>currency</code> (estándar ISO 4217) antes de mostrarlo, cobrarlo o enviarlo.</span><span class="l-en">The number of decimals depends on the currency. The euro has 2 decimals (<code>12.5</code> is €12.50), but other currencies have none (for example, the Japanese yen or the Chilean peso). Validate and round each amount to its <code>currency</code> decimals (ISO 4217 standard) before displaying, charging or sending it.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-3-s1" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Tramos de precio</span><span class="l-en">Price tiers</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Una tarifa de entrada puede tener varios precios (<code>prices</code>), pero solo uno está activo cada vez: <code>current_price</code>. Cuando se agota su cupo (<code>quantity</code>) o pasa su fecha (<code>valid_until</code>), entra el siguiente.</span><span class="l-en">A ticket rate can have several prices (<code>prices</code>), but only one is active at a time: <code>current_price</code>. When its allocation (<code>quantity</code>) sells out or its date (<code>valid_until</code>) passes, the next one kicks in.</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo del precio</span><span class="l-en">Price field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Significado</span><span class="l-en">Meaning</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>_id</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Identificador del precio. Envíalo como <code>price_id</code> al comprar.</span><span class="l-en">Price identifier. Send it as <code>price_id</code> when buying.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>name</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Nombre del tramo (p. ej. «Early Bird»).</span><span class="l-en">Tier name (e.g. "Early Bird").</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>price</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Precio base de la entrada, sin gastos.</span><span class="l-en">Base ticket price, without fees.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>fee_type</code>, <code>fee_quantity</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Gastos de gestión: <code>percentage</code> (porcentaje sobre <code>price</code>) o <code>fixed</code> (importe fijo por entrada).</span><span class="l-en">Service fees: <code>percentage</code> (percentage of <code>price</code>) or <code>fixed</code> (fixed amount per ticket).</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>quantity</code>, <code>used</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Cupo del tramo y entradas vendidas en él. <code>quantity: 0</code> es ilimitado.</span><span class="l-en">Tier allocation and tickets sold in it. <code>quantity: 0</code> is unlimited.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>valid_until</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Fecha hasta la que vale el tramo.</span><span class="l-en">Date until which the tier is valid.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>includes</code>, <code>additional_info</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Lo que incluye la entrada y sus condiciones. Muéstralos junto al precio.</span><span class="l-en">What the ticket includes and its conditions. Show them next to the price.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">Si la tarifa tiene <code>show_all_prices: true</code>, el venue quiere que se vean todos los tramos, también los futuros. Si no, muestra solo <code>current_price</code>.</span><span class="l-en">If the rate has <code>show_all_prices: true</code>, the venue wants all tiers shown, including future ones. Otherwise, show only <code>current_price</code>.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-3-s2" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Orden de cálculo</span><span class="l-en">Calculation order</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Fourvenues calcula cada entrada siempre en este orden. Con la pasarela de Fourvenues lo hace por ti. Con pasarela externa (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-5">flujo 5</a>) tienes que seguir el mismo orden para que tus importes cuadren.</span><span class="l-en">Fourvenues always calculates each ticket in this order. With the Fourvenues payment gateway it does it for you.
