With an external gateway (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-5">flow 5</a>) you must follow the same order so your amounts match.</span></p><ol><li><span class="l-es">Descuento (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-12">flujo 12</a>).</span><span class="l-en">Discount on the ticket price.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Gastos de gestión de la entrada.</span><span class="l-en">Ticket service fee, on the discounted price.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Complementos y sus gastos de gestión (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-10">flujo 10</a>).</span><span class="l-en">Add-ons and their fee (the ticket's fee rules).</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Garantía de cancelación y sus gastos de gestión (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-13">flujo 13</a>).</span><span class="l-en">Warranty and its fee.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Envío por WhatsApp, si aplica.</span><span class="l-en">WhatsApp delivery, if applicable.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Impuestos y tasas personalizadas, si el venue los tiene.</span><span class="l-en">Custom fees.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Total de la entrada.</span><span class="l-en">Totals.</span></li></ol><div class="note info"><span class="note-i" aria-hidden="true">i</span><p><span class="l-es">Redondea el resultado de cada paso a los decimales de la moneda antes de pasar al siguiente. Si redondeas solo al final, tus importes pueden no cuadrar por céntimos.</span><span class="l-en">Round each step's result to the currency's decimals before moving to the next. Do not carry unrounded values between steps: the total must match what Fourvenues calculates.</span></p></div><h3 id="flujo-3-s3" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Gastos de gestión de la entrada</span><span class="l-en">Ticket service fee</span></h3><ul><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Con <code>fee_type: fixed</code></strong> = <code>fee_quantity</code>, una vez por entrada.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>With <code>fee_type: fixed</code></strong> = <code>fee_quantity</code>, once per ticket.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Con <code>fee_type: percentage</code></strong> = (<code>price</code> − descuento de esa entrada) × <code>fee_quantity</code> / 100. La base es el precio del tramo con el descuento ya aplicado.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>With <code>fee_type: percentage</code></strong> = (<code>price</code> − that ticket's discount) × <code>fee_quantity</code> / 100. The base is the tier price with the discount already applied.</span></li></ul><p><span class="l-es">Los gastos de gestión totales de una entrada son la suma de tres partes: los de la entrada, los de sus complementos y los de su garantía.</span><span class="l-en">A ticket's total service fee is the sum of three parts: the ticket's own fee, its add-ons' fees and its warranty's fee.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-3-s4" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Envío por WhatsApp</span><span class="l-en">WhatsApp delivery</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Si las entradas se envían por WhatsApp, el envío cuesta 0,99 € por entrada. Solo está disponible para eventos en euros. Si el venue lo repercute al comprador, se suma al total de cada entrada. Si no, lo asume el venue.</span><span class="l-en">If the event sends tickets by WhatsApp, delivery costs <strong>€0.99 per ticket</strong> (EUR events only). If the venue passes it on to the buyer, add it to the total. If not, the venue absorbs it. Fourvenues never covers it.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-3-s5" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Ejemplo completo</span><span class="l-en">Full example</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Entrada de 20,00 € con gastos del 10 % (<code>percentage</code>), un código de descuento del 10 %, un complemento de 5,00 € sin gastos propios y garantía del 20 %:</span><span class="l-en">A €20.00 ticket with a 10% service fee (<code>percentage</code>), a 10% discount code, a €5.00 add-on with no fee of its own and a 20% warranty:</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Paso</span><span class="l-en">Step</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Cálculo</span><span class="l-en">Calculation</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Importe</span><span class="l-en">Amount</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="l-es">Precio del tramo</span><span class="l-en">Tier price</span></td><td>—</td><td><span class="l-es">20,00 €</span><span class="l-en">€20.00</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Descuento del 10 %</span><span class="l-en">10% discount</span></td><td><span class="l-es">20,00 × 10 %</span><span class="l-en">20.00 × 10%</span></td><td><span class="l-es">−2,00 €</span><span class="l-en">−€2.00</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Precio con descuento</span><span class="l-en">Discounted price</span></td><td><span class="l-es">20,00 − 2,00</span><span class="l-en">20.00 − 2.00</span></td><td><span class="l-es">18,00 €</span><span class="l-en">€18.00</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Gastos de la entrada</span><span class="l-en">Ticket service fee</span></td><td><span class="l-es">18,00 × 10 %</span><span class="l-en">18.00 × 10%</span></td><td><span class="l-es">1,80 €</span><span class="l-en">€1.80</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Complemento</span><span class="l-en">Add-on</span></td><td><span class="l-es">5,00 × 1</span><span class="l-en">5.00 × 1</span></td><td><span class="l-es">5,00 €</span><span class="l-en">€5.00</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Gastos del complemento (usa los de la entrada)</span><span class="l-en">Add-on service fee (uses the ticket's)</span></td><td><span class="l-es">5,00 × 10 %</span><span class="l-en">5.00 × 10%</span></td><td><span class="l-es">0,50 €</span><span class="l-en">€0.50</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Garantía</span><span class="l-en">Warranty</span></td><td><span class="l-es">(18,00 + 5,00) × 20 %</span><span class="l-en">(18.00 + 5.00) × 20%</span></td><td><span class="l-es">4,60 €</span><span class="l-en">€4.60</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Gastos de la garantía (usa los de la entrada)</span><span class="l-en">Warranty service fee (uses the ticket's)</span></td><td><span class="l-es">4,60 × 10 %</span><span class="l-en">4.60 × 10%</span></td><td><span class="l-es">0,46 €</span><span class="l-en">€0.46</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es"><strong>Total</strong></span><span class="l-en"><strong>Total</strong></span></td><td><span class="l-es">18,00 + 1,80 + 5,00 + 0,50 + 4,60 + 0,46</span><span class="l-en">18.00 + 1.80 + 5.00 + 0.50 + 4.60 + 0.46</span></td><td><span class="l-es"><strong>30,36 €</strong></span><span class="l-en"><strong>€30.36</strong></span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><h3 id="flujo-3-s6" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Compras de varias entradas</span><span class="l-en">Multi-ticket purchases</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Si quedan 2 entradas en el tramo actual y el comprador quiere 3, la tercera va al tramo siguiente. Pide el desglose antes de mostrar el total:</span><span class="l-en">If 2 tickets are left in the current tier and the buyer wants 3, the third goes to the next tier. Request the breakdown before showing the total:</span></p><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">cURL</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> GET \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/ticket-rates/tr_111/pricing-info?quantity=3'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span></code></pre></div><p><span class="l-es">La respuesta trae una línea por entrada con <code>price_id</code>, <code>price</code>, <code>fee_type</code> y <code>fee_quantity</code>. Suma las líneas para el total.</span><span class="l-en">The response returns one line per ticket with <code>price_id</code>, <code>price</code>, <code>fee_type</code> and <code>fee_quantity</code>. Add up the lines for the total.</span></p><p><span class="l-es">Al comprar, envía en cada entrada el <code>price_id</code> que has mostrado. Si el precio ha cambiado entre medias, la respuesta del checkout lo indica con <code>conditions_changed</code>, y debes enseñar el precio nuevo al comprador antes de cobrar.</span><span class="l-en">When buying, send in each ticket the <code>price_id</code> you displayed. If the price changed in the meantime, the checkout response flags it with <code>conditions_changed</code>, and you must show the new price to the buyer before charging.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-3-s7" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Otros importes que afectan al total</span><span class="l-en">Other amounts that affect the total</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Cada uno se detalla en su flujo. Aquí solo lo que tienes que tener en cuenta al calcular precios.</span><span class="l-en">Each one is detailed in its own flow. Here is only what you need to consider when calculating prices.</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Concepto</span><span class="l-en">Item</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Dónde viene</span><span class="l-en">Where it comes from</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Cómo se calcula</span><span class="l-en">How it's calculated</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="l-es">Descuentos</span><span class="l-en">Discounts</span></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>discount_code</code> al comprar</span><span class="l-en"><code>discount_code</code> when buying</span></td><td><span class="l-es">En porcentaje, se aplica al precio del tramo de cada entrada. Si es fijo, se aplica una vez por compra y se reparte entre las entradas en proporción a su precio (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-12">flujo 12</a>).</span><span class="l-en">As a percentage, applied to each ticket's tier price. If fixed, applied once per purchase and split across tickets in proportion to their price (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-12">flow 12</a>).</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Complementos</span><span class="l-en">Add-ons</span></td><td><code>ticket_rate.supplements</code></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>price</code> por pack × cantidad, más sus gastos de gestión. Los gastos son los de la entrada: si son porcentaje se aplican al complemento; si son fijos, se cobran una sola vez por entrada (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-10">flujo 10</a>). Si <code>has_fake_price</code> es <code>true</code>, <code>fake_price</code> es el precio original tachado.</span><span class="l-en"><code>price</code> per pack × quantity, plus their service fee. Fees are the ticket's: if a percentage, they apply to the add-on; if fixed, they are charged only once per ticket (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-10">flow 10</a>). If <code>has_fake_price</code> is <code>true</code>, <code>fake_price</code> is the original struck-through price.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Garantía de cancelación</span><span class="l-en">Cancellation warranty</span></td><td><code>ticket_rate.warranty</code></td><td><span class="l-es">En porcentaje, sobre el precio con descuento más los complementos sin gastos. Suma los gastos de gestión de la entrada cuando son porcentaje (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-13">flujo 13</a>). Opcional para el comprador.</span><span class="l-en">As a percentage, of the discounted price plus add-ons without fees. It adds the ticket's service fee when that is a percentage (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-13">flow 13</a>). Optional for the buyer.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Impuestos personalizados</span><span class="l-en">Custom taxes</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/custom-taxes?organization_id=...</code></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>calculation.type</code> y <code>calculation.value</code> (p. ej. porcentaje 10). En porcentaje, la base es el precio con descuento, los complementos sin gastos y la garantía. <code>apply_to</code> indica si afectan a <code>tickets</code>, <code>reservations</code> o ambos.</span><span class="l-en"><code>calculation.type</code> and <code>calculation.value</code> (e.g. percentage 10). As a percentage, the base is the discounted price, add-ons without fees and the warranty. <code>apply_to</code> says whether they apply to <code>tickets</code>, <code>reservations</code> or both.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Reservas</span><span class="l-en">Bookings</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Tarifas de mesa (<code>rates</code>)</span><span class="l-en">Table rates (<code>rates</code>)</span></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>price</code> de la mesa para <code>included_persons</code> personas. Cada persona extra suma <code>supplement_price</code>. El depósito sale de <code>deposit</code> (<code>none</code>, <code>fixed</code> o <code>percent</code>). Si <code>full_payment</code> es <code>true</code>, se puede pagar todo por adelantado.</span><span class="l-en">Table <code>price</code> for <code>included_persons</code> people. Each extra person adds <code>supplement_price</code>. The deposit comes from <code>deposit</code> (<code>none</code>, <code>fixed</code> or <code>percent</code>). If <code>full_payment</code> is <code>true</code>, everything can be paid upfront.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Propinas en reservas</span><span class="l-en">Booking tips</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tip-rates?organization_id=...</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Porcentajes (<code>value</code>). <code>is_default</code> marca la opción preseleccionada.</span><span class="l-en">Percentages (<code>value</code>). <code>is_default</code> marks the preselected option.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><h3 id="flujo-3-s8" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Qué mostrar al comprador</span><span class="l-en">What to show the buyer</span></h3><ul><li><span class="l-es">Precio de la entrada y gastos de gestión por separado, y el total.</span><span class="l-en">Ticket price and service fees separately, plus the total.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Nombre del tramo, <code>includes</code> y <code>additional_info</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Tier name, <code>includes</code> and <code>additional_info</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Complementos y garantía como opciones, con su precio.</span><span class="l-en">Add-ons and guarantee as options, with their price.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">El total final antes de enviarlo a pagar.</span><span class="l-en">The final total before sending to payment.</span></li></ul><div class="check" data-flow="3"><div class="check-h"><span class="l-es">Checklist</span><span class="l-en">Checklist</span><span class="check-n" data-count="3"></span></div><ul><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="3-0"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Tratas los importes como unidades de moneda (no céntimos) y los validas con los decimales de cada moneda.</span><span class="l-en">You treat amounts as currency units (not cents) and validate them against each currency's decimals.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="3-1"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Sigues el orden de cálculo y calculas los gastos sobre el precio con descuento, según <code>fee_type</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You follow the calculation order and calculate fees on the discounted price, according to <code>fee_type</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="3-2"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Usas <code>pricing-info</code> cuando la compra es de más de una entrada.</span><span class="l-en">You use <code>pricing-info</code> when the purchase is for more than one ticket.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="3-3"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Envías <code>price_id</code> y gestionas <code>conditions_changed</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You send <code>price_id</code> and handle <code>conditions_changed</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="3-4"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Cargas impuestos personalizados y propinas de cada venue con el que trabajas.</span><span class="l-en">You load custom taxes and tips for every venue you work with.</span></label></li></ul></div></div></article></div></div><div class="panel has-side" role="tabpanel" id="tab-entradas" data-tab="entradas" hidden><aside class="side"><nav class="side-nav"><a href="#flujo-4"><span class="num">04</span><span class="l-es">Venta de tickets con pasarela de Fourvenues</span><span class="l-en">Ticket sales with the Fourvenues gateway</span></a><a href="#flujo-5"><span class="num">05</span><span class="l-es">Venta de tickets con pasarela externa</span><span class="l-en">Ticket sales with an external gateway</span></a></nav></aside><div class="panel-main"><div class="tab-head"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Venta de tickets</span><span class="l-en">Ticket sales</span></div><h2 class="tab-h"><span class="l-es">Elige uno o los dos</span><span class="l-en">Choose one or both</span></h2></div><section class="card intro"><p><span class="l-es">Hay dos formas de vender tickets. Elige antes de empezar:</span><span class="l-en">There are two ways to sell tickets. Choose before you start:</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es"></span><span class="l-en"></span></th><th><span class="l-es">Flujo 4: pasarela de Fourvenues</span><span class="l-en">Flow 4: Fourvenues gateway</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Flujo 5: pasarela externa</span><span class="l-en">Flow 5: external gateway</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="l-es">Quién cobra</span><span class="l-en">Who charges</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Fourvenues</span><span class="l-en">Fourvenues</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Tú, con tu pasarela</span><span class="l-en">You, with your gateway</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Endpoint</span><span class="l-en">Endpoint</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets/checkout</code></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets</code></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Precio y gastos</span><span class="l-en">Price and fees</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Los calcula Fourvenues desde la tarifa</span><span class="l-en">Fourvenues calculates them from the rate</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Los calculas tú y los envías</span><span class="l-en">You calculate and send them</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Confirmación</span><span class="l-en">Confirmation</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Webhook <code>payment.success</code></span><span class="l-en"><code>payment.success</code> webhook</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Respuesta de la llamada</span><span class="l-en">The call's response</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Requisito</span><span class="l-en">Requirement</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Alta en la pasarela de pagos de Fourvenues (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-1">flujo 1</a>, paso 4)</span><span class="l-en">Onboarding onto the Fourvenues payment gateway (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-1">flow 1</a>, step 4)</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Tu propia pasarela</span><span class="l-en">Your own gateway</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div></section><article class="card flow" id="flujo-4" data-flow="4"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow</span> <b>04</b></div><h2><span class="l-es">Venta de tickets con pasarela de Fourvenues</span><span class="l-en">Ticket sales with the Fourvenues gateway</span></h2><div class="flow-body"><p><span class="l-es">Creas una sesión de pago con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets/checkout</code>, rediriges al comprador a la <code>payment_url</code> y Fourvenues cobra. La venta se confirma cuando recibes el webhook <code>payment.success</code>. No gestionas datos de tarjeta ni reembolsos de pasarela.</span><span class="l-en">You create a payment session with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets/checkout</code>, redirect the buyer to the <code>payment_url</code> and Fourvenues charges. The sale is confirmed when you receive the <code>payment.success</code> webhook. You don't handle card data or gateway refunds.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-4-s1" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Resumen del flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow summary</span></h3><ol><li><span class="l-es">Validas la tarifa y el precio (flujos 2 y 3).</span><span class="l-en">You validate the rate and price (flows 2 and 3).</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Creas la sesión con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets/checkout</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You create the session with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets/checkout</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Rediriges al comprador a la <code>payment_url</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You redirect the buyer to the <code>payment_url</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">El comprador paga en Fourvenues.</span><span class="l-en">The buyer pays on Fourvenues.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Recibes <code>payment.success</code> y das la venta por buena.</span><span class="l-en">You receive <code>payment.success</code> and consider the sale complete.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">El comprador vuelve a tu <code>redirect_url</code> (o a <code>error_url</code> si falla).</span><span class="l-en">The buyer returns to your <code>redirect_url</code> (or <code>error_url</code> if it fails).</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Entregas las entradas, o lo hace Fourvenues si pediste <code>send_resources</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You deliver the tickets, or Fourvenues does if you asked for <code>send_resources</code>.</span></li></ol><h3 id="flujo-4-s2" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 1. Valida la tarifa justo antes de comprar</span><span class="l-en">Step 1. Validate the rate right before buying</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Lee la tarifa (<code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/ticket-rates/{id}</code>) y comprueba:</span><span class="l-en">Read the rate (<code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/ticket-rates/{id}</code>) and check:</span></p><ul><li><span class="l-es"><code>available</code> es <code>true</code> y hay stock (<code>availability.available</code>) para la cantidad elegida.</span><span class="l-en"><code>available</code> is <code>true</code> and there is stock (<code>availability.available</code>) for the chosen quantity.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">La cantidad está entre <code>min</code> y <code>max</code>.</span><span class="l-en">The quantity is between <code>min</code> and <code>max</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">El precio que vas a enviar coincide con el que ha visto el comprador. Para varias entradas, usa <code>pricing-info</code> (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flujo 3</a>).</span><span class="l-en">The price you will send matches the one the buyer saw. For several tickets, use <code>pricing-info</code> (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flow 3</a>).</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Tienes todos los datos obligatorios del comprador y las respuestas a las <code>questions</code> con <code>required: true</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You have all the buyer's required data and answers to <code>questions</code> with <code>required: true</code>.</span></li></ul><h3 id="flujo-4-s3" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 2. Crea la sesión de pago</span><span class="l-en">Step 2. Create the payment session</span></h3><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">cURL</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> POST \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/tickets/checkout'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'Content-Type: application/json'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--data</span> '{
    <span class="t-k">"ticket_rate_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"tr_111"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"redirect_url"</span>: <span class="t-s">"https://tuapp.com/pedido/987/ok"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"error_url"</span>: <span class="t-s">"https://tuapp.com/pedido/987/error"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"send_resources"</span>: <span class="t-kw">true</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"metadata"</span>: { <span class="t-k">"order_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"987"</span> },
    <span class="t-k">"tickets"</span>: [
      {
        <span class="t-k">"price_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"price_early"</span>,
        <span class="t-k">"full_name"</span>: <span class="t-s">"Ana García"</span>,
        <span class="t-k">"email"</span>: <span class="t-s">"ana@example.com"</span>,
        <span class="t-k">"phone"</span>: <span class="t-s">"+34600000000"</span>,
        <span class="t-k">"warranty"</span>: <span class="t-kw">false</span>,
        <span class="t-k">"answers"</span>: [ { <span class="t-k">"question_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"q_1"</span>, <span class="t-k">"answer"</span>: <span class="t-s">"Sí"</span> } ]
      }
    ]
  }'</code></pre></div><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Obligatorio</span><span class="l-en">Required</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué es</span><span class="l-en">What it is</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>ticket_rate_id</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Sí</span><span class="l-en">Yes</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Tarifa que se compra. Una sesión es de una sola tarifa.</span><span class="l-en">Rate being bought. A session covers a single rate.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>redirect_url</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Sí</span><span class="l-en">Yes</span></td><td><span class="l-es">A dónde vuelve el comprador si el pago va bien.</span><span class="l-en">Where the buyer returns if payment succeeds.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>error_url</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Sí</span><span class="l-en">Yes</span></td><td><span class="l-es">A dónde vuelve si el pago falla.</span><span class="l-en">Where they return if payment fails.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>tickets[]</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Sí</span><span class="l-en">Yes</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Un objeto por entrada, con los datos del asistente.</span><span class="l-en">One object per ticket, with the attendee's data.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>tickets[].price_id</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Recomendado</span><span class="l-en">Recommended</span></td><td><span class="l-es">El precio que has mostrado. Sirve para detectar cambios de precio.</span><span class="l-en">The price you displayed. Used to detect price changes.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>tickets[].full_name</code>, <code>email</code>, <code>phone</code>…</td><td><span class="l-es">Según la tarifa</span><span class="l-en">Depends on the rate</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Datos del asistente que exige la tarifa.</span><span class="l-en">Attendee data required by the rate.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>tickets[].answers</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si hay preguntas obligatorias</span><span class="l-en">If there are required questions</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Respuestas a las <code>questions</code> de la tarifa.</span><span class="l-en">Answers to the rate's <code>questions</code>.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>tickets[].warranty</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si la tarifa tiene garantía</span><span class="l-en">If the rate has a guarantee</span></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>true</code> si el comprador la añade.</span><span class="l-en"><code>true</code> if the buyer adds it.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>tickets[].supplements</code></td><td><span class="l-es">No</span><span class="l-en">No</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Complementos (<code>supplement_id</code> y <code>quantity</code>). Ver el <a class="xref" href="#flujo-10">flujo 10</a>.</span><span class="l-en">Add-ons (<code>supplement_id</code> and <code>quantity</code>). See <a class="xref" href="#flujo-10">flow 10</a>.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>tickets[].qr_code</code></td><td><span class="l-es">No</span><span class="l-en">No</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Opcional. Si no lo envías, Fourvenues asigna uno automáticamente (lo habitual). Si lo envías, cada entrada debe tener un código distinto.</span><span class="l-en">Optional. If you don't send it, Fourvenues assigns one automatically (the usual case). If you send it, each ticket must have a different code.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>discount_code</code></td><td><span class="l-es">No</span><span class="l-en">No</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Se aplica a todas las entradas de la sesión.</span><span class="l-en">Applies to all tickets in the session.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>send_resources</code></td><td><span class="l-es">No</span><span class="l-en">No</span></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>true</code> para que Fourvenues envíe las entradas por email (y por WhatsApp si está activo, con coste por entrada; ver <a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flujo 3</a>).</span><span class="l-en"><code>true</code> for Fourvenues to send the tickets by email (and WhatsApp if enabled, with a per-ticket cost; see <a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flow 3</a>).</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>metadata</code></td><td><span class="l-es">No</span><span class="l-en">No</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Tu identificador de pedido. Máximo 1 KB, sin datos personales. Vuelve en el webhook.</span><span class="l-en">Your order identifier. Max 1 KB, no personal data. It comes back in the webhook.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><h3 id="flujo-4-s4" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 3. Revisa la respuesta antes de redirigir</span><span class="l-en">Step 3. Check the response before redirecting</span></h3><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">JSON</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code>{
  <span class="t-k">"success"</span>: <span class="t-kw">true</span>,
  <span class="t-k">"data"</span>: {
    <span class="t-k">"payment_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"pay_123"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"payment_url"</span>: <span class="t-s">"https://..."</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"total_amount"</span>: <span class="t-n">13.2</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"conditions_changed"</span>: <span class="t-kw">false</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"tickets"</span>: [ { <span class="t-k">"_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"tk_1"</span>, <span class="t-k">"status"</span>: <span class="t-s">"pending_payment"</span>, <span class="t-k">"qr_code"</span>: <span class="t-s">"ABCDE12345"</span>, <span class="t-k">"total_price"</span>: <span class="t-n">13.2</span> } ]
  }
}</code></pre></div><ul><li><span class="l-es">Guarda <code>payment_id</code> y los <code>_id</code> de las entradas junto a tu pedido.</span><span class="l-en">Store <code>payment_id</code> and the tickets' <code>_id</code> with your order.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Si <code>conditions_changed</code> es <code>true</code>, el precio ha cambiado desde que lo mostraste. Enseña al comprador el nuevo <code>total_amount</code> y pide confirmación antes de redirigir.</span><span class="l-en">If <code>conditions_changed</code> is <code>true</code>, the price changed since you displayed it. Show the buyer the new <code>total_amount</code> and ask for confirmation before redirecting.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Comprueba que <code>total_amount</code> coincide con el total que has mostrado.</span><span class="l-en">Check that <code>total_amount</code> matches the total you displayed.</span></li></ul><h3 id="flujo-4-s5" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 4. Redirige al comprador</span><span class="l-en">Step 4. Redirect the buyer</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Envía al comprador a <code>payment_url</code>. Al terminar vuelve a <code>redirect_url</code> o a <code>error_url</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Send the buyer to <code>payment_url</code>. When done they return to <code>redirect_url</code> or <code>error_url</code>.</span></p><div class="note warn"><span class="note-i" aria-hidden="true">!</span><p><span class="l-es">La vuelta a <code>redirect_url</code> no confirma el pago. Muestra «pago en proceso» hasta que llegue el webhook.</span><span class="l-en">Returning to <code>redirect_url</code> does not confirm payment. Show "payment in progress" until the webhook arrives.</span></p></div><h3 id="flujo-4-s6" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 5. Confirma con el webhook</span><span class="l-en">Step 5. Confirm with the webhook</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Cuando llega <code>payment.success</code> (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-16">flujo 16</a>):</span><span class="l-en">When <code>payment.success</code> arrives (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-16">flow 16</a>):</span></p><ol><li><span class="l-es">Verifica la firma.</span><span class="l-en">Verify the signature.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Localiza tu pedido con <code>metadata</code> o <code>payment_id</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Find your order with <code>metadata</code> or <code>payment_id</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Para cada id de <code>resource_ids</code>, consulta <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets/{id}</code> y guarda el <code>qr_code</code> y el <code>status</code>.</span><span class="l-en">For each id in <code>resource_ids</code>, query <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets/{id}</code> and store the <code>qr_code</code> and <code>status</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Marca el pedido como pagado.</span><span class="l-en">Mark the order as paid.</span></li></ol><h3 id="flujo-4-s7" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 6. Entrega las entradas</span><span class="l-en">Step 6. Deliver the tickets</span></h3><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">send_resources</span><span class="l-en">send_resources</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Quién entrega</span><span class="l-en">Who delivers</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>true</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Fourvenues envía las entradas al email del asistente, y por WhatsApp si está activo (con coste por entrada; ver <a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flujo 3</a>).</span><span class="l-en">Fourvenues sends the tickets to the attendee's email, and by WhatsApp if enabled (with a per-ticket cost; see <a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flow 3</a>).</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>false</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Tú. Muestra o envía el <code>qr_code</code> de cada entrada desde tu plataforma.</span><span class="l-en">You. Display or send each ticket's <code>qr_code</code> from your platform.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><h3 id="flujo-4-s8" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Estados de la entrada</span><span class="l-en">Ticket statuses</span></h3><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Estado</span><span class="l-en">Status</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Significado</span><span class="l-en">Meaning</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>pending_payment</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Creada, esperando el pago.</span><span class="l-en">Created, awaiting payment.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>processing</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Pago en curso.</span><span class="l-en">Payment in progress.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>active</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Pagada y válida para entrar.</span><span class="l-en">Paid and valid for entry.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>voided</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Anulada (por ejemplo, tras un reembolso).</span><span class="l-en">Voided (for example, after a refund).</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><h3 id="flujo-4-s9" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Errores habituales</span><span class="l-en">Common errors</span></h3><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Error</span><span class="l-en">Error</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Causa</span><span class="l-en">Cause</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué hacer</span><span class="l-en">What to do</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Complemento inexistente, cantidad fuera de límites o más de un complemento gratuito por entrada</span><span class="l-en">Non-existent add-on, quantity out of bounds or more than one free add-on per ticket</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Revisa los <code>supplements</code> contra la tarifa.</span><span class="l-en">Check <code>supplements</code> against the rate.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>422</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Falta un dato obligatorio del asistente o un campo tiene un valor no válido</span><span class="l-en">A required attendee field is missing or a field has an invalid value</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Revisa los datos que exige la tarifa.</span><span class="l-en">Check the data required by the rate.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>conditions_changed: true</code></td><td><span class="l-es">El precio ha cambiado</span><span class="l-en">The price has changed</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Muestra el nuevo total antes de redirigir.</span><span class="l-en">Show the new total before redirecting.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">No llega el webhook</span><span class="l-en">Webhook doesn't arrive</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Tu endpoint falló o estuvo caído</span><span class="l-en">Your endpoint failed or was down</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Consulta <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/payments/{payment_id}</code> y concilia.</span><span class="l-en">Query <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/payments/{payment_id}</code> and reconcile.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="check" data-flow="4"><div class="check-h"><span class="l-es">Checklist</span><span class="l-en">Checklist</span><span class="check-n" data-count="4"></span></div><ul><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="4-0"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Validas la tarifa justo antes de crear la sesión.</span><span class="l-en">You validate the rate right before creating the session.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="4-1"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Envías <code>price_id</code> y gestionas <code>conditions_changed</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You send <code>price_id</code> and handle <code>conditions_changed</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="4-2"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Guardas <code>payment_id</code> y tu <code>order_id</code> en <code>metadata</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You store <code>payment_id</code> and your <code>order_id</code> in <code>metadata</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="4-3"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Solo das la venta por buena al recibir <code>payment.success</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You only consider the sale complete when you receive <code>payment.success</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="4-4"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Tu <code>redirect_url</code> muestra «pago en proceso» si el webhook aún no ha llegado.</span><span class="l-en">Your <code>redirect_url</code> shows "payment in progress" if the webhook hasn't arrived yet.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="4-5"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Tienes decidido quién entrega las entradas (<code>send_resources</code>).</span><span class="l-en">You have decided who delivers the tickets (<code>send_resources</code>).</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="4-6"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Has hecho una compra de prueba completa en alpha.</span><span class="l-en">You have made a full test purchase in alpha.</span></label></li></ul></div></div></article><article class="card flow" id="flujo-5" data-flow="5"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow</span> <b>05</b></div><h2><span class="l-es">Venta de tickets con pasarela externa</span><span class="l-en">Ticket sales with an external gateway</span></h2><div class="flow-body"><p><span class="l-es">Cobras al comprador con tu propia pasarela y después creas las entradas en Fourvenues con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets</code>. Las entradas se crean ya activas y la respuesta de la llamada es la confirmación: no hay webhook de pago. Como cobras tú, también eres responsable del importe que envías y de devolver el dinero si algo falla.</span><span class="l-en">You charge the buyer with your own gateway and then create the tickets in Fourvenues with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets</code>. Tickets are created already active and the call's response is the confirmation: there is no payment webhook. Since you charge, you are also responsible for the amount you send and for refunding if something fails.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-5-s1" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Resumen del flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow summary</span></h3><ol><li><span class="l-es">Validas la tarifa y calculas el precio (flujos 2 y 3).</span><span class="l-en">You validate the rate and calculate the price (flows 2 and 3).</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Cobras al comprador en tu pasarela. Si puedes, solo autoriza el cargo.</span><span class="l-en">You charge the buyer on your gateway. If you can, only authorize the charge.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Validas la tarifa otra vez, justo antes de crear las entradas.</span><span class="l-en">You validate the rate again, right before creating the tickets.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Creas las entradas con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You create the tickets with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Si la creación va bien, capturas el cargo. Si falla, anulas o devuelves el cargo.</span><span class="l-en">If creation succeeds, you capture the charge. If it fails, you void or refund the charge.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Entregas las entradas, o lo hace Fourvenues si pediste <code>send_resources</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You deliver the tickets, or Fourvenues does if you asked for <code>send_resources</code>.</span></li></ol><h3 id="flujo-5-s2" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 1. Calcula el importe de cada entrada</span><span class="l-en">Step 1. Calculate each ticket's amount</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Aquí Fourvenues no calcula nada por ti. Aplica las reglas del <a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flujo 3</a> con el tramo vigente:</span><span class="l-en">Here Fourvenues calculates nothing for you. Apply the rules from <a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flow 3</a> with the current tier:</span></p><ul><li><span class="l-es"><code>total_price</code> = precio con descuento + gastos de gestión + garantía y sus gastos (si la compra), calculados en el orden del <a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flujo 3</a>. No incluye los complementos, que van aparte.</span><span class="l-en"><code>total_price</code> = discounted price + service fee + warranty and its fee (if bought), calculated in the order from <a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flow 3</a>. It does not include add-ons, which go separately.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><code>fee</code> = los gastos de gestión incluidos en <code>total_price</code>.</span><span class="l-en"><code>fee</code> = the service fees included in <code>total_price</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><code>price_id</code> = el <code>_id</code> del tramo que has cobrado.</span><span class="l-en"><code>price_id</code> = the <code>_id</code> of the tier you charged.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Si el comprador compra varias entradas, usa <code>pricing-info</code> para saber qué tramo corresponde a cada una.</span><span class="l-en">If the buyer buys several tickets, use <code>pricing-info</code> to know which tier applies to each.</span></li></ul><h3 id="flujo-5-s3" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 2. Cobra primero, crea después</span><span class="l-en">Step 2. Charge first, create after</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">El orden importa, porque entre tu cobro y la creación de las entradas la tarifa puede agotarse o cambiar.</span><span class="l-en">Order matters, because between your charge and ticket creation the rate can sell out or change.</span></p><div class="note info"><span class="note-i" aria-hidden="true">i</span><p><span class="l-es"><strong>Recomendado:</strong> autoriza el cargo, crea las entradas y captura solo si la creación ha ido bien. Si tu pasarela no permite autorizar y capturar por separado, cobra y devuelve el dinero de inmediato cuando <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets</code> falle.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Recommended:</strong> authorize the charge, create the tickets and capture only if creation succeeded. If your gateway can't authorize and capture separately, charge and refund immediately when <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets</code> fails.</span></p></div><p><span class="l-es">No crees entradas antes de cobrar. <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets</code> las crea activas y válidas para entrar desde ese momento.</span><span class="l-en">Don't create tickets before charging. <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets</code> creates them active and valid for entry from that moment.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-5-s4" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 3. Crea las entradas</span><span class="l-en">Step 3. Create the tickets</span></h3><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">cURL</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> POST \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/tickets'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'Content-Type: application/json'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--data</span> '{
    <span class="t-k">"ticket_rate_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"tr_111"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"send_resources"</span>: <span class="t-kw">false</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"tickets"</span>: [
      {
        <span class="t-k">"price_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"price_early"</span>,
        <span class="t-k">"total_price"</span>: <span class="t-n">13.2</span>,
        <span class="t-k">"fee"</span>: <span class="t-n">1.2</span>,
        <span class="t-k">"full_name"</span>: <span class="t-s">"Ana García"</span>,
        <span class="t-k">"email"</span>: <span class="t-s">"ana@example.com"</span>,
        <span class="t-k">"phone"</span>: <span class="t-s">"+34600000000"</span>,
        <span class="t-k">"warranty"</span>: { <span class="t-k">"enabled"</span>: <span class="t-kw">false</span> },
        <span class="t-k">"supplements"</span>: [ { <span class="t-k">"supplement_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"sup_1"</span>, <span class="t-k">"price"</span>: <span class="t-n">5</span>, <span class="t-k">"quantity"</span>: <span class="t-n">1</span> } ]
      }
    ]
  }'</code></pre></div><p><span class="l-es">Los campos son los del checkout (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flujo 4</a>) con estas diferencias:</span><span class="l-en">The fields are those of checkout (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flow 4</a>) with these differences:</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Obligatorio</span><span class="l-en">Required</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Diferencia con el checkout</span><span class="l-en">Difference from checkout</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>tickets[].total_price</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Sí</span><span class="l-en">Yes</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Lo envías tú: importe cobrado por la entrada, gastos incluidos.</span><span class="l-en">You send it: amount charged for the ticket, fees included.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>tickets[].fee</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Recomendado</span><span class="l-en">Recommended</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Gastos de gestión incluidos en <code>total_price</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Service fees included in <code>total_price</code>.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>tickets[].price_id</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Sí</span><span class="l-en">Yes</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Obligatorio aquí.</span><span class="l-en">Required here.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>tickets[].warranty</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si la tarifa tiene garantía</span><span class="l-en">If the rate has a guarantee</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Es un objeto: <code>enabled</code> y <code>hours</code> (horas antes del evento en que se puede usar).</span><span class="l-en">It is an object: <code>enabled</code> and <code>hours</code> (hours before the event it can be used).</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>tickets[].supplements</code></td><td><span class="l-es">No</span><span class="l-en">No</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Envías <code>supplement_id</code>, el <code>price</code> unitario cobrado y <code>quantity</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You send <code>supplement_id</code>, the unit <code>price</code> charged and <code>quantity</code>.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>tickets[].qr_code</code></td><td><span class="l-es">No</span><span class="l-en">No</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Opcional, igual que en el checkout. Si no lo envías, Fourvenues asigna uno automáticamente (lo habitual). Si lo envías, cada entrada debe tener un código distinto.</span><span class="l-en">Optional, same as checkout. If you don't send it, Fourvenues assigns one automatically (the usual case). If you send it, each ticket must have a different code.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>redirect_url</code>, <code>error_url</code>, <code>metadata</code></td><td>—</td><td><span class="l-es">No existen en esta llamada.</span><span class="l-en">They don't exist in this call.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="note warn"><span class="note-i" aria-hidden="true">!</span><p><span class="l-es"><strong>Los complementos gratuitos no se añaden solos.</strong> En el checkout de Fourvenues los complementos gratuitos y los mínimos obligatorios se añaden automáticamente. Aquí no: si la tarifa incluye un complemento gratuito o un mínimo, tienes que enviarlo tú.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Free add-ons are not added automatically.</strong> In the Fourvenues checkout, free add-ons and required minimums are added automatically. Not here: if the rate includes a free add-on or a minimum, you have to send it.</span></p></div><h3 id="flujo-5-s5" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 4. Guarda la respuesta</span><span class="l-en">Step 4. Store the response</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">La respuesta devuelve un array con las entradas creadas, cada una con su <code>_id</code>, <code>qr_code</code>, <code>status</code> (<code>active</code>) y el desglose de importes. Guarda los <code>_id</code> y <code>qr_code</code> junto a tu pedido.</span><span class="l-en">The response returns an array with the created tickets, each with its <code>_id</code>, <code>qr_code</code>, <code>status</code> (<code>active</code>) and amount breakdown. Store the <code>_id</code> and <code>qr_code</code> with your order.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-5-s6" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 5. Gestiona los fallos sin duplicar entradas</span><span class="l-en">Step 5. Handle failures without duplicating tickets</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Si la llamada falla con un error (<code>400</code>, <code>422</code>), no se ha creado ninguna entrada. Corrige los datos o anula el cargo.</span><span class="l-en">If the call fails with an error (<code>400</code>, <code>422</code>), no ticket was created. Fix the data or void the charge.</span></p><p><span class="l-es">Si la llamada da un timeout o no recibes respuesta, no sabes si las entradas se han creado. Antes de reintentar, comprueba si ya existen:</span><span class="l-en">If the call times out or you get no response, you don't know whether the tickets were created. Before retrying, check whether they already exist:</span></p><ul><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Si dejas que Fourvenues asigne el QR</strong> (lo habitual): consulta <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets?event_id=...&amp;ticket_rate_id=...</code> y busca entradas con el email, el teléfono y el nombre de los asistentes de tu pedido.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>If you let Fourvenues assign the QR</strong> (the usual case): query <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets?event_id=...&amp;ticket_rate_id=...</code> and look for tickets with the email, phone and name of your order's attendees.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Si envías tu propio <code>qr_code</code>:</strong> consulta <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets/by-code/{code}</code> con cada código.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>If you send your own <code>qr_code</code>:</strong> query <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets/by-code/{code}</code> with each code.</span></li></ul><p><span class="l-es">Si las entradas existen, no las vuelvas a crear: guárdalas en tu pedido y continúa.</span><span class="l-en">If the tickets exist, don't create them again: store them in your order and continue.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-5-s7" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 6. Entrega las entradas</span><span class="l-en">Step 6. Deliver the tickets</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Igual que en el <a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flujo 4</a>: con <code>send_resources: true</code> las envía Fourvenues. Con <code>false</code>, entregas tú el <code>qr_code</code> de cada entrada.</span><span class="l-en">Same as <a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flow 4</a>: with <code>send_resources: true</code> Fourvenues sends them. With <code>false</code>, you deliver each ticket's <code>qr_code</code>.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-5-s8" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Reembolsos</span><span class="l-en">Refunds</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Como el dinero lo has cobrado tú, el reembolso también lo haces tú en tu pasarela. Fourvenues te avisa con el webhook <code>ticket.request_refund</code> cuando hay que devolver una entrada, y procesarlo es obligatorio: devuelves el dinero y lo registras en Fourvenues para anular la entrada. Se detalla en el <a class="xref" href="#flujo-14">flujo 14</a>.</span><span class="l-en">Since you took the money, you also refund it on your gateway. Fourvenues notifies you with the <code>ticket.request_refund</code> webhook when a ticket has to be refunded, and processing it is required: you refund the money and record it in Fourvenues to void the ticket. See <a class="xref" href="#flujo-14">flow 14</a>.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-5-s9" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Errores habituales</span><span class="l-en">Common errors</span></h3><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Error</span><span class="l-en">Error</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Causa</span><span class="l-en">Cause</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué hacer</span><span class="l-en">What to do</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Complemento inexistente, cantidad fuera de límites o más de un complemento gratuito por entrada</span><span class="l-en">Non-existent add-on, quantity out of bounds or more than one free add-on per ticket</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Revisa los <code>supplements</code> contra la tarifa.</span><span class="l-en">Check <code>supplements</code> against the rate.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>403</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Tu key no tiene permiso para crear entradas</span><span class="l-en">Your key has no permission to create tickets</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Revisa las capabilities de tu key.</span><span class="l-en">Check your key's capabilities.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>422</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Falta un dato obligatorio o un valor no es válido</span><span class="l-en">A required field is missing or a value is invalid</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Revisa los datos que exige la tarifa.</span><span class="l-en">Check the data required by the rate.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Timeout</span><span class="l-en">Timeout</span></td><td><span class="l-es">No sabes si se crearon</span><span class="l-en">You don't know whether they were created</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Comprueba si ya existen antes de reintentar (paso 5).</span><span class="l-en">Check whether they exist before retrying (step 5).</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="check" data-flow="5"><div class="check-h"><span class="l-es">Checklist</span><span class="l-en">Checklist</span><span class="check-n" data-count="5"></span></div><ul><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="5-0"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Calculas <code>total_price</code> y <code>fee</code> con las reglas del <a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flujo 3</a> y el tramo vigente.</span><span class="l-en">You calculate <code>total_price</code> and <code>fee</code> with <a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flow 3</a> rules and the current tier.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="5-1"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Validas la tarifa justo antes de crear las entradas.</span><span class="l-en">You validate the rate right before creating the tickets.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="5-2"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Autorizas el cargo antes de crear las entradas y lo capturas solo si la creación va bien.</span><span class="l-en">You authorize the charge before creating tickets and capture it only if creation succeeds.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="5-3"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Tienes un proceso para devolver el dinero si <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets</code> falla.</span><span class="l-en">You have a process to refund if <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets</code> fails.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="5-4"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Tras un timeout, compruebas si las entradas ya existen antes de reintentar.</span><span class="l-en">After a timeout, you check whether the tickets exist before retrying.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="5-5"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Envías los complementos gratuitos y los mínimos obligatorios.</span><span class="l-en">You send free add-ons and required minimums.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="5-6"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Has hecho una compra de prueba completa en alpha.</span><span class="l-en">You have made a full test purchase in alpha.</span></label></li></ul></div></div></article></div></div><div class="panel has-side" role="tabpanel" id="tab-reservas" data-tab="reservas" hidden><aside class="side"><nav class="side-nav"><a href="#flujo-6"><span class="num">06</span><span class="l-es">Reserva con pasarela de Fourvenues</span><span class="l-en">Booking with the Fourvenues gateway</span></a><a href="#flujo-7"><span class="num">07</span><span class="l-es">Solicitud de reserva sin pago</span><span class="l-en">Booking request without payment</span></a></nav></aside><div class="panel-main"><div class="tab-head"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Reservas</span><span class="l-en">Bookings</span></div><h2 class="tab-h"><span class="l-es">Elige uno o los dos</span><span class="l-en">Choose one or both</span></h2></div><section class="card intro"><p><span class="l-es">Hay dos formas de reservar como canal. Elige antes de empezar:</span><span class="l-en">There are two ways to book as a channel. Choose before you start:</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es"></span><span class="l-en"></span></th><th><span class="l-es">Flujo 6: pasarela de Fourvenues</span><span class="l-en">Flow 6: Fourvenues gateway</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Flujo 7: solicitud sin pago</span><span class="l-en">Flow 7: request without payment</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="l-es">Quién cobra</span><span class="l-en">Who charges</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Fourvenues</span><span class="l-en">Fourvenues</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Nadie al reservar</span><span class="l-en">Nobody at booking time</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Endpoint</span><span class="l-en">Endpoint</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/bookings/checkout</code></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/bookings/request</code></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Confirmación</span><span class="l-en">Confirmation</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Al completarse el pago</span><span class="l-en">When payment completes</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Cuando el venue la acepta en FV Pro</span><span class="l-en">When the venue accepts it in FV Pro</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">Las reservas cobradas con una pasarela externa solo están disponibles para los propios venues, con una clave de la Integrations API. No forman parte de esta guía.</span><span class="l-en">Bookings charged through an external gateway are only available to venues themselves, with an Integrations API key. They are not part of this guide.</span></p></section><article class="card flow" id="flujo-6" data-flow="6"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow</span> <b>06</b></div><h2><span class="l-es">Reserva con pasarela de Fourvenues</span><span class="l-en">Booking with the Fourvenues gateway</span></h2><div class="flow-body"><p><span class="l-es">El comprador elige zona, tarifa y, si el venue lo permite, mesa. Creas la sesión de pago con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/bookings/checkout</code> y Fourvenues cobra el depósito o el importe completo. El proceso es el mismo que en la venta de tickets con checkout (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flujo 4</a>), pero con los datos de la reserva.</span><span class="l-en">The buyer chooses a zone, rate and, if the venue allows it, a table. You create the payment session with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/bookings/checkout</code> and Fourvenues charges the deposit or the full amount. The process is the same as ticket sales with checkout (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flow 4</a>), but with booking data.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-6-s1" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Resumen del flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow summary</span></h3><ol><li><span class="l-es">Consultas la disponibilidad para el número de personas.</span><span class="l-en">You check availability for the number of people.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">El comprador elige zona, tarifa y, si se permite, mesa.</span><span class="l-en">The buyer chooses zone, rate and, if allowed, table.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Calculas y muestras lo que paga ahora y sus condiciones.</span><span class="l-en">You calculate and show what they pay now and the conditions.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Creas la sesión con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/bookings/checkout</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You create the session with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/bookings/checkout</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Rediriges al comprador a la <code>payment_url</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You redirect the buyer to the <code>payment_url</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Confirmas el pago y el estado de la reserva.</span><span class="l-en">You confirm the payment and the booking status.</span></li></ol><h3 id="flujo-6-s2" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 1. Consulta la disponibilidad</span><span class="l-en">Step 1. Check availability</span></h3><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">cURL</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> GET \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/bookings/availability?event_id=ev_abc123&amp;quantity=6'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span></code></pre></div><p><span class="l-es">La respuesta es una lista de zonas con sitio para ese número de personas. En cada zona:</span><span class="l-en">The response is a list of zones with room for that number of people. In each zone:</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué te dice</span><span class="l-en">What it tells you</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>available</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si la zona está a la venta. Es <code>false</code> cuando ha pasado el límite de venta del evento.</span><span class="l-en">Whether the zone is on sale. It is <code>false</code> once the event's sales cutoff has passed.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>is_full</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si la zona está llena.</span><span class="l-en">Whether the zone is full.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>slug</code>, <code>normalized_zone_name</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Identificadores de la zona para la reserva.</span><span class="l-en">Zone identifiers for the booking.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>can_select_client</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si el comprador puede elegir mesa. Si es <code>false</code>, la mesa la asigna el venue.</span><span class="l-en">Whether the buyer can choose a table. If <code>false</code>, the venue assigns the table.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>spaces</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Mesas de la zona, cada una con <code>capacity</code>, <code>available</code> y sus tarifas (<code>rates</code>).</span><span class="l-en">Zone tables, each with <code>capacity</code>, <code>available</code> and its rates (<code>rates</code>).</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>rates</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Tarifas de la zona cuando no tiene mesas.</span><span class="l-en">Zone rates when it has no tables.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>background_image</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Plano de la zona, si lo hay. Las mesas traen su <code>position</code> para dibujarlas encima.</span><span class="l-en">Zone floor plan, if any. Tables include their <code>position</code> to draw them on top.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">Como con las entradas, la disponibilidad cambia con cada reserva. Vuelve a consultarla al iniciar la compra y justo antes de crear la sesión.</span><span class="l-en">As with tickets, availability changes with every booking. Check it again when starting the purchase and right before creating the session.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-6-s3" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 2. Muestra la tarifa y sus condiciones</span><span class="l-en">Step 2. Show the rate and its conditions</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Cada tarifa de mesa trae:</span><span class="l-en">Each table rate includes:</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué es</span><span class="l-en">What it is</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>name</code>, <code>content</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Nombre y lo que incluye (p. ej. «1 botella de champán. Zona VIP con sofá»).</span><span class="l-en">Name and what it includes (e.g. "1 bottle of champagne. VIP area with sofa").</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>conditions</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Condiciones de la reserva (consumo mínimo, dress code…). Muéstralas siempre antes de pagar.</span><span class="l-en">Booking conditions (minimum spend, dress code…). Always show them before payment.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>price</code>, <code>included_persons</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Precio de la tarifa y personas incluidas.</span><span class="l-en">Rate price and people included.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>supplement_price</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Precio por cada persona adicional.</span><span class="l-en">Price per additional person.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>max_clients</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Máximo de personas en la mesa.</span><span class="l-en">Maximum people at the table.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>deposit</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Depósito a pagar al reservar: <code>none</code>, <code>fixed</code> (importe fijo) o <code>percent</code> (porcentaje de <code>price</code>).</span><span class="l-en">Deposit payable at booking: <code>none</code>, <code>fixed</code> (fixed amount) or <code>percent</code> (percentage of <code>price</code>).</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>full_payment</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si es <code>true</code>, el comprador puede pagar el total por adelantado en lugar del depósito.</span><span class="l-en">If <code>true</code>, the buyer can pay the full amount upfront instead of the deposit.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">Muestra con claridad qué paga ahora (depósito o total, más gastos y propina) y qué queda pendiente según las condiciones de la tarifa. Cómo se cobra el resto lo decide el venue.</span><span class="l-en">Show clearly what they pay now (deposit or total, plus fees and tip) and what remains pending under the rate's conditions. How the rest is charged is up to the venue.</span></p><p><span class="l-es"><strong>Propinas.</strong> Si el venue las usa, <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tip-rates?organization_id=...</code> devuelve los porcentajes disponibles. <code>is_default</code> marca el que va preseleccionado. Envía el elegido como <code>tip_rate_id</code>.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Tips.</strong> If the venue uses them, <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tip-rates?organization_id=...</code> returns the available percentages. <code>is_default</code> marks the preselected one. Send the chosen one as <code>tip_rate_id</code>.</span></p><p><span class="l-es"><strong>Descuentos.</strong> Valida el código antes de crear la sesión con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/discount-codes/bookings/validate/{code}</code> y envíalo como <code>discount_code</code>.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Discounts.</strong> Validate the code before creating the session with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/discount-codes/bookings/validate/{code}</code> and send it as <code>discount_code</code>.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-6-s4" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 3. Crea la sesión de pago</span><span class="l-en">Step 3. Create the payment session</span></h3><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">cURL</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> POST \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/bookings/checkout'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'Content-Type: application/json'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--data</span> '{
    <span class="t-k">"event_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"ev_abc123"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"zone_slug"</span>: <span class="t-s">"vip"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"rate_slug"</span>: <span class="t-s">"mesa-vip"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"table_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"tb_12"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"full_payment"</span>: <span class="t-kw">false</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"tip_rate_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"tip_10"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"redirect_url"</span>: <span class="t-s">"https://tuapp.com/reserva/555/ok"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"error_url"</span>: <span class="t-s">"https://tuapp.com/reserva/555/error"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"send_resources"</span>: <span class="t-kw">true</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"metadata"</span>: { <span class="t-k">"order_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"555"</span> },
    <span class="t-k">"observations_client"</span>: <span class="t-s">"Celebramos un cumpleaños"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"marketing_consent"</span>: <span class="t-kw">false</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"info"</span>: {
      <span class="t-k">"full_name"</span>: <span class="t-s">"Ana García"</span>,
      <span class="t-k">"email"</span>: <span class="t-s">"ana@example.com"</span>,
      <span class="t-k">"phone"</span>: <span class="t-s">"+34600000000"</span>,
      <span class="t-k">"quantity"</span>: <span class="t-n">6</span>
    }
  }'</code></pre></div><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué es</span><span class="l-en">What it is</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>event_id</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Evento de la reserva.</span><span class="l-en">Booking event.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>zone_slug</code> o <code>normalized_zone_name</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Zona elegida. Usa uno de los dos.</span><span class="l-en">Chosen zone. Use one of the two.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>rate_slug</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Tarifa elegida.</span><span class="l-en">Chosen rate.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>table_id</code> o <code>normalized_table_name</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Mesa elegida. Solo si la zona tiene <code>can_select_client: true</code>. Usa uno de los dos.</span><span class="l-en">Chosen table. Only if the zone has <code>can_select_client: true</code>. Use one of the two.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>full_payment</code></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>true</code> para pagar el total. Solo si la tarifa lo permite.</span><span class="l-en"><code>true</code> to pay the full amount. Only if the rate allows it.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>info.full_name</code>, <code>email</code>, <code>phone</code>, <code>quantity</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Titular de la reserva y número de personas. <code>birthdate</code> es opcional.</span><span class="l-en">Booking holder and number of people. <code>birthdate</code> is optional.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>info.billing</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Datos de facturación, si el comprador quiere factura.</span><span class="l-en">Billing details, if the buyer wants an invoice.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>observations_client</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Comentarios del comprador para el venue.</span><span class="l-en">Buyer's comments for the venue.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>marketing_consent</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si el comprador acepta comunicaciones comerciales.</span><span class="l-en">Whether the buyer accepts marketing communications.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>tip_rate_id</code>, <code>discount_code</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Propina y código de descuento, opcionales.</span><span class="l-en">Tip and discount code, optional.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>redirect_url</code>, <code>error_url</code>, <code>send_resources</code>, <code>metadata</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Igual que en el checkout de tickets (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flujo 4</a>).</span><span class="l-en">Same as ticket checkout (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flow 4</a>).</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><h3 id="flujo-6-s5" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 4. Revisa la respuesta y redirige</span><span class="l-en">Step 4. Check the response and redirect</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">La respuesta trae <code>payment_id</code>, <code>payment_url</code>, <code>total_amount</code> y la reserva creada (<code>booking</code>), con su <code>_id</code> y <code>status</code>.</span><span class="l-en">The response includes <code>payment_id</code>, <code>payment_url</code>, <code>total_amount</code> and the created booking (<code>booking</code>), with its <code>_id</code> and <code>status</code>.</span></p><ul><li><span class="l-es">Guarda el <code>_id</code> de la reserva y el <code>payment_id</code> junto a tu pedido.</span><span class="l-en">Store the booking <code>_id</code> and <code>payment_id</code> with your order.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Comprueba que <code>total_amount</code> coincide con lo que has mostrado.</span><span class="l-en">Check that <code>total_amount</code> matches what you displayed.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Redirige al comprador a <code>payment_url</code>. Al terminar vuelve a <code>redirect_url</code> o a <code>error_url</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Redirect the buyer to <code>payment_url</code>. When done they return to <code>redirect_url</code> or <code>error_url</code>.</span></li></ul><h3 id="flujo-6-s6" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 5. Confirma la reserva</span><span class="l-en">Step 5. Confirm the booking</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">La vuelta a <code>redirect_url</code> no confirma el pago. Cuando el pago se completa, la reserva pasa a <code>accepted</code>. Consulta la reserva con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/bookings/{bookingId}</code> y comprueba que está en <code>accepted</code> antes de darla por confirmada.</span><span class="l-en">Returning to <code>redirect_url</code> does not confirm payment. When payment completes, the booking moves to <code>accepted</code>.
