Query the booking with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/bookings/{bookingId}</code> and check that it is <code>accepted</code> before considering it confirmed.</span></p><p><span class="l-es">Para dejar notas de tu canal al venue (por ejemplo, «cliente VIP»), usa <code class="ep"><span class="m m-patch">PATCH</span>/bookings/{bookingId}/observations</code> con <code>observations_referral</code>.</span><span class="l-en">To leave notes from your channel for the venue (for example, "VIP customer"), use <code class="ep"><span class="m m-patch">PATCH</span>/bookings/{bookingId}/observations</code> with <code>observations_referral</code>.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-6-s7" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Estados de una reserva</span><span class="l-en">Booking statuses</span></h3><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Estado</span><span class="l-en">Status</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Significado</span><span class="l-en">Meaning</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>pending</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Creada, pendiente de completar.</span><span class="l-en">Created, pending completion.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>filled-by-client</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Datos completados, esperando un pago externo. Solo se da en reservas de venues propios.</span><span class="l-en">Data completed, awaiting an external payment. Only occurs in venues' own bookings.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>to-review</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Pendiente de que el venue la revise.</span><span class="l-en">Pending venue review.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>accepted</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Confirmada.</span><span class="l-en">Confirmed.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>rejected</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Rechazada por el venue.</span><span class="l-en">Rejected by the venue.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>cancelled-by-client</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Cancelada por el cliente.</span><span class="l-en">Cancelled by the customer.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>not-completed</code></td><td><span class="l-es">El proceso no se completó.</span><span class="l-en">The process was not completed.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>arrived</code>, <code>arrived-partial</code></td><td><span class="l-es">El grupo ha llegado al venue, entero o en parte.</span><span class="l-en">The group has arrived at the venue, fully or partly.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>seated</code>, <code>seated-partial</code></td><td><span class="l-es">El grupo está en la mesa, entero o en parte.</span><span class="l-en">The group is at the table, fully or partly.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>released</code></td><td><span class="l-es">La mesa se ha liberado.</span><span class="l-en">The table has been released.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>no-show</code></td><td><span class="l-es">El grupo no se presentó.</span><span class="l-en">The group did not show up.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="check" data-flow="6"><div class="check-h"><span class="l-es">Checklist</span><span class="l-en">Checklist</span><span class="check-n" data-count="6"></span></div><ul><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="6-0"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Consultas la disponibilidad con el número de personas y la refrescas antes de crear la sesión.</span><span class="l-en">You check availability with the number of people and refresh it before creating the session.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="6-1"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Solo envías mesa si la zona permite elegirla.</span><span class="l-en">You only send a table if the zone allows choosing it.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="6-2"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Muestras <code>conditions</code> y el importe a pagar ahora antes de redirigir.</span><span class="l-en">You show <code>conditions</code> and the amount due now before redirecting.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="6-3"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Validas el código de descuento antes de enviarlo.</span><span class="l-en">You validate the discount code before sending it.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="6-4"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Guardas <code>_id</code> de la reserva y <code>payment_id</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You store the booking <code>_id</code> and <code>payment_id</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="6-5"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Confirmas el estado con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/bookings/{bookingId}</code> antes de dar la reserva por buena.</span><span class="l-en">You confirm the status with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/bookings/{bookingId}</code> before considering the booking complete.</span></label></li></ul></div></div></article><article class="card flow" id="flujo-7" data-flow="7"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow</span> <b>07</b></div><h2><span class="l-es">Solicitud de reserva sin pago</span><span class="l-en">Booking request without payment</span></h2><div class="flow-body"><p><span class="l-es">Envías una solicitud con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/bookings/request</code> y el venue la revisa en FV Pro para aceptarla o rechazarla. No se cobra nada al reservar. Úsalo cuando el venue gestiona las reservas a mano, por ejemplo en zonas VIP o para grupos grandes.</span><span class="l-en">You send a request with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/bookings/request</code> and the venue reviews it in FV Pro to accept or reject it. Nothing is charged at booking. Use it when the venue handles bookings manually, for example in VIP areas or for large groups.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-7-s1" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Resumen del flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow summary</span></h3><ol><li><span class="l-es">Consultas disponibilidad y tarifa (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-6">flujo 6</a>, pasos 1 y 2).</span><span class="l-en">You check availability and rate (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-6">flow 6</a>, steps 1 and 2).</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Envías la solicitud con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/bookings/request</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You send the request with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/bookings/request</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">El venue la revisa en FV Pro.</span><span class="l-en">The venue reviews it in FV Pro.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Consultas el estado hasta que el venue la acepte o la rechace.</span><span class="l-en">You check the status until the venue accepts or rejects it.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Informas al comprador del resultado.</span><span class="l-en">You inform the buyer of the outcome.</span></li></ol><h3 id="flujo-7-s2" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 1. Envía la solicitud</span><span class="l-en">Step 1. Send the request</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Los campos de zona, tarifa, mesa, titular y facturación son los mismos que en el <a class="xref" href="#flujo-6">flujo 6</a>. No se envían <code>redirect_url</code>, <code>error_url</code>, <code>full_payment</code> ni <code>tip_rate_id</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Zone, rate, table, holder and billing fields are the same as in <a class="xref" href="#flujo-6">flow 6</a>. <code>redirect_url</code>, <code>error_url</code>, <code>full_payment</code> and <code>tip_rate_id</code> are not sent.</span></p><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">cURL</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> POST \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/bookings/request'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'Content-Type: application/json'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--data</span> '{
    <span class="t-k">"event_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"ev_abc123"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"zone_slug"</span>: <span class="t-s">"vip"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"rate_slug"</span>: <span class="t-s">"mesa-vip"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"observations_client"</span>: <span class="t-s">"Somos 12, ¿podéis juntar dos mesas?"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"info"</span>: {
      <span class="t-k">"full_name"</span>: <span class="t-s">"Ana García"</span>,
      <span class="t-k">"email"</span>: <span class="t-s">"ana@example.com"</span>,
      <span class="t-k">"phone"</span>: <span class="t-s">"+34600000000"</span>,
      <span class="t-k">"quantity"</span>: <span class="t-n">12</span>
    }
  }'</code></pre></div><ul><li><span class="l-es"><code>send_resources</code> es <code>true</code> por defecto: Fourvenues envía al cliente un aviso de que ha recibido su solicitud. Envía <code>false</code> si prefieres avisarle tú.</span><span class="l-en"><code>send_resources</code> is <code>true</code> by default: Fourvenues sends the customer a notice that their request was received. Send <code>false</code> if you prefer to notify them yourself.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Usa <code>observations_client</code> para lo que el venue necesita saber para decidir.</span><span class="l-en">Use <code>observations_client</code> for what the venue needs to know to decide.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Guarda el <code>_id</code> de la reserva que devuelve la respuesta.</span><span class="l-en">Store the booking <code>_id</code> returned in the response.</span></li></ul><h3 id="flujo-7-s3" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 2. Consulta el estado</span><span class="l-en">Step 2. Check the status</span></h3><div class="note info"><span class="note-i" aria-hidden="true">i</span><p><span class="l-es">No hay webhook para los cambios de estado de una reserva. Consulta <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/bookings/{bookingId}</code> periódicamente mientras la reserva esté pendiente.</span><span class="l-en">There is no webhook for booking status changes. Query <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/bookings/{bookingId}</code> periodically while the booking is pending.</span></p></div><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Estado</span><span class="l-en">Status</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué haces</span><span class="l-en">What you do</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>pending</code>, <code>to-review</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Sigue esperando. Indica al comprador que su solicitud está en revisión.</span><span class="l-en">Keep waiting. Tell the buyer their request is under review.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>accepted</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Confirma la reserva al comprador.</span><span class="l-en">Confirm the booking to the buyer.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>rejected</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Informa al comprador y ofrécele otras opciones.</span><span class="l-en">Inform the buyer and offer other options.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="check" data-flow="7"><div class="check-h"><span class="l-es">Checklist</span><span class="l-en">Checklist</span><span class="check-n" data-count="7"></span></div><ul><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="7-0"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Explicas al comprador que la reserva no está confirmada hasta que el venue la acepte.</span><span class="l-en">You explain to the buyer that the booking isn't confirmed until the venue accepts it.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="7-1"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Guardas el <code>_id</code> de la reserva.</span><span class="l-en">You store the booking <code>_id</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="7-2"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Consultas el estado periódicamente y dejas de hacerlo cuando la reserva se acepta o se rechaza.</span><span class="l-en">You check the status periodically and stop once the booking is accepted or rejected.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="7-3"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Decides quién avisa al cliente de la solicitud (<code>send_resources</code>).</span><span class="l-en">You decide who notifies the customer about the request (<code>send_resources</code>).</span></label></li></ul></div></div></article></div></div><div class="panel has-side" role="tabpanel" id="tab-listas" data-tab="listas" hidden><aside class="side"><nav class="side-nav"><a href="#flujo-8"><span class="num">08</span><span class="l-es">Listas de invitados</span><span class="l-en">Guest lists</span></a><a href="#flujo-9"><span class="num">09</span><span class="l-es">Preregistro</span><span class="l-en">Pre-registration</span></a></nav></aside><div class="panel-main"><div class="tab-head"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Listas y preregistro</span><span class="l-en">Lists and pre-registration</span></div><h2 class="tab-h"><span class="l-es">Listas de invitados y preregistro</span><span class="l-en">Guest lists and pre-registration</span></h2></div><article class="card flow" id="flujo-8" data-flow="8"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow</span> <b>08</b></div><h2><span class="l-es">Listas de invitados</span><span class="l-en">Guest lists</span></h2><div class="flow-body"><p><span class="l-es">Apuntas a una persona (y a sus acompañantes) en una lista del evento con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/lists</code>. No hay pago en la API: la lista da acceso en las condiciones que haya configurado el venue. Cada alta genera un QR con el que entra el grupo.</span><span class="l-en">You add a person (and their companions) to an event list with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/lists</code>. There is no payment in the API: the list grants entry on the conditions the venue has set. Each sign-up generates a QR the group enters with.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-8-s1" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Resumen del flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow summary</span></h3><ol><li><span class="l-es">Lees las tarifas de lista del evento.</span><span class="l-en">You read the event's list rates.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Compruebas que la tarifa está disponible y qué datos pide.</span><span class="l-en">You check the rate is available and what data it requires.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Das de alta la lista con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/lists</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You create the list entry with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/lists</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Entregas el QR, o lo envía Fourvenues.</span><span class="l-en">You deliver the QR, or Fourvenues sends it.</span></li></ol><h3 id="flujo-8-s2" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 1. Lee las tarifas de lista</span><span class="l-en">Step 1. Read the list rates</span></h3><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">cURL</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> GET \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/list-rates?event_id=ev_abc123'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span></code></pre></div><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué es</span><span class="l-en">What it is</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>_id</code>, <code>name</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Identificador y nombre de la tarifa de lista.</span><span class="l-en">List rate identifier and name.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>available</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si se pueden dar altas ahora. Si es <code>false</code>, no la ofrezcas.</span><span class="l-en">Whether sign-ups are possible now. If <code>false</code>, don't offer it.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>valid_from</code>, <code>valid_until</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Periodo en el que se pueden dar altas.</span><span class="l-en">Period in which sign-ups are allowed.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>availability.available</code>, <code>availability.sold</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Plazas disponibles y altas hechas por tu canal.</span><span class="l-en">Places available and sign-ups made by your channel.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>max</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Máximo de altas por pedido.</span><span class="l-en">Maximum sign-ups per order.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>prices</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Condiciones de la lista por franja.</span><span class="l-en">List conditions per time slot.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">Cada elemento de <code>prices</code> describe una franja de la lista:</span><span class="l-en">Each item in <code>prices</code> describes a list time slot:</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué es</span><span class="l-en">What it is</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>price</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Precio de entrada con la lista en esa franja. Se abona en el venue al entrar, no a través de la API.</span><span class="l-en">Entry price with the list in that slot. Paid at the venue on entry, not through the API.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>valid_from</code>, <code>valid_until</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Periodo de la franja.</span><span class="l-en">Slot period.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>from</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Hora a partir de la que se puede entrar con la lista.</span><span class="l-en">Time from which entry with the list is allowed.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>men</code>, <code>women</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si la franja admite hombres, mujeres o ambos.</span><span class="l-en">Whether the slot admits men, women or both.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>age</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Edad mínima.</span><span class="l-en">Minimum age.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>additional_info</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Condiciones de la lista. Muéstralas siempre al usuario.</span><span class="l-en">List conditions. Always show them to the user.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">Como en el resto de tarifas, las listas pueden cambiar o cerrarse en cualquier momento. Vuelve a leer la tarifa justo antes de dar el alta.</span><span class="l-en">As with other rates, lists can change or close at any time. Read the rate again right before the sign-up.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-8-s3" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 2. Da de alta la lista</span><span class="l-en">Step 2. Create the list entry</span></h3><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">cURL</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> POST \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/lists'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'Content-Type: application/json'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--data</span> '{
    <span class="t-k">"list_rate_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"lr_888"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"send_notification"</span>: <span class="t-kw">true</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"list"</span>: {
      <span class="t-k">"full_name"</span>: <span class="t-s">"Ana García"</span>,
      <span class="t-k">"email"</span>: <span class="t-s">"ana@example.com"</span>,
      <span class="t-k">"for"</span>: <span class="t-n">3</span>,
      <span class="t-k">"language"</span>: <span class="t-s">"es"</span>
    }
  }'</code></pre></div><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Obligatorio</span><span class="l-en">Required</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué es</span><span class="l-en">What it is</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>list_rate_id</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Sí</span><span class="l-en">Yes</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Tarifa de lista.</span><span class="l-en">List rate.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>list.for</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Recomendado</span><span class="l-en">Recommended</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Número de personas que entran con esta lista.</span><span class="l-en">Number of people entering with this list.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>list.full_name</code>, <code>list.email</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Según la tarifa</span><span class="l-en">Depends on the rate</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Titular de la lista. Si la tarifa los exige y faltan, la llamada devuelve <code>422</code>.</span><span class="l-en">List holder. If the rate requires them and they're missing, the call returns <code>422</code>.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>list.language</code></td><td><span class="l-es">No</span><span class="l-en">No</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Idioma de la notificación: <code>es</code> (por defecto), <code>en</code>, <code>ca</code>, <code>fr</code>, <code>nl</code> o <code>it</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Notification language: <code>es</code> (default), <code>en</code>, <code>ca</code>, <code>fr</code>, <code>nl</code> or <code>it</code>.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>list.observations_referral</code></td><td><span class="l-es">No</span><span class="l-en">No</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Notas de tu canal para el venue.</span><span class="l-en">Notes from your channel for the venue.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>list.qr_code</code></td><td><span class="l-es">No</span><span class="l-en">No</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Si no lo envías, Fourvenues asigna uno (lo habitual). Si lo envías, cada lista debe tener un código distinto.</span><span class="l-en">If you don't send it, Fourvenues assigns one (the usual case). If you send it, each list must have a different code.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>send_notification</code></td><td><span class="l-es">No</span><span class="l-en">No</span></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>true</code> para que Fourvenues envíe el QR al email del titular. Por defecto es <code>false</code>.</span><span class="l-en"><code>true</code> for Fourvenues to send the QR to the holder's email. Default is <code>false</code>.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><h3 id="flujo-8-s4" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 3. Guarda la respuesta y entrega el QR</span><span class="l-en">Step 3. Store the response and deliver the QR</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">La respuesta devuelve la lista creada con su <code>_id</code>, <code>qr_code</code>, <code>for</code> (personas apuntadas) y <code>enter</code> (personas que ya han entrado). Guarda <code>_id</code> y <code>qr_code</code> junto a tu pedido.</span><span class="l-en">The response returns the created list with its <code>_id</code>, <code>qr_code</code>, <code>for</code> (people signed up) and <code>enter</code> (people who have already entered). Store <code>_id</code> and <code>qr_code</code> with your order.</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">send_notification</span><span class="l-en">send_notification</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Quién entrega el QR</span><span class="l-en">Who delivers the QR</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>true</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Fourvenues lo envía por email al titular, en el idioma de <code>language</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Fourvenues emails it to the holder, in the <code>language</code> set.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>false</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Tú. Muestra o envía el <code>qr_code</code> desde tu plataforma.</span><span class="l-en">You. Display or send the <code>qr_code</code> from your platform.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">Para consultar tus listas más tarde, usa <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/lists?event_id=...</code> (o filtra por <code>list_rate_id</code>), <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/lists/{id}</code> o <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/lists/by-code/{code}</code>.</span><span class="l-en">To look up your lists later, use <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/lists?event_id=...</code> (or filter by <code>list_rate_id</code>), <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/lists/{id}</code> or <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/lists/by-code/{code}</code>.</span></p><div class="check" data-flow="8"><div class="check-h"><span class="l-es">Checklist</span><span class="l-en">Checklist</span><span class="check-n" data-count="8"></span></div><ul><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="8-0"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Solo ofreces tarifas de lista con <code>available: true</code> y dentro de su periodo.</span><span class="l-en">You only offer list rates with <code>available: true</code> and within their period.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="8-1"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Muestras las condiciones (<code>additional_info</code>, <code>from</code>, <code>age</code>) antes del alta.</span><span class="l-en">You show the conditions (<code>additional_info</code>, <code>from</code>, <code>age</code>) before sign-up.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="8-2"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Envías los datos del titular que exige la tarifa.</span><span class="l-en">You send the holder data the rate requires.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="8-3"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Guardas <code>_id</code> y <code>qr_code</code> de cada lista.</span><span class="l-en">You store each list's <code>_id</code> and <code>qr_code</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="8-4"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Tienes decidido quién entrega el QR (<code>send_notification</code>).</span><span class="l-en">You have decided who delivers the QR (<code>send_notification</code>).</span></label></li></ul></div></div></article><article class="card flow" id="flujo-9" data-flow="9"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow</span> <b>09</b></div><h2><span class="l-es">Preregistro</span><span class="l-en">Pre-registration</span></h2><div class="flow-body"><div class="note warn"><span class="note-i" aria-hidden="true">!</span><p><span class="l-es"><strong>Flujo exclusivo para eventos propios.</strong> Solo puedes preregistrar usuarios en eventos de tu propia organización. No está disponible para los eventos de los venues con los que colaboras como canal.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Own events only.</strong> You can only pre-register users for events of your own organization. It is not available for events of venues you partner with as a channel.</span></p></div><p><span class="l-es">Registras a un interesado en el preregistro de un evento con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/events/{id}/preregister</code>. Se usa antes de que salgan las entradas a la venta, para captar público y avisarle cuando abra la venta. No crea entradas ni tiene pago.</span><span class="l-en">You register someone interested in an event's pre-registration with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/events/{id}/preregister</code>. It is used before tickets go on sale, to capture an audience and notify them when sales open. It creates no tickets and has no payment.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-9-s1" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Resumen del flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow summary</span></h3><ol><li><span class="l-es">Compruebas que el evento tiene el preregistro activo y abierto.</span><span class="l-en">You check the event has pre-registration active and open.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Muestras el formulario con los campos que pide el evento.</span><span class="l-en">You show the form with the fields the event requires.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Registras al usuario con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/events/{id}/preregister</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You register the user with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/events/{id}/preregister</code>.</span></li></ol><h3 id="flujo-9-s2" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Requisitos</span><span class="l-en">Requirements</span></h3><ul><li><span class="l-es">Tu key necesita la capability <code>preregister:write</code> (o <code>all:write</code>). Sin ella la llamada devuelve <code>403</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Your key needs the <code>preregister:write</code> capability (or <code>all:write</code>). Without it the call returns <code>403</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">El evento tiene que tener el preregistro activo, seguir a la venta y estar dentro del periodo de preregistro.</span><span class="l-en">The event must have pre-registration active, still be on sale and be within the pre-registration period.</span></li></ul><h3 id="flujo-9-s3" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 1. Lee la configuración del preregistro</span><span class="l-en">Step 1. Read the pre-registration settings</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">La configuración viene por defecto en <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/events</code> y <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/events/{id}</code>, en el campo <code>preregister</code>. Es <code>null</code> si el evento no tiene preregistro.</span><span class="l-en">The settings come by default in <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/events</code> and <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/events/{id}</code>, in the <code>preregister</code> field. It is <code>null</code> if the event has no pre-registration.</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué es</span><span class="l-en">What it is</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>is_active</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si el preregistro está activo. Si es <code>false</code>, no muestres el formulario.</span><span class="l-en">Whether pre-registration is active. If <code>false</code>, don't show the form.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>end_date</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Cierre del preregistro, como timestamp Unix en segundos. <code>null</code> si no tiene fecha de cierre.</span><span class="l-en">Pre-registration close, as a Unix timestamp in seconds. <code>null</code> if there is no close date.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>fields</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Campos del formulario. Cada uno trae <code>key</code>, <code>is_required</code> y, en los desplegables, <code>options</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Form fields. Each includes <code>key</code>, <code>is_required</code> and, for dropdowns, <code>options</code>.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><h3 id="flujo-9-s4" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 2. Construye el formulario</span><span class="l-en">Step 2. Build the form</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Pide siempre nombre completo y email. Añade los campos de <code>fields</code> y marca como obligatorios los que tengan <code>is_required: true</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Always ask for full name and email. Add the fields from <code>fields</code> and mark as required those with <code>is_required: true</code>.</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo de la llamada</span><span class="l-en">Call field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué es</span><span class="l-en">What it is</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>fullname</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Nombre completo. Siempre obligatorio.</span><span class="l-en">Full name. Always required.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>email</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Email. Siempre obligatorio.</span><span class="l-en">Email. Always required.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>phone</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Teléfono.</span><span class="l-en">Phone.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>birthdate</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Fecha de nacimiento (<code>YYYY-MM-DD</code>).</span><span class="l-en">Date of birth (<code>YYYY-MM-DD</code>).</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>gender</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Género: <code>0</code> (mujer), <code>1</code> (hombre) o <code>2</code> (otro).</span><span class="l-en">Gender: <code>0</code> (female), <code>1</code> (male) or <code>2</code> (other).</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>country</code>, <code>state</code>, <code>zipCode</code></td><td><span class="l-es">País, provincia y código postal.</span><span class="l-en">Country, state/province and postcode.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>personalDocumentType</code>, <code>personalDocument</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Tipo (<code>dni</code>, <code>nie</code>, <code>passport</code>, <code>other</code>) y número de documento.</span><span class="l-en">Type (<code>dni</code>, <code>nie</code>, <code>passport</code>, <code>other</code>) and document number.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>remarketing</code>, <code>remarketing_sms</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si acepta comunicaciones comerciales por email o SMS. Por defecto <code>false</code>. Pídelo con una casilla sin marcar.</span><span class="l-en">Whether they accept marketing by email or SMS. Default <code>false</code>. Ask with an unchecked box.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><h3 id="flujo-9-s5" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 3. Registra al usuario</span><span class="l-en">Step 3. Register the user</span></h3><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">cURL</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> POST \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/events/ev_abc123/preregister'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'Content-Type: application/json'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--data</span> '{
    <span class="t-k">"fullname"</span>: <span class="t-s">"Ana García"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"email"</span>: <span class="t-s">"ana@example.com"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"phone"</span>: <span class="t-s">"+34600000000"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"remarketing"</span>: <span class="t-kw">true</span>
  }'</code></pre></div><p><span class="l-es">La respuesta (<code>201</code>) devuelve el <code>_id</code> del registro. Guárdalo junto a tu usuario.</span><span class="l-en">The response (<code>201</code>) returns the registration <code>_id</code>. Store it with your user.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-9-s6" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Errores habituales</span><span class="l-en">Common errors</span></h3><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Error</span><span class="l-en">Error</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Causa</span><span class="l-en">Cause</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué hacer</span><span class="l-en">What to do</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Falta un campo obligatorio, un valor no es válido, o el preregistro está cerrado o inactivo</span><span class="l-en">A required field is missing, a value is invalid, or pre-registration is closed or inactive</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Revisa <code>fields</code>, <code>is_active</code> y <code>end_date</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Check <code>fields</code>, <code>is_active</code> and <code>end_date</code>.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>403</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Tu key no tiene <code>preregister:write</code></span><span class="l-en">Your key lacks <code>preregister:write</code></span></td><td><span class="l-es">Pide la capability.</span><span class="l-en">Request the capability.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span class="l-es">El evento no existe o no es visible para tu canal</span><span class="l-en">The event doesn't exist or isn't visible to your channel</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Revisa el id del evento.</span><span class="l-en">Check the event id.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>429</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Demasiadas peticiones</span><span class="l-en">Too many requests</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Backoff exponencial.</span><span class="l-en">Exponential backoff.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="check" data-flow="9"><div class="check-h"><span class="l-es">Checklist</span><span class="l-en">Checklist</span><span class="check-n" data-count="9"></span></div><ul><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="9-0"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Solo muestras el formulario si <code>preregister</code> no es <code>null</code>, <code>is_active</code> es <code>true</code> y no ha pasado <code>end_date</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You only show the form if <code>preregister</code> isn't <code>null</code>, <code>is_active</code> is <code>true</code> and <code>end_date</code> hasn't passed.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="9-1"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Pides los campos de <code>fields</code> y respetas <code>is_required</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You ask for the fields in <code>fields</code> and respect <code>is_required</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="9-2"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Pides el consentimiento de remarketing con una casilla sin marcar.</span><span class="l-en">You ask for remarketing consent with an unchecked box.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="9-3"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Tu key tiene la capability <code>preregister:write</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Your key has the <code>preregister:write</code> capability.</span></label></li></ul></div></div></article></div></div><div class="panel has-side" role="tabpanel" id="tab-extras" data-tab="extras" hidden><aside class="side"><nav class="side-nav"><a href="#flujo-10"><span class="num">10</span><span class="l-es">Complementos al comprar</span><span class="l-en">Add-ons at purchase</span></a><a href="#flujo-11"><span class="num">11</span><span class="l-es">Upselling después de la compra</span><span class="l-en">Upselling after purchase</span></a><a href="#flujo-12"><span class="num">12</span><span class="l-es">Descuentos</span><span class="l-en">Discounts</span></a><a href="#flujo-13"><span class="num">13</span><span class="l-es">Garantía de cancelación y propinas</span><span class="l-en">Cancellation guarantee and tips</span></a></nav></aside><div class="panel-main"><div class="tab-head"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Extras</span><span class="l-en">Extras</span></div><h2 class="tab-h"><span class="l-es">Complementos, upselling, descuentos, garantía y propinas</span><span class="l-en">Add-ons, upselling, discounts, guarantee and tips</span></h2></div><article class="card flow" id="flujo-10" data-flow="10"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow</span> <b>10</b></div><h2><span class="l-es">Complementos al comprar</span><span class="l-en">Add-ons at purchase</span></h2><div class="flow-body"><p><span class="l-es">Los complementos son extras que se añaden a una entrada en el momento de la compra: copas, packs de consumición, parking… Se envían por entrada en <code>tickets[].supplements</code>. El comportamiento cambia según la pasarela: en el checkout de Fourvenues los precios y los complementos automáticos los resuelve Fourvenues; con pasarela externa lo haces tú.</span><span class="l-en">Add-ons (supplements) are extras added to a ticket at purchase: drinks, drink packs, parking… They are sent per ticket in <code>tickets[].supplements</code>. Behaviour depends on the gateway: in the Fourvenues checkout, Fourvenues resolves prices and automatic add-ons; with an external gateway you do.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-10-s1" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 1. Lee los complementos de la tarifa</span><span class="l-en">Step 1. Read the rate's add-ons</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Vienen en <code>supplements</code> de cada tarifa de entrada (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-2">flujo 2</a>).</span><span class="l-en">They come in each ticket rate's <code>supplements</code> (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-2">flow 2</a>).</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué es</span><span class="l-en">What it is</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>_id</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Identificador. Envíalo como <code>supplement_id</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Identifier. Send it as <code>supplement_id</code>.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>label</code>, <code>description</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Nombre y descripción para mostrar al comprador.</span><span class="l-en">Name and description to show the buyer.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>price</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Precio por pack. Si es <code>0</code>, el complemento es gratuito.</span><span class="l-en">Price per pack. If <code>0</code>, the add-on is free.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>product_quantity</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Unidades que incluye cada pack (p. ej. 4 copas por pack).</span><span class="l-en">Units included in each pack (e.g. 4 drinks per pack).</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>has_fake_price</code>, <code>fake_price</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si <code>has_fake_price</code> es <code>true</code>, muestra <code>fake_price</code> tachado como precio original.</span><span class="l-en">If <code>has_fake_price</code> is <code>true</code>, show <code>fake_price</code> struck through as the original price.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>purchase_limit</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Límites por entrada: <code>min_quantity</code>, <code>max_quantity</code> y <code>is_unlimited</code> (si es <code>true</code>, no hay máximo).</span><span class="l-en">Per-ticket limits: <code>min_quantity</code>, <code>max_quantity</code> and <code>is_unlimited</code> (if <code>true</code>, no maximum).</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>redemption_deadline</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si <code>is_expiration_enabled</code> es <code>true</code>, <code>expiration_deadline</code> (timestamp Unix en segundos) es la fecha límite del complemento. Muéstrala al comprador.</span><span class="l-en">If <code>is_expiration_enabled</code> is <code>true</code>, <code>expiration_deadline</code> (Unix timestamp in seconds) is the add-on deadline. Show it to the buyer.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><h3 id="flujo-10-s2" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 2. Calcula el importe</span><span class="l-en">Step 2. Calculate the amount</span></h3><ul><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Importe del complemento</strong> = <code>price</code> × cantidad de packs.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Add-on amount</strong> = <code>price</code> × number of packs.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Gastos de gestión:</strong> los complementos usan los gastos de la tarifa de entrada. Si son porcentaje (<code>fee_type: percentage</code>), se aplica el mismo porcentaje al importe del complemento. Si son fijos (<code>fixed</code>), no se suma nada más: el importe fijo se cobra una sola vez por entrada.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Service fee:</strong> add-ons use the ticket rate's fee. If it is a percentage (<code>fee_type: percentage</code>), the same percentage applies to the add-on amount. If it is fixed (<code>fixed</code>), nothing more is added: the fixed amount is charged only once per ticket.</span></li></ul><p><span class="l-es">El importe de los complementos y sus gastos se suman al de la entrada, después del descuento y de los gastos de la entrada (orden de cálculo en el <a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flujo 3</a>).</span><span class="l-en">The add-on amount and its fees are added to the ticket's, after the discount and the ticket's fee (calculation order in <a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flow 3</a>).</span></p><h3 id="flujo-10-s3" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 3. Envíalos en la compra</span><span class="l-en">Step 3. Send them with the purchase</span></h3><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es"></span><span class="l-en"></span></th><th><span class="l-es">Checkout de Fourvenues (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flujo 4</a>)</span><span class="l-en">Fourvenues checkout (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flow 4</a>)</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Pasarela externa (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-5">flujo 5</a>)</span><span class="l-en">External gateway (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-5">flow 5</a>)</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="l-es">Qué envías</span><span class="l-en">What you send</span></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>supplement_id</code> y <code>quantity</code> (por defecto 1)</span><span class="l-en"><code>supplement_id</code> and <code>quantity</code> (default 1)</span></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>supplement_id</code>, <code>price</code> (precio unitario cobrado) y <code>quantity</code></span><span class="l-en"><code>supplement_id</code>, <code>price</code> (unit price charged) and <code>quantity</code></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Precios y gastos</span><span class="l-en">Prices and fees</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Los toma Fourvenues de la tarifa</span><span class="l-en">Fourvenues takes them from the rate</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Envías tú el precio unitario cobrado. Los gastos siguen las reglas de la tarifa (paso 2).</span><span class="l-en">You send the unit price charged. Fees follow the rate's rules (step 2).</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Complementos con mínimo</span><span class="l-en">Add-ons with a minimum</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Si no los envías, se añaden solos con la cantidad mínima. Si envías menos, se sube al mínimo.</span><span class="l-en">If you don't send them, they are added automatically at the minimum quantity. If you send fewer, it is raised to the minimum.</span></td><td><span class="l-es">No se añaden solos. Envíalos tú con al menos la cantidad mínima.</span><span class="l-en">Not added automatically. Send them with at least the minimum quantity.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Complementos gratuitos</span><span class="l-en">Free add-ons</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Se añaden solos, un pack por entrada.</span><span class="l-en">Added automatically, one pack per ticket.</span></td><td><span class="l-es">No se añaden solos. Envíalos tú.</span><span class="l-en">Not added automatically. Send them yourself.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Máximo</span><span class="l-en">Maximum</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Se valida. Si lo superas, <code>400</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Validated. If exceeded, <code>400</code>.</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Se valida. Si lo superas, <code>400</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Validated. If exceeded, <code>400</code>.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">En los dos casos:</span><span class="l-en">In both cases:</span></p><ul><li><span class="l-es">Si envías el mismo <code>supplement_id</code> varias veces en una entrada, se juntan en una línea y se suman las cantidades antes de validar los límites.</span><span class="l-en">If you send the same <code>supplement_id</code> several times in a ticket, they are merged into one line and quantities are added before validating limits.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Solo se permite un tipo de complemento gratuito por entrada.</span><span class="l-en">Only one type of free add-on is allowed per ticket.</span></li></ul><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">JSON</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-k">"tickets"</span>: [
  {
    <span class="t-k">"price_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"price_early"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"full_name"</span>: <span class="t-s">"Ana García"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"email"</span>: <span class="t-s">"ana@example.com"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"supplements"</span>: [
      { <span class="t-k">"supplement_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"sup_1"</span>, <span class="t-k">"quantity"</span>: <span class="t-n">2</span> }
    ]
  }
]</code></pre></div><h3 id="flujo-10-s4" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 4. Comprueba los complementos de la entrada</span><span class="l-en">Step 4. Check the ticket's add-ons</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Cada entrada devuelve sus complementos en <code>supplements</code>, con <code>supplement_units</code> (packs comprados), <code>product_quantity</code> (unidades por pack) y <code>total_price</code> de la línea. En la entrada, <code>total_supplements</code> y <code>supplements_service_charge_total</code> dan el total de complementos y de sus gastos.</span><span class="l-en">Each ticket returns its add-ons in <code>supplements</code>, with <code>supplement_units</code> (packs bought), <code>product_quantity</code> (units per pack) and the line's <code>total_price</code>. On the ticket, <code>total_supplements</code> and <code>supplements_service_charge_total</code> give the add-on total and their fees.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-10-s5" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Errores habituales</span><span class="l-en">Common errors</span></h3><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Error</span><span class="l-en">Error</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Causa</span><span class="l-en">Cause</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué hacer</span><span class="l-en">What to do</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>400 Supplement not found</code></td><td><span class="l-es">El <code>supplement_id</code> no pertenece a la tarifa</span><span class="l-en">The <code>supplement_id</code> doesn't belong to the rate</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Vuelve a leer la tarifa.</span><span class="l-en">Read the rate again.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es"><code>400</code> cantidad fuera de límites</span><span class="l-en"><code>400</code> quantity out of bounds</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Menos del mínimo o más del máximo, después de juntar duplicados</span><span class="l-en">Below the minimum or above the maximum, after merging duplicates</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Ajusta <code>quantity</code> a <code>purchase_limit</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Adjust <code>quantity</code> to <code>purchase_limit</code>.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es"><code>400</code> más de un gratuito</span><span class="l-en"><code>400</code> more than one free add-on</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Has enviado dos tipos de complemento gratuito en una entrada</span><span class="l-en">You sent two types of free add-on in one ticket</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Envía solo uno.</span><span class="l-en">Send only one.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="check" data-flow="10"><div class="check-h"><span class="l-es">Checklist</span><span class="l-en">Checklist</span><span class="check-n" data-count="10"></span></div><ul><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="10-0"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Muestras <code>label</code>, precio, <code>product_quantity</code> y, si aplica, el precio tachado y la fecha límite.</span><span class="l-en">You show <code>label</code>, price, <code>product_quantity</code> and, where applicable, the struck-through price and deadline.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="10-1"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Respetas <code>min_quantity</code> y <code>max_quantity</code> en tu interfaz.</span><span class="l-en">You respect <code>min_quantity</code> and <code>max_quantity</code> in your UI.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="10-2"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Con pasarela externa, envías tú los complementos gratuitos y los mínimos, y el precio que has cobrado.</span><span class="l-en">With an external gateway, you send free add-ons, minimums and the price you charged.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="10-3"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Sumas complementos y sus gastos al total que muestras.</span><span class="l-en">You add add-ons and their fees to the total you display.</span></label></li></ul></div></div></article><article class="card flow" id="flujo-11" data-flow="11"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow</span> <b>11</b></div><h2><span class="l-es">Upselling después de la compra</span><span class="l-en">Upselling after purchase</span></h2><div class="flow-body"><p><span class="l-es">El comprador añade complementos a una entrada que ya tiene. Consultas qué complementos puede comprar con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets/{id}/upselling</code> y creas una sesión de pago con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets/{id}/upselling/checkout</code>. El cobro lo hace siempre la pasarela de Fourvenues.</span><span class="l-en">The buyer adds add-ons to a ticket they already have. You check which add-ons they can buy with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets/{id}/upselling</code> and create a payment session with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets/{id}/upselling/checkout</code>. Payment is always handled by the Fourvenues gateway.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-11-s1" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Resumen del flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow summary</span></h3><ol><li><span class="l-es">Compruebas que el evento permite upselling.</span><span class="l-en">You check the event allows upselling.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Consultas el catálogo de complementos disponibles para esa entrada.</span><span class="l-en">You query the catalog of add-ons available for that ticket.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Creas la sesión de pago con los complementos elegidos.</span><span class="l-en">You create the payment session with the chosen add-ons.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Rediriges al comprador a la <code>payment_url</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You redirect the buyer to the <code>payment_url</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Confirmas con el webhook <code>payment.success</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You confirm with the <code>payment.success</code> webhook.</span></li></ol><h3 id="flujo-11-s2" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 1. Comprueba que el evento lo permite</span><span class="l-en">Step 1. Check the event allows it</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">El evento tiene que tener <code>is_upselling_enabled: true</code> (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-2">flujo 2</a>). Si es <code>false</code>, no ofrezcas añadir complementos después de la compra.</span><span class="l-en">The event must have <code>is_upselling_enabled: true</code> (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-2">flow 2</a>). If <code>false</code>, don't offer adding add-ons after purchase.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-11-s3" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 2. Consulta el catálogo</span><span class="l-en">Step 2. Query the catalog</span></h3><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">cURL</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> GET \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/tickets/tk_1/upselling'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span></code></pre></div><p><span class="l-es">Devuelve los complementos que se pueden comprar para esa entrada, con los mismos campos que en la tarifa (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-10">flujo 10</a>) y dos más:</span><span class="l-en">Returns the add-ons that can be bought for that ticket, with the same fields as the rate (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-10">flow 10</a>) plus two more:</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué es</span><span class="l-en">What it is</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>current_purchase_quantity</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Cantidad que la entrada ya tiene de ese complemento.</span><span class="l-en">Quantity of that add-on the ticket already has.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>available_quantity</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Cantidad que todavía se puede añadir, teniendo en cuenta el máximo.</span><span class="l-en">Quantity that can still be added, considering the maximum.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">También devuelve los gastos de gestión de la operación (<code>fee_type</code>, <code>fee_quantity</code>), que se aplican al checkout de upselling.</span><span class="l-en">It also returns the operation's service fees (<code>fee_type</code>, <code>fee_quantity</code>), which apply to the upselling checkout.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-11-s4" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 3. Crea la sesión de pago</span><span class="l-en">Step 3. Create the payment session</span></h3><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">cURL</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> POST \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/tickets/tk_1/upselling/checkout'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'Content-Type: application/json'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--data</span> '{
    <span class="t-k">"redirect_url"</span>: <span class="t-s">"https://tuapp.com/pedido/987/extras/ok"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"error_url"</span>: <span class="t-s">"https://tuapp.com/pedido/987/extras/error"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"send_resources"</span>: <span class="t-kw">false</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"metadata"</span>: { <span class="t-k">"order_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"987-extras-1"</span> },
    <span class="t-k">"supplements"</span>: [ { <span class="t-k">"supplement_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"sup_1"</span>, <span class="t-k">"quantity"</span>: <span class="t-n">2</span> } ]
  }'</code></pre></div><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Obligatorio</span><span class="l-en">Required</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué es</span><span class="l-en">What it is</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>supplements</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Sí</span><span class="l-en">Yes</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Complementos a añadir, cada uno con <code>supplement_id</code> y <code>quantity</code> (mínimo 1).</span><span class="l-en">Add-ons to add, each with <code>supplement_id</code> and <code>quantity</code> (minimum 1).</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>redirect_url</code>, <code>error_url</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Sí</span><span class="l-en">Yes</span></td><td><span class="l-es">A dónde vuelve el comprador tras pagar o si falla.</span><span class="l-en">Where the buyer returns after paying or on failure.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>metadata</code></td><td><span class="l-es">No</span><span class="l-en">No</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Tu identificador. Vuelve en el webhook <code>payment.success</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Your identifier. It comes back in the <code>payment.success</code> webhook.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>send_resources</code></td><td><span class="l-es">No</span><span class="l-en">No</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Se reenvía en el webhook <code>payment.success</code>. Por defecto <code>false</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Passed back in the <code>payment.success</code> webhook. Default <code>false</code>.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">La respuesta trae <code>payment_id</code>, <code>payment_url</code>, el total de cada línea de complemento y los gastos. Comprueba el total y redirige al comprador a <code>payment_url</code>.</span><span class="l-en">The response includes <code>payment_id</code>, <code>payment_url</code>, each add-on line total and the fees. Check the total and redirect the buyer to <code>payment_url</code>.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-11-s5" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 4. Confirma el pago</span><span class="l-en">Step 4. Confirm the payment</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Igual que en la venta de tickets (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flujo 4</a>): no des el upselling por bueno al volver a <code>redirect_url</code>. Espera al webhook <code>payment.success</code>, localiza la operación con <code>metadata</code> o <code>payment_id</code> y vuelve a consultar la entrada (<code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets/{id}</code>) para ver sus complementos actualizados.</span><span class="l-en">Same as ticket sales (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flow 4</a>): don't consider the upsell complete on return to <code>redirect_url</code>. Wait for the <code>payment.success</code> webhook, find the operation with <code>metadata</code> or <code>payment_id</code> and query the ticket again (<code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets/{id}</code>) to see its updated add-ons.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-11-s6" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Errores habituales</span><span class="l-en">Common errors</span></h3><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Error</span><span class="l-en">Error</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Causa</span><span class="l-en">Cause</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué hacer</span><span class="l-en">What to do</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Complemento no disponible, o cantidad por encima de <code>available_quantity</code></span><span class="l-en">Add-on unavailable, or quantity above <code>available_quantity</code></span></td><td><span class="l-es">Vuelve a consultar el catálogo.</span><span class="l-en">Query the catalog again.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span class="l-es">La entrada no existe o no es visible para tu canal</span><span class="l-en">The ticket doesn't exist or isn't visible to your channel</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Revisa el id de la entrada.</span><span class="l-en">Check the ticket id.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="check" data-flow="11"><div class="check-h"><span class="l-es">Checklist</span><span class="l-en">Checklist</span><span class="check-n" data-count="11"></span></div><ul><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="11-0"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Solo ofreces upselling si el evento tiene <code>is_upselling_enabled: true</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You only offer upselling if the event has <code>is_upselling_enabled: true</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="11-1"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Consultas el catálogo justo antes de crear la sesión y respetas <code>available_quantity</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You query the catalog right before creating the session and respect <code>available_quantity</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="11-2"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Envías un <code>metadata</code> distinto por cada operación de upselling.</span><span class="l-en">You send a different <code>metadata</code> for each upselling operation.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="11-3"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Confirmas con <code>payment.success</code> y refrescas la entrada.</span><span class="l-en">You confirm with <code>payment.success</code> and refresh the ticket.</span></label></li></ul></div></div></article><article class="card flow" id="flujo-12" data-flow="12"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow</span> <b>12</b></div><h2><span class="l-es">Descuentos</span><span class="l-en">Discounts</span></h2><div class="flow-body"><p><span class="l-es">El comprador introduce un código, lo validas con Fourvenues para esa tarifa y esa cantidad, y envías el código en la compra como <code>discount_code</code>. Los códigos los crea el venue. El canal solo los valida y los aplica.</span><span class="l-en">The buyer enters a code, you validate it with Fourvenues for that rate and quantity, and you send the code with the purchase as <code>discount_code</code>. Codes are created by the venue. The channel only validates and applies them.</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es"></span><span class="l-en"></span></th><th><span class="l-es">Tickets</span><span class="l-en">Tickets</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Reservas</span><span class="l-en">Bookings</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="l-es">Endpoint</span><span class="l-en">Endpoint</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/discount-codes/tickets/validate/{code}</code></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/discount-codes/bookings/validate/{code}</code></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Parámetros</span><span class="l-en">Parameters</span></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>rate_id</code> (tarifa de entrada), <code>quantity</code> (nº de entradas), <code>supplements_total_price</code> (importe de los complementos elegidos)</span><span class="l-en"><code>rate_id</code> (ticket rate), <code>quantity</code> (number of tickets), <code>supplements_total_price</code> (amount of chosen add-ons)</span></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>event_id</code>, <code>rate_id</code> (tarifa de mesa), <code>quantity</code> (nº de personas)</span><span class="l-en"><code>event_id</code>, <code>rate_id</code> (table rate), <code>quantity</code> (number of people)</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><h3 id="flujo-12-s1" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 1. Muestra el campo de código solo si aplica</span><span class="l-en">Step 1. Show the code field only when it applies</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">En tickets, la tarifa trae <code>has_discount_codes_enabled</code>. Si es <code>false</code>, no muestres el campo de código de descuento.</span><span class="l-en">For tickets, the rate includes <code>has_discount_codes_enabled</code>. If <code>false</code>, don't show the discount code field.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-12-s2" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 2. Valida el código</span><span class="l-en">Step 2. Validate the code</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Valida el código cada vez que cambie la tarifa, la cantidad o los complementos elegidos. Los parámetros van en la URL.</span><span class="l-en">Validate the code every time the rate, quantity or chosen add-ons change. Parameters go in the URL.</span></p><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">cURL</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> POST \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/discount-codes/tickets/validate/VERANO10?rate_id=tr_111&amp;quantity=2&amp;supplements_total_price=10'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span></code></pre></div><p><span class="l-es">Si el código es válido, la respuesta trae sus condiciones:</span><span class="l-en">If the code is valid, the response includes its conditions:</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué es</span><span class="l-en">What it is</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>code</code></td><td><span class="l-es">El código.</span><span class="l-en">The code.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>fixedAmount</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Descuento fijo por compra. Se reparte entre las entradas (ver abajo).</span><span class="l-en">Fixed discount per purchase. Split across tickets (see below).</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>percentage</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Descuento en porcentaje sobre el precio del tramo de cada entrada.</span><span class="l-en">Percentage discount on each ticket's tier price.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>minAmount</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Importe mínimo de compra para que aplique.</span><span class="l-en">Minimum purchase amount for it to apply.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>minUnits</code>, <code>maxUnits</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Unidades mínimas y máximas a las que se aplica.</span><span class="l-en">Minimum and maximum units it applies to.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>maxRedeems</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Veces que un mismo usuario puede usarlo.</span><span class="l-en">Times a single user can use it.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>maxSales</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Usos totales permitidos entre todos los usuarios.</span><span class="l-en">Total uses allowed across all users.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">Si no es válido, la llamada devuelve <code>400</code> o <code>404</code>. Muestra al comprador que el código no es válido y deja que siga sin descuento.</span><span class="l-en">If it isn't valid, the call returns <code>400</code> or <code>404</code>. Tell the buyer the code is invalid and let them continue without a discount.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-12-s3" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Cómo se aplica el descuento</span><span class="l-en">How the discount is applied</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">El descuento es el primer paso del cálculo (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flujo 3</a>): los gastos de gestión y la garantía se calculan sobre el precio ya descontado.</span><span class="l-en">The discount is the first step of the calculation (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flow 3</a>): service fees and the warranty are calculated on the discounted price.</span></p><ul><li><span class="l-es"><strong>En porcentaje:</strong> se aplica al precio del tramo de cada entrada. No se aplica a los complementos.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Percentage:</strong> applied to each ticket's tier price. It does not apply to add-ons.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Fijo:</strong> se aplica una sola vez por compra y se reparte entre las entradas en proporción a su precio. Por ejemplo, con dos entradas de 20,00 € y 30,00 € y un descuento de 10,00 €, la primera recibe el 40 % (4,00 €) y queda en 16,00 €, y la segunda el 60 % (6,00 €) y queda en 24,00 €.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Fixed:</strong> applied only once per purchase and split across tickets in proportion to their price. For example, with two tickets of €20.00 and €30.00 and a €10.00 discount, the first gets 40% (€4.00) and ends at €16.00, and the second gets 60% (€6.00) and ends at €24.00.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Céntimos sobrantes:</strong> si al repartir un descuento fijo sobran o faltan céntimos, se asignan a la primera entrada del mismo tipo con descuento mayor que cero (o a la primera entrada, si ninguna tiene descuento).</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Leftover cents:</strong> if splitting a fixed discount leaves cents over or short, they go to the first ticket of the same type with a discount greater than zero (or to the first ticket, if none has a discount).</span></li></ul><h3 id="flujo-12-s4" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 3. Envíalo en la compra</span><span class="l-en">Step 3. Send it with the purchase</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Añade <code>discount_code</code> al checkout de tickets (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flujo 4</a>), a la creación de entradas (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-5">flujo 5</a>) o al checkout de reserva (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-6">flujo 6</a>). En tickets se aplica a todas las entradas de la compra.</span><span class="l-en">Add <code>discount_code</code> to ticket checkout (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flow 4</a>), ticket creation (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-5">flow 5</a>) or booking checkout (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-6">flow 6</a>). For tickets it applies to all tickets in the purchase.</span></p><ul><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Con la pasarela de Fourvenues:</strong> Fourvenues aplica el descuento. Comprueba que el <code>total_amount</code> de la respuesta coincide con el total con descuento que has mostrado.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>With the Fourvenues gateway:</strong> Fourvenues applies the discount. Check that the response's <code>total_amount</code> matches the discounted total you displayed.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Con pasarela externa:</strong> cobra el total con el descuento ya aplicado y envía <code>total_price</code> en consecuencia. Cada entrada devuelve el descuento aplicado en <code>discount_amount</code>.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>With an external gateway:</strong> charge the total with the discount already applied and send <code>total_price</code> accordingly. Each ticket returns the discount applied in <code>discount_amount</code>.</span></li></ul><div class="note info"><span class="note-i" aria-hidden="true">i</span><p><span class="l-es">Un código válido al validarlo puede dejar de serlo al comprar, por ejemplo si se agota <code>maxSales</code> entre medias. Trata el error de la compra igual que un código no válido.</span><span class="l-en">A code that was valid when checked can stop being valid at purchase, for example if <code>maxSales</code> runs out in the meantime. Treat the purchase error the same as an invalid code.</span></p></div><div class="check" data-flow="12"><div class="check-h"><span class="l-es">Checklist</span><span class="l-en">Checklist</span><span class="check-n" data-count="12"></span></div><ul><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="12-0"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Solo muestras el campo de código si la tarifa lo admite.</span><span class="l-en">You only show the code field if the rate accepts it.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="12-1"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Validas el código con la tarifa, la cantidad y los complementos actuales.</span><span class="l-en">You validate the code with the current rate, quantity and add-ons.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="12-2"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Vuelves a validar si el comprador cambia la cantidad o los complementos.</span><span class="l-en">You validate again if the buyer changes quantity or add-ons.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="12-3"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Compruebas que el total final coincide con el que has mostrado.</span><span class="l-en">You check that the final total matches what you displayed.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="12-4"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Si el código falla, el comprador puede seguir sin descuento.</span><span class="l-en">If the code fails, the buyer can continue without a discount.</span></label></li></ul></div></div></article><article class="card flow" id="flujo-13" data-flow="13"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow</span> <b>13</b></div><h2><span class="l-es">Garantía de cancelación y propinas</span><span class="l-en">Cancellation guarantee and tips</span></h2><div class="flow-body"><p><span class="l-es">Dos extras opcionales que elige el comprador: la garantía de cancelación en las entradas y la propina en las reservas. Los dos los configura el venue. Si no están activos, no los muestres.</span><span class="l-en">Two optional extras the buyer chooses: the cancellation guarantee on tickets and the tip on bookings. Both are configured by the venue. If they aren't active, don't show them.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-13-s1" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Garantía de cancelación (tickets)</span><span class="l-en">Cancellation guarantee (tickets)</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">La garantía permite al comprador cancelar su entrada hasta unas horas antes del evento. Viene en <code>warranty</code> de cada tarifa de entrada:</span><span class="l-en">The guarantee lets the buyer cancel their ticket up to a few hours before the event. It comes in each ticket rate's <code>warranty</code>:</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué es</span><span class="l-en">What it is</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>enabled</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si la tarifa ofrece garantía. Si es <code>false</code>, no la muestres.</span><span class="l-en">Whether the rate offers a guarantee. If <code>false</code>, don't show it.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>percentage</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Coste de la garantía en porcentaje. La base es el precio del tramo con descuento más los complementos, sin gastos de gestión.</span><span class="l-en">Warranty cost as a percentage. The base is the discounted tier price plus add-ons, without service fees.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>hours</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Horas antes del evento hasta las que se puede usar.</span><span class="l-en">Hours before the event until which it can be used.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">La garantía también lleva gastos de gestión, que siempre son un porcentaje sobre el coste de la garantía.</span><span class="l-en">The warranty also carries a service fee, which is always a percentage of the warranty cost.</span></p><p><span class="l-es">Ejemplo: entrada con precio con descuento de 18,00 €, un complemento de 5,00 €, garantía del 20 % y gastos de la entrada del 10 %. La garantía cuesta (18,00 + 5,00) × 20 % = 4,60 €, más 4,60 × 10 % = 0,46 € de gastos de gestión.</span><span class="l-en">Example: a ticket with a discounted price of €18.00, a €5.00 add-on, a 20% warranty and a 10% ticket fee.
