The warranty costs (18.00 + 5.00) × 20% = €4.60, plus 4.60 × 10% = €0.46 in service fee.</span></p><p><span class="l-es">Cómo se envía:</span><span class="l-en">How to send it:</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es"></span><span class="l-en"></span></th><th><span class="l-es">Checkout de Fourvenues (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flujo 4</a>)</span><span class="l-en">Fourvenues checkout (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flow 4</a>)</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Pasarela externa (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-5">flujo 5</a>)</span><span class="l-en">External gateway (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-5">flow 5</a>)</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>tickets[].warranty: true</code> o <code>false</code></span><span class="l-en"><code>tickets[].warranty: true</code> or <code>false</code></span></td><td><code>tickets[].warranty: { &quot;enabled&quot;: true, &quot;hours&quot;: 12 }</code></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Importe</span><span class="l-en">Amount</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Lo calcula y cobra Fourvenues</span><span class="l-en">Fourvenues calculates and charges it</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Lo calculas tú y lo incluyes en <code>total_price</code></span><span class="l-en">You calculate it and include it in <code>total_price</code></span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">Si la tarifa tiene garantía, envía siempre el campo en cada entrada, aunque el comprador no la quiera (<code>false</code> o <code>enabled: false</code>). En la entrada comprada, <code>warranty</code> devuelve <code>total</code> (lo que ha costado) y <code>hours</code>.</span><span class="l-en">If the rate has a guarantee, always send the field in each ticket, even if the buyer doesn't want it (<code>false</code> or <code>enabled: false</code>). On the purchased ticket, <code>warranty</code> returns <code>total</code> (what it cost) and <code>hours</code>.</span></p><p><span class="l-es">Muestra la garantía como una opción desmarcada, con su precio y el plazo en el que se puede usar.</span><span class="l-en">Show the guarantee as an unchecked option, with its price and the window in which it can be used.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-13-s2" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Propinas (reservas)</span><span class="l-en">Tips (bookings)</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">La propina solo existe en las reservas con pasarela de Fourvenues (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-6">flujo 6</a>).</span><span class="l-en">Tips only exist in bookings with the Fourvenues gateway (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-6">flow 6</a>).</span></p><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">cURL</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> GET \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/tip-rates?organization_id=org_xyz789'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span></code></pre></div><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué es</span><span class="l-en">What it is</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>id</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Identificador. Envíalo como <code>tip_rate_id</code> en <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/bookings/checkout</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Identifier. Send it as <code>tip_rate_id</code> in <code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/bookings/checkout</code>.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>value</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Porcentaje de la propina.</span><span class="l-en">Tip percentage.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>is_default</code></td><td><span class="l-es">La opción que va preseleccionada.</span><span class="l-en">The preselected option.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>is_active</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Si está activa. Muestra solo las activas.</span><span class="l-en">Whether it's active. Show only active ones.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">Muestra las opciones activas con la de <code>is_default</code> preseleccionada, y permite al comprador no dejar propina.</span><span class="l-en">Show the active options with the <code>is_default</code> one preselected, and let the buyer leave no tip.</span></p><div class="check" data-flow="13"><div class="check-h"><span class="l-es">Checklist</span><span class="l-en">Checklist</span><span class="check-n" data-count="13"></span></div><ul><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="13-0"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Solo muestras la garantía si <code>warranty.enabled</code> es <code>true</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You only show the guarantee if <code>warranty.enabled</code> is <code>true</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="13-1"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Envías <code>warranty</code> en cada entrada de una tarifa con garantía, en el formato de tu pasarela.</span><span class="l-en">You send <code>warranty</code> in every ticket of a rate with a guarantee, in your gateway's format.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="13-2"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Con pasarela externa, incluyes el coste de la garantía en <code>total_price</code>.</span><span class="l-en">With an external gateway, you include the guarantee cost in <code>total_price</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="13-3"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Solo muestras propinas activas y preseleccionas la de <code>is_default</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You only show active tips and preselect the <code>is_default</code> one.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="13-4"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">El comprador puede reservar sin propina.</span><span class="l-en">The buyer can book without a tip.</span></label></li></ul></div></div></article></div></div><div class="panel has-side" role="tabpanel" id="tab-postventa" data-tab="postventa" hidden><aside class="side"><nav class="side-nav"><a href="#flujo-14"><span class="num">14</span><span class="l-es">Reembolsos</span><span class="l-en">Refunds</span></a><a href="#flujo-15"><span class="num">15</span><span class="l-es">Consulta y conciliación</span><span class="l-en">Lookup and reconciliation</span></a><a href="#flujo-16"><span class="num">16</span><span class="l-es">Webhooks</span><span class="l-en">Webhooks</span></a></nav></aside><div class="panel-main"><div class="tab-head"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Postventa y notificaciones</span><span class="l-en">Post-sale and notifications</span></div><h2 class="tab-h"><span class="l-es">Reembolsos, conciliación y webhooks</span><span class="l-en">Refunds, reconciliation and webhooks</span></h2></div><section class="card intro"><div class="note info"><span class="note-i" aria-hidden="true">i</span><p><span class="l-es">Webhooks (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-16">flujo 16</a>): imprescindibles. Confirman los pagos con la pasarela de Fourvenues y avisan de los reembolsos que debes procesar con pasarela externa.</span><span class="l-en">Webhooks (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-16">flow 16</a>): required. They confirm payments with the Fourvenues payment gateway and notify you of refunds you must process with an external gateway.</span></p></div></section><article class="card flow" id="flujo-14" data-flow="14"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow</span> <b>14</b></div><h2><span class="l-es">Reembolsos</span><span class="l-en">Refunds</span></h2><div class="flow-body"><p><span class="l-es">Quién devuelve el dinero depende de quién lo cobró. Si vendiste con tu pasarela, devuelves tú el dinero y lo registras en Fourvenues con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-put">PUT</span>/tickets/{id}/refund</code>. Si vendiste con la pasarela de Fourvenues, el reembolso lo gestiona el venue desde FV Pro.</span><span class="l-en">Who refunds depends on who charged. If you sold with your gateway, you refund and record it in Fourvenues with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-put">PUT</span>/tickets/{id}/refund</code>. If you sold with the Fourvenues gateway, the venue handles the refund from FV Pro.</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Venta</span><span class="l-en">Sale</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Quién devuelve el dinero</span><span class="l-en">Who refunds</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué haces tú</span><span class="l-en">What you do</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="l-es">Tickets con pasarela externa (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-5">flujo 5</a>)</span><span class="l-en">Tickets with external gateway (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-5">flow 5</a>)</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Tú, en tu pasarela</span><span class="l-en">You, on your gateway</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Registras el reembolso con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-put">PUT</span>/tickets/{id}/refund</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You record the refund with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-put">PUT</span>/tickets/{id}/refund</code>.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Tickets con pasarela de Fourvenues (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flujo 4</a>)</span><span class="l-en">Tickets with Fourvenues gateway (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flow 4</a>)</span></td><td><span class="l-es">El venue, desde FV Pro</span><span class="l-en">The venue, from FV Pro</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Nada obligatorio. Puedes escuchar el webhook de reembolso o consultar la entrada para reflejarlo en tu plataforma.</span><span class="l-en">Nothing required. You can listen to the refund webhook or fetch the ticket to reflect it on your platform.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Reservas (flujos 6 y 7)</span><span class="l-en">Bookings (flows 6 and 7)</span></td><td><span class="l-es">El venue, desde FV Pro</span><span class="l-en">The venue, from FV Pro</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Consultas el estado de la reserva.</span><span class="l-en">You check the booking status.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Listas (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-8">flujo 8</a>)</span><span class="l-en">Lists (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-8">flow 8</a>)</span></td><td><span class="l-es">No aplica: no hay pago</span><span class="l-en">Not applicable: no payment</span></td><td>—</td></tr></tbody></table></div><h3 id="flujo-14-s1" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 1. Devuelve el dinero en tu pasarela</span><span class="l-en">Step 1. Refund on your gateway</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Solo para entradas vendidas con pasarela externa. Las ventas con pasarela de Fourvenues las reembolsa el venue desde FV Pro: no uses este endpoint para ellas. Haz primero la devolución en tu pasarela y, cuando se confirme, regístrala en Fourvenues.</span><span class="l-en">Only for tickets sold with an external gateway. Sales with the Fourvenues gateway are refunded by the venue from FV Pro: don't use this endpoint for them. Refund on your gateway first and, once confirmed, record it in Fourvenues.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-14-s2" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 2. Registra el reembolso</span><span class="l-en">Step 2. Record the refund</span></h3><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">cURL</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> PUT \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/tickets/tk_1/refund'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'Content-Type: application/json'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--data</span> '{ <span class="t-k">"amount"</span>: <span class="t-n">13.2</span> }'</code></pre></div><ul><li><span class="l-es"><code>amount</code> es el importe devuelto en esta operación, en la moneda de la entrada.</span><span class="l-en"><code>amount</code> is the amount refunded in this operation, in the ticket's currency.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Puedes registrar reembolsos parciales varias veces, hasta completar el importe de la entrada.</span><span class="l-en">You can record partial refunds several times, up to the ticket amount.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">La respuesta devuelve la entrada actualizada, con <code>refunded</code> (total devuelto) y <code>refunds</code> (cada devolución, con <code>amount</code> y fecha <code>at</code>).</span><span class="l-en">The response returns the updated ticket, with <code>refunded</code> (total refunded) and <code>refunds</code> (each refund, with <code>amount</code> and date <code>at</code>).</span></li></ul><div class="note warn"><span class="note-i" aria-hidden="true">!</span><p><span class="l-es"><strong>Importante: el primer reembolso anula la entrada.</strong> Con la primera llamada, aunque sea parcial, la entrada pasa a <code>voided</code> y deja de ser válida para entrar. No uses este endpoint para devolver solo una parte (por ejemplo, un complemento) si el asistente tiene que poder entrar.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Important: the first refund voids the ticket.</strong> With the first call, even if partial, the ticket moves to <code>voided</code> and is no longer valid for entry. Don't use this endpoint to refund only part (for example, an add-on) if the attendee still needs to get in.</span></p></div><h3 id="flujo-14-s3" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 3. Atiende las solicitudes de reembolso</span><span class="l-en">Step 3. Handle refund requests</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Cuando hay que devolver el dinero de una entrada, Fourvenues envía el webhook <code>ticket.request_refund</code> (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-16">flujo 16</a>) con <code>ticket_id</code>, <code>qr_code</code>, <code>amount_to_refund</code> y <code>currency</code>. Si tienes que procesarlo depende de quién cobró:</span><span class="l-en">When money has to be returned for a ticket, Fourvenues sends the <code>ticket.request_refund</code> webhook (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-16">flow 16</a>) with <code>ticket_id</code>, <code>qr_code</code>, <code>amount_to_refund</code> and <code>currency</code>. Whether you must process it depends on who took the payment:</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Venta</span><span class="l-en">Sale</span></th><th><span class="l-es">¿Hay que procesarlo?</span><span class="l-en">Must you process it?</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué haces</span><span class="l-en">What you do</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="l-es">Pasarela externa (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-5">flujo 5</a>)</span><span class="l-en">External gateway (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-5">flow 5</a>)</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Sí, obligatorio</span><span class="l-en">Yes, required</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Devuelves el dinero en tu pasarela y registras el reembolso en Fourvenues.</span><span class="l-en">You refund the money on your gateway and record the refund in Fourvenues.</span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Pasarela de Fourvenues (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flujo 4</a>)</span><span class="l-en">Fourvenues payment gateway (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flow 4</a>)</span></td><td><span class="l-es">No, opcional</span><span class="l-en">No, optional</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Fourvenues procesa la devolución sin que intervengas. Puedes usar el aviso para actualizar tu plataforma.</span><span class="l-en">Fourvenues processes the refund without your involvement. You can use the notice to update your platform.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es">Con pasarela externa, sigue estos pasos:</span><span class="l-en">With an external gateway, follow these steps:</span></p><ol><li><span class="l-es">Verifica la firma del webhook.</span><span class="l-en">Verify the webhook signature.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Devuelve <code>amount_to_refund</code> al comprador en tu pasarela.</span><span class="l-en">Refund <code>amount_to_refund</code> to the buyer on your gateway.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Registra el reembolso con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-put">PUT</span>/tickets/{ticket_id}/refund</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Record the refund with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-put">PUT</span>/tickets/{ticket_id}/refund</code>.</span></li></ol><h3 id="flujo-14-s4" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 4. Refleja los reembolsos que hace el venue</span><span class="l-en">Step 4. Reflect refunds made by the venue</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Para ventas con pasarela de Fourvenues y para reservas, consulta la entrada (<code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets/{id}</code>) o la reserva (<code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/bookings/{bookingId}</code>). Una entrada reembolsada aparece como <code>voided</code> y con <code>refunded</code> mayor que cero. Una reserva cancelada o rechazada cambia de <code>status</code> (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-6">flujo 6</a>).</span><span class="l-en">For sales with the Fourvenues gateway and for bookings, query the ticket (<code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets/{id}</code>) or booking (<code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/bookings/{bookingId}</code>). A refunded ticket appears as <code>voided</code> with <code>refunded</code> greater than zero. A cancelled or rejected booking changes <code>status</code> (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-6">flow 6</a>).</span></p><h3 id="flujo-14-s5" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Errores habituales</span><span class="l-en">Common errors</span></h3><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Error</span><span class="l-en">Error</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Causa</span><span class="l-en">Cause</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué hacer</span><span class="l-en">What to do</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><span class="l-es">El importe supera lo que queda por devolver, o no es válido</span><span class="l-en">The amount exceeds what remains to be refunded, or is invalid</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Revisa <code>refunded</code> en la entrada antes de volver a llamar.</span><span class="l-en">Check <code>refunded</code> on the ticket before calling again.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span class="l-es">La entrada no existe o no es visible para tu canal</span><span class="l-en">The ticket doesn't exist or isn't visible to your channel</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Revisa el id de la entrada.</span><span class="l-en">Check the ticket id.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="check" data-flow="14"><div class="check-h"><span class="l-es">Checklist</span><span class="l-en">Checklist</span><span class="check-n" data-count="14"></span></div><ul><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="14-0"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Solo usas <code class="ep"><span class="m m-put">PUT</span>/tickets/{id}/refund</code> en entradas vendidas con tu pasarela.</span><span class="l-en">You only use <code class="ep"><span class="m m-put">PUT</span>/tickets/{id}/refund</code> on tickets sold with your gateway.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="14-1"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Registras el reembolso después de confirmar la devolución en tu pasarela.</span><span class="l-en">You record the refund after confirming it on your gateway.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="14-2"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Sabes que el primer reembolso anula la entrada.</span><span class="l-en">You know the first refund voids the ticket.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="14-3"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Con pasarela externa, procesas <code>ticket.request_refund</code>: devuelves el dinero y lo registras.</span><span class="l-en">With an external gateway, you process <code>ticket.request_refund</code>: you refund the money and record it.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="14-4"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Reflejas en tu plataforma los reembolsos y cancelaciones que hace el venue.</span><span class="l-en">You reflect on your platform the refunds and cancellations made by the venue.</span></label></li></ul></div></div></article><article class="card flow" id="flujo-15" data-flow="15"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow</span> <b>15</b></div><h2><span class="l-es">Consulta y conciliación</span><span class="l-en">Lookup and reconciliation</span></h2><div class="flow-body"><p><span class="l-es">Concilia periódicamente tus pedidos con lo que hay en Fourvenues: pagos, entradas, reservas y listas. Así detectas webhooks que no llegaron, compras que fallaron a medias y reembolsos que hizo el venue.</span><span class="l-en">Periodically reconcile your orders with what is in Fourvenues: payments, tickets, bookings and lists. This way you catch webhooks that didn't arrive, half-failed purchases and refunds the venue made.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-15-s1" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Qué puedes consultar</span><span class="l-en">What you can query</span></h3><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Recurso</span><span class="l-en">Resource</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Endpoints</span><span class="l-en">Endpoints</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Filtros</span><span class="l-en">Filters</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="l-es">Pagos</span><span class="l-en">Payments</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/payments</code> · <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/payments/{id}</code></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>organization_id</code>, <code>event_id</code>, <code>start_date</code>, <code>end_date</code>, <code>limit</code> (50 por defecto), <code>offset</code></span><span class="l-en"><code>organization_id</code>, <code>event_id</code>, <code>start_date</code>, <code>end_date</code>, <code>limit</code> (50 by default), <code>offset</code></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Entradas</span><span class="l-en">Tickets</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets</code> · <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets/{id}</code> · <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets/by-code/{code}</code></td><td><code>event_id</code>, <code>ticket_rate_id</code>, <code>limit</code>, <code>offset</code></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Reservas</span><span class="l-en">Bookings</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/bookings</code> · <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/bookings/{bookingId}</code></td><td><code>event_id</code>, <code>limit</code>, <code>offset</code></td></tr><tr><td><span class="l-es">Listas</span><span class="l-en">Lists</span></td><td><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/lists</code> · <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/lists/{id}</code> · <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/lists/by-code/{code}</code></td><td><code>event_id</code>, <code>list_rate_id</code></td></tr></tbody></table></div><h3 id="flujo-15-s2" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Campos para conciliar</span><span class="l-en">Fields for reconciliation</span></h3><p><span class="l-es"><strong>Pagos</strong> (ventas con pasarela de Fourvenues):</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Payments</strong> (sales with the Fourvenues gateway):</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué es</span><span class="l-en">What it is</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>status</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Estado del pago (por ejemplo, pending).</span><span class="l-en">Payment status (for example, pending).</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>resource_type</code>, <code>resource_ids</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Qué se ha pagado (<code>ticket</code>, <code>booking</code>, <code>list</code>…) y los ids.</span><span class="l-en">What was paid (<code>ticket</code>, <code>booking</code>, <code>list</code>…) and the ids.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>total.amount</code>, <code>total.fees</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Importe total y gastos.</span><span class="l-en">Total amount and fees.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>currency</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Moneda.</span><span class="l-en">Currency.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>metadata</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Lo que enviaste al crear el checkout. Tu identificador de pedido.</span><span class="l-en">What you sent when creating the checkout. Your order identifier.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>paid_at</code>, <code>expires_at</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Fecha de pago y de caducidad de la sesión.</span><span class="l-en">Payment date and session expiry date.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><span class="l-es"><strong>Entradas:</strong></span><span class="l-en"><strong>Tickets:</strong></span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Campo</span><span class="l-en">Field</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué es</span><span class="l-en">What it is</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>status</code></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>active</code>, <code>voided</code>, <code>pending_payment</code>… (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flujo 4</a>).</span><span class="l-en"><code>active</code>, <code>voided</code>, <code>pending_payment</code>… (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flow 4</a>).</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>total_price</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Importe total de la entrada con todos los extras.</span><span class="l-en">Total ticket amount with all extras.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>total_fees</code>, <code>total_supplements</code>, <code>supplements_service_charge_total</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Desglose de gastos y complementos.</span><span class="l-en">Fee and add-on breakdown.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>discount_amount</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Descuento aplicado.</span><span class="l-en">Discount applied.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>warranty.total</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Coste de la garantía, si se compró.</span><span class="l-en">Guarantee cost, if bought.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>refunded</code>, <code>refunds</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Total devuelto y detalle de cada devolución.</span><span class="l-en">Total refunded and detail of each refund.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>for</code>, <code>enter</code>, <code>entry_time</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Personas que pueden entrar, personas que han entrado y hora de entrada.</span><span class="l-en">People allowed in, people who entered and entry time.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><h3 id="flujo-15-s3" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Rutina recomendada</span><span class="l-en">Recommended routine</span></h3><ol><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Cada día, por evento con ventas:</strong> recorre <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/payments?event_id=...</code> y comprueba que cada pago pagado tiene su pedido confirmado en tu sistema. Si falta alguno, es un webhook que no llegó: procesa el pago como si hubiera llegado.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Every day, per event with sales:</strong> go through <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/payments?event_id=...</code> and check each paid payment has its order confirmed in your system. If one is missing, it's a webhook that didn't arrive: process the payment as if it had.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Pedidos pendientes:</strong> para cada pedido que siga pendiente en tu sistema, consulta <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/payments/{payment_id}</code>. Si la sesión ha caducado (<code>expires_at</code>) sin pagarse, cancela el pedido.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Pending orders:</strong> for each order still pending in your system, query <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/payments/{payment_id}</code>. If the session expired (<code>expires_at</code>) unpaid, cancel the order.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Reembolsos:</strong> compara <code>refunded</code> de tus entradas con los reembolsos de tu sistema, para reflejar los que haya hecho el venue.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>Refunds:</strong> compare your tickets' <code>refunded</code> with the refunds in your system, to reflect any the venue made.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><strong>Después del evento:</strong> <code>enter</code> y <code>entry_time</code> te dan la asistencia real de cada entrada y lista.</span><span class="l-en"><strong>After the event:</strong> <code>enter</code> and <code>entry_time</code> give you actual attendance for each ticket and list.</span></li></ol><p><span class="l-es">Pagina siempre con <code>offset</code> hasta el final y respeta el límite de 10 peticiones por segundo (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-1">flujo 1</a>).</span><span class="l-en">Always paginate with <code>offset</code> to the end and respect the 10 requests per second limit (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-1">flow 1</a>).</span></p><div class="check" data-flow="15"><div class="check-h"><span class="l-es">Checklist</span><span class="l-en">Checklist</span><span class="check-n" data-count="15"></span></div><ul><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="15-0"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Tienes un proceso diario de conciliación por evento.</span><span class="l-en">You have a daily reconciliation process per event.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="15-1"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Recuperas los pagos cuyo webhook no llegó.</span><span class="l-en">You recover payments whose webhook didn't arrive.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="15-2"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Cancelas los pedidos cuya sesión de pago ha caducado.</span><span class="l-en">You cancel orders whose payment session expired.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="15-3"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Reflejas en tu sistema los reembolsos hechos por el venue.</span><span class="l-en">You reflect in your system the refunds made by the venue.</span></label></li></ul></div></div></article><article class="card flow" id="flujo-16" data-flow="16"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Flujo</span><span class="l-en">Flow</span> <b>16</b></div><h2><span class="l-es">Webhooks</span><span class="l-en">Webhooks</span></h2><div class="flow-body"><p><span class="l-es">Los webhooks confirman los pagos y avisan de las solicitudes de reembolso. Son la única confirmación fiable de un pago: no des una compra por buena solo porque el comprador ha vuelto a tu <code>redirect_url</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Webhooks confirm payments and notify refund requests. They are the only reliable payment confirmation: don't consider a purchase complete just because the buyer returned to your <code>redirect_url</code>.</span></p><ol><li><span class="l-es">Tu backend crea el checkout (<code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets/checkout</code>) y recibe una <code>payment_url</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Your backend creates the checkout (<code class="ep"><span class="m m-post">POST</span>/tickets/checkout</code>) and receives a <code>payment_url</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Rediriges al comprador a la <code>payment_url</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You redirect the buyer to the <code>payment_url</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">El comprador paga en Fourvenues.</span><span class="l-en">The buyer pays on Fourvenues.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Fourvenues envía el webhook <code>payment.success</code> a tu endpoint.</span><span class="l-en">Fourvenues sends the <code>payment.success</code> webhook to your endpoint.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Tu backend verifica la firma, guarda el evento y responde <code>200</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Your backend verifies the signature, stores the event and responds <code>200</code>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Fourvenues redirige al comprador a tu <code>redirect_url</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Fourvenues redirects the buyer to your <code>redirect_url</code>.</span></li></ol><div class="note info"><span class="note-i" aria-hidden="true">i</span><p><span class="l-es">El orden entre el webhook y la vuelta del comprador a <code>redirect_url</code> no está garantizado. Tu página de confirmación debe poder mostrar «pago en proceso» hasta que llegue el webhook.</span><span class="l-en">The order between the webhook and the buyer's return to <code>redirect_url</code> is not guaranteed. Your confirmation page must be able to show "payment in progress" until the webhook arrives.</span></p></div><h3 id="flujo-16-s1" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 1. Registra tu endpoint</span><span class="l-en">Step 1. Register your endpoint</span></h3><div class="code pair" data-pair="2"><div class="code-bar"><div class="seg" role="tablist"><button type="button" class="on" data-show="req"><span class="l-es">Petición</span><span class="l-en">Request</span></button><button type="button" data-show="res"><span class="l-es">Respuesta</span><span class="l-en">Response</span></button></div><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre data-pane="req"><code><span class="t-kw">curl</span> <span class="t-f">--request</span> POST \
  <span class="t-f">--url</span> <span class="t-s">'https://channels-service-alpha.fourvenues.com/webhooks/endpoints'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'X-Api-Key: TU_API_KEY'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--header</span> <span class="t-s">'Content-Type: application/json'</span> \
  <span class="t-f">--data</span> '{ <span class="t-k">"name"</span>: <span class="t-s">"Producción"</span>, <span class="t-k">"url"</span>: <span class="t-s">"https://tuapp.com/webhooks/fourvenues"</span> }'</code></pre><pre data-pane="res" hidden><code>{
  <span class="t-k">"success"</span>: <span class="t-kw">true</span>,
  <span class="t-k">"data"</span>: { <span class="t-k">"_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"wh_123"</span>, <span class="t-k">"name"</span>: <span class="t-s">"Producción"</span>, <span class="t-k">"url"</span>: <span class="t-s">"https://tuapp.com/webhooks/fourvenues"</span>, <span class="t-k">"sign_secret"</span>: <span class="t-s">"..."</span> }
}</code></pre></div><ul><li><span class="l-es">Guarda <code>sign_secret</code> como secreto en cuanto lo recibas. Lo necesitas para verificar cada evento.</span><span class="l-en">Store <code>sign_secret</code> as a secret as soon as you receive it. You need it to verify every event.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">El endpoint recibe todos los eventos de tu canal. <code>name</code> es solo una etiqueta y no filtra nada.</span><span class="l-en">The endpoint receives all events for your channel. <code>name</code> is just a label and filters nothing.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">La URL tiene que ser HTTPS y pública. Para probar en local, expón tu servidor con una herramienta como <a href="https://ngrok.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">ngrok</a>.</span><span class="l-en">The URL must be HTTPS and public. To test locally, expose your server with a tool such as <a href="https://ngrok.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">ngrok</a>.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/webhooks/endpoints</code> lista tus endpoints y <code class="ep"><span class="m m-delete">DELETE</span>/webhooks/endpoints/{id}</code> borra uno.</span><span class="l-en"><code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/webhooks/endpoints</code> lists your endpoints and <code class="ep"><span class="m m-delete">DELETE</span>/webhooks/endpoints/{id}</code> deletes one.</span></li></ul><h3 id="flujo-16-s2" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 2. Verifica la firma de cada evento</span><span class="l-en">Step 2. Verify each event's signature</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Cada evento llega como un POST con JSON y dos cabeceras:</span><span class="l-en">Each event arrives as a JSON POST with two headers:</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Cabecera</span><span class="l-en">Header</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Contenido</span><span class="l-en">Content</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>X-Webhook-Id</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Identificador único del evento.</span><span class="l-en">Unique event identifier.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>X-Webhook-Signature</code></td><td><span class="l-es">HMAC-SHA256 en hexadecimal del cuerpo de la petición, firmado con tu <code>sign_secret</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Hex HMAC-SHA256 of the request body, signed with your <code>sign_secret</code>.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">Node.js</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code><span class="t-kw">const</span> crypto = <span class="t-kw">require</span>(<span class="t-s">'crypto'</span>);
<span class="t-kw">const</span> express = <span class="t-kw">require</span>(<span class="t-s">'express'</span>);
<span class="t-kw">const</span> app = express();

app.post(<span class="t-s">'/webhooks/fourvenues'</span>, express.raw({ type: <span class="t-s">'application/json'</span> }), (req, res) =&gt; {
  <span class="t-kw">const</span> signature = req.header(<span class="t-s">'X-Webhook-Signature'</span>) || <span class="t-s">''</span>;
  <span class="t-kw">const</span> expected = crypto
    .createHmac(<span class="t-s">'sha256'</span>, process.env.FV_SIGN_SECRET)
    .update(req.body) <span class="t-c">// cuerpo tal cual llega, sin parsear</span>
    .digest(<span class="t-s">'hex'</span>);

  <span class="t-kw">const</span> valid = signature.length === expected.length &amp;&amp;
    crypto.timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(signature), Buffer.from(expected));
  <span class="t-kw">if</span> (!valid) <span class="t-kw">return</span> res.status(<span class="t-n">403</span>).send(<span class="t-s">'Invalid signature'</span>);

  <span class="t-kw">const</span> event = JSON.parse(req.body);
  queue.push(event); <span class="t-c">// procesa en segundo plano</span>
  res.status(<span class="t-n">200</span>).send();
});</code></pre></div><p><span class="l-es">Calcula la firma sobre el cuerpo tal cual llega. Si lo parseas antes y lo vuelves a serializar, la firma puede no coincidir. Rechaza con <code>403</code> cualquier evento con firma incorrecta y regístralo.</span><span class="l-en">Compute the signature over the body exactly as received. If you parse and re-serialize it first, the signature may not match. Reject any event with an incorrect signature with <code>403</code> and log it.</span></p><h3 id="flujo-16-s3" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 3. Procesa el evento</span><span class="l-en">Step 3. Process the event</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Todos los eventos tienen la misma estructura: <code>id</code>, <code>event</code> (tipo) y <code>payload</code> (datos del evento).</span><span class="l-en">All events share the same structure: <code>id</code>, <code>event</code> (type) and <code>payload</code> (event data).</span></p><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Evento</span><span class="l-en">Event</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Cuándo llega</span><span class="l-en">When it arrives</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Campos del payload</span><span class="l-en">Payload fields</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>payment.success</code></td><td><span class="l-es">El comprador ha completado el pago de un checkout.</span><span class="l-en">The buyer completed a checkout payment.</span></td><td><span class="l-es"><code>payment_id</code>, <code>resource_type</code>, <code>resource_ids</code>, <code>metadata</code> (la que enviaste en el checkout), <code>send_resources</code></span><span class="l-en"><code>payment_id</code>, <code>resource_type</code>, <code>resource_ids</code>, <code>metadata</code> (what you sent in the checkout), <code>send_resources</code></span></td></tr><tr><td><code>ticket.request_refund</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Hay que reembolsar una entrada. Obligatorio procesarlo si vendiste con pasarela externa; opcional con la pasarela de Fourvenues (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-14">flujo 14</a>).</span><span class="l-en">A ticket must be refunded. Required to process if you sold with an external gateway; optional with the Fourvenues payment gateway (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-14">flow 14</a>).</span></td><td><code>ticket_id</code>, <code>qr_code</code>, <code>event_id</code>, <code>client_name</code>, <code>client_email</code>, <code>amount_to_refund</code>, <code>currency</code>, <code>resource_type</code></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="code"><div class="code-bar"><span class="code-lang">JSON</span><button class="copy" type="button"><span class="l-es">Copiar</span><span class="l-en">Copy</span></button></div><pre><code>{
  <span class="t-k">"id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"5f7b1b3b-7b7b-4b7b-8b7b-9b7b1b3b7b7b"</span>,
  <span class="t-k">"event"</span>: <span class="t-s">"payment.success"</span>,
  <span class="t-k">"payload"</span>: {
    <span class="t-k">"payment_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"pay_123"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"resource_type"</span>: <span class="t-s">"ticket"</span>,
    <span class="t-k">"resource_ids"</span>: [<span class="t-s">"tk_1"</span>, <span class="t-s">"tk_2"</span>],
    <span class="t-k">"metadata"</span>: { <span class="t-k">"order_id"</span>: <span class="t-s">"tu-pedido-987"</span> },
    <span class="t-k">"send_resources"</span>: <span class="t-kw">true</span>
  }
}</code></pre></div><ul><li><span class="l-es">Usa <code>metadata</code> para enlazar el pago con tu pedido. Envía tu identificador interno al crear el checkout (máximo 1 KB, sin datos personales).</span><span class="l-en">Use <code>metadata</code> to link the payment to your order. Send your internal identifier when creating the checkout (max 1 KB, no personal data).</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Con <code>resource_ids</code>, consulta cada recurso (p. ej. <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets/{id}</code>) para obtener el QR y el estado final.</span><span class="l-en">With <code>resource_ids</code>, query each resource (e.g. <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets/{id}</code>) to get the QR and final status.</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><code>resource_type</code> indica qué se ha pagado. Si llega un valor que no reconoces, responde <code>200</code> y regístralo.</span><span class="l-en"><code>resource_type</code> indicates what was paid. If you get a value you don't recognize, respond <code>200</code> and log it.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Responde <code>200</code> también a los tipos de evento que no uses. Pueden añadirse eventos nuevos.</span><span class="l-en">Also respond <code>200</code> to event types you don't use. New events may be added.</span></li></ul><h3 id="flujo-16-s4" class="sub"><span class="l-es">Paso 4. Reintentos e idempotencia</span><span class="l-en">Step 4. Retries and idempotency</span></h3><p><span class="l-es">Si tu endpoint no responde con un 2xx, Fourvenues reintenta el envío hasta 40 veces. La espera antes del reintento n es n^1,5 segundos más hasta 1 segundo aleatorio. En total los reintentos cubren unos 70 minutos. Después, el evento queda marcado como fallido.</span><span class="l-en">If your endpoint doesn't respond with a 2xx, Fourvenues retries delivery up to 40 times. The wait before retry n is n^1.5 seconds plus up to 1 random second. In total retries cover about 70 minutes. After that, the event is marked as failed.</span></p><ul><li><span class="l-es">Responde en menos de 5 segundos. Encola el trabajo y procésalo aparte.</span><span class="l-en">Respond in under 5 seconds. Queue the work and process it separately.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Un mismo evento puede llegar más de una vez. Guarda el <code>id</code> de cada evento procesado e ignora los repetidos.</span><span class="l-en">The same event can arrive more than once. Store each processed event's <code>id</code> and ignore duplicates.</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Si tu sistema ha estado caído más tiempo, concilia con <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/payments</code> y <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets</code>.</span><span class="l-en">If your system was down longer, reconcile with <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/payments</code> and <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/tickets</code>.</span></li></ul><div class="check" data-flow="16"><div class="check-h"><span class="l-es">Checklist</span><span class="l-en">Checklist</span><span class="check-n" data-count="16"></span></div><ul><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="16-0"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Endpoint registrado en alpha y <code>sign_secret</code> guardado como secreto.</span><span class="l-en">Endpoint registered in alpha and <code>sign_secret</code> stored as a secret.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="16-1"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Verificas la firma sobre el cuerpo sin parsear y rechazas con <code>403</code> las incorrectas.</span><span class="l-en">You verify the signature over the unparsed body and reject incorrect ones with procesas en segundo plano.</span><span class="l-en">You respond <code>200</code> in under 5 seconds and process in the background.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="16-3"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Deduplicas por <code>id</code> del evento.</span><span class="l-en">You deduplicate by event <code>id</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="16-4"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Enlazas cada pago con tu pedido mediante <code>metadata</code>.</span><span class="l-en">You link each payment to your order via <code>metadata</code>.</span></label></li><li><label><input type="checkbox" data-ck="16-5"><span class="box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="l-es">Tu página de confirmación funciona aunque el webhook llegue después que el comprador.</span><span class="l-en">Your confirmation page works even if the Con <code>429</code>, backoff exponencial (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-1">flujo 1</a>).</span><span class="l-en">Max 10 requests per second per key. On <code>429</code>, exponential backoff (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-1">flow 1</a>).</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><code>limit</code> es 50 por defecto en <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/events</code> y <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/payments</code>. Pagina con <code>offset</code> hasta el final (flujos 2 y 15).</span><span class="l-en"><code>limit</code> defaults to 50 in <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/events</code> and <code class="ep"><span class="m m-get">GET</span>/payments</code>. Paginate with <code>offset</code> to the end (flows 2 and 15).</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><code>radius</code> en búsquedas por ubicación: km, máximo 500 (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-2">flujo 2</a>).</span><span class="l-en"><code>radius</code> in location searches: km, max 500 (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-2">flow 2</a>).</span></li><li><span class="l-es"><code>metadata</code>: máximo 1 KB, sin datos personales (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flujo 4</a>).</span><span class="l-en"><code>metadata</code>: max 1 KB, no personal data (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-4">flow 4</a>).</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Importes en unidades de la moneda, no en céntimos, con los decimales ISO 4217 de cada <code>currency</code> (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flujo 3</a>).</span><span class="l-en">Amounts in currency units, not cents, with each <code>currency</code>'s ISO 4217 decimals (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-3">flow 3</a>).</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Webhooks: responde en menos de 5 segundos. Hasta 40 reintentos durante unos 70 minutos (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-16">flujo 16</a>).</span><span class="l-en">Webhooks: respond in under 5 seconds. Up to 40 retries over about 70 minutes (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-16">flow 16</a>).</span></li><li><span class="l-es">Las keys caducan y son de un solo entorno. El enlace de la key solo se abre una vez (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-1">flujo 1</a>).</span><span class="l-en">Keys expire and belong to one environment. The key link opens only once (<a class="xref" href="#flujo-1">flow 1</a>).</span></li></ul></section><section class="card" id="ref-errores"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Códigos de error</span><span class="l-en">Error codes</span></div><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Código</span><span class="l-en">Code</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Causa</span><span class="l-en">Cause</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Qué hacer</span><span class="l-en">What to do</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Falta la cabecera o la key no es válida</span><span class="l-en">The header is missing or the key is invalid</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Revisa el nombre <code>X-Api-Key</code>, el valor y que usas la key del entorno correcto.</span><span class="l-en">Check the <code>X-Api-Key</code> name, the value, and that you are using the key for the right environment.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>403</code></td><td><span class="l-es">La key no tiene permiso para ese endpoint o ese venue</span><span class="l-en">The key has no permission for that endpoint or venue</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Pide ampliar las capabilities o confirma que el venue te tiene como partner.</span><span class="l-en">Ask for extra capabilities or confirm that the venue has you as a partner.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span class="l-es">El recurso no existe o no es visible para tu canal</span><span class="l-en">The resource does not exist or is not visible to your channel</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Revisa el <code>_id</code> o el <code>slug</code>.</span><span class="l-en">Check the <code>_id</code> or <code>slug</code>.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>429</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Has superado el límite de peticiones</span><span class="l-en">You exceeded the request limit</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Backoff exponencial.</span><span class="l-en">Exponential backoff.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>500</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Error inesperado</span><span class="l-en">Unexpected error</span></td><td><span class="l-es">Reintenta una vez. Si se repite, contacta con soporte.</span><span class="l-en">Retry once. If it repeats, contact support.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div></section><section class="card" id="ref-estados-t"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Estados de la entrada</span><span class="l-en">Ticket statuses</span></div><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Estado</span><span class="l-en">Status</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Significado</span><span class="l-en">Meaning</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>pending_payment</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Creada, esperando el pago.</span><span class="l-en">Created, awaiting payment.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>processing</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Pago en curso.</span><span class="l-en">Payment in progress.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>active</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Pagada y válida para entrar.</span><span class="l-en">Paid and valid for entry.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>voided</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Anulada (por ejemplo, tras un reembolso).</span><span class="l-en">Voided (for example, after a refund).</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div></section><section class="card" id="ref-estados-r"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Estados de una reserva</span><span class="l-en">Booking statuses</span></div><div class="t-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th><span class="l-es">Estado</span><span class="l-en">Status</span></th><th><span class="l-es">Significado</span><span class="l-en">Meaning</span></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>pending</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Creada, pendiente de completar.</span><span class="l-en">Created, pending completion.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>filled-by-client</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Datos completados, esperando un pago externo. Solo se da en reservas de venues propios.</span><span class="l-en">Data completed, awaiting an external payment. Only occurs in venues' own bookings.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>to-review</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Pendiente de que el venue la revise.</span><span class="l-en">Pending venue review.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>accepted</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Confirmada.</span><span class="l-en">Confirmed.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>rejected</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Rechazada por el venue.</span><span class="l-en">Rejected by the venue.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>cancelled-by-client</code></td><td><span class="l-es">Cancelada por el cliente.</span><span class="l-en">Cancelled by the customer.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>not-completed</code></td><td><span class="l-es">El proceso no se completó.</span><span class="l-en">The process was not completed.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>arrived</code>, <code>arrived-partial</code></td><td><span class="l-es">El grupo ha llegado al venue, entero o en parte.</span><span class="l-en">The group has arrived at the venue, fully or partly.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>seated</code>, <code>seated-partial</code></td><td><span class="l-es">El grupo está en la mesa, entero o en parte.</span><span class="l-en">The group is at the table, fully or partly.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>released</code></td><td><span class="l-es">La mesa se ha liberado.</span><span class="l-en">The table has been released.</span></td></tr><tr><td><code>no-show</code></td><td><span class="l-es">El grupo no se presentó.</span><span class="l-en">The group did not show up.</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div></section><section class="card" id="ref-webhooks"><div class="eyebrow"><span class="l-es">Eventos de webhook</span><span class="l-en">Webhook alt="Fourvenues">
    <h2><span class="l-es">¿Dudas con la integración?</span><span class="l-en">Questions about the integration?</span></h2>
    <p class="close-p"><span class="l-es">Escribe al equipo de integraciones de Fourvenues.</span><span class="l-en">Write to the Fourvenues integrations team.</span></p>
    <a class="cta" href="mailto:integrations@fourvenues.com">integrations@fourvenues.com</a>
  </div>
  <footer class="foot">© Fourvenues · Channel Manager API</footer>
</div>

<div class="search" id="search" hidden>
  <div class="search-box" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Buscar">
    <div class="search-in">
      <svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" width="17" height="17" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="8.5" cy="8.5" r="5.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"/><path d="M13 13l4 4" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>
      <input id="q" type="search" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false">
      <kbd>esc</kbd>
    </div>
    <div class="results" id="results"></div>
  </div>
</div>
<script>
(function(){
  var store = {
    get: function(k){ try { return localStorage.getItem(k); } catch(e){ return null; } },
    set: function(k,v){ try { localStorage.setItem(k,v); } catch(e){} },
    del: function(k){ try { localStorage.removeItem(k); } catch(e){} }
  };
  var root = document.documentElement;
  var S = document.getElementById('search'), Q = document.getElementById('q'), R = document.getElementById('results'), sel = 0;
  var tabs = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.tabs [role=tab]'));
  var panels = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.panel'));

  /* ---------- idioma ---------- */
  var PH = { es: 'Busca un endpoint, campo o error…', en: 'Search an endpoint, field or error…' };
  function setLang(l){
    if (l !== 'es' && l !== 'en') l = 'es';
    root.lang = l;
    document.querySelectorAll('.lang button').forEach(function(b){ b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.dataset.lang === l ? 'true' : 'false'); });
    Q.placeholder = PH[l];
    store.set('fv-cm-lang', l);
    try { var u = new URL(location.href); u.searchParams.set('lang', l); history.replaceState(null, '', u.toString()); } catch(e){}
    if (!S.hidden) runSearch();
  }
  var qp = null; try { qp = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('lang'); } catch(e){}
  setLang(qp || store.get('fv-cm-lang') || 'es');
  document.querySelectorAll('.lang button').forEach(function(b){ b.addEventListener('click', function(){ setLang(b.dataset.lang); }); });

  /* ---------- tabs y rutas ---------- */
  function showTab(id, push){
    var found = false;
    tabs.forEach(function(t){ var on = t.dataset.tab === id; t.setAttribute('aria-selected', on ? 'true':'false'); if (on) { found = true; t.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest', inline:'nearest'}); } });
    if (!found) return showTab('empezar', push);
    panels.forEach(function(p){ p.hidden = p.dataset.tab !== id; });
    observeSide();
  }
  function tabOf(el){ var p = el.closest('.panel'); return p ? p.dataset.tab : 'empezar'; }
  function goHash(hash, smooth){
    var id = (hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
    if (!id) { showTab('empezar'); return; }
    var tab = panels.filter(function(p){ return p.dataset.tab === id; })[0];
    if (tab) { showTab(id); window.scrollTo({ top: document.querySelector('.tabs').offsetTop - 70, behavior: smooth ? 'smooth':'auto' }); return; }
    var el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) { showTab(tabOf(el)); requestAnimationFrame(function(){ el.scrollIntoView({behavior: smooth ? 'smooth':'auto', block:'start'}); }); }
  }
  tabs.forEach(function(t){ t.addEventListener('click', function(){ history.replaceState(null,'', location.pathname + location.search + '#' + t.dataset.tab); showTab(t.dataset.tab); window.scrollTo({top: document.querySelector('.tabs').offsetTop - 70, behavior:'smooth'}); }); });
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var a = e.target.closest('a[href^="#"]');
    if (!a) return;
    var h = a.getAttribute('href');
    if (h.length < 2) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    history.replaceState(null, '', location.pathname + location.search + h);
    goHash(h, true);
    closeSearch();
  });
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function(){ goHash(location.hash, true); });
  // arrow keys on tablist
  document.querySelector('.tabs').addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
    var i = tabs.indexOf(document.activeElement); if (i < 0) return;
    if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
      e.preventDefault(); var n = tabs[(i + (e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : tabs.length - 1)) % tabs.length]; n.focus(); n.click();
    }
  });

  /* ---------- navegación lateral activa ---------- */
  var io = null;
  function observeSide(){
    if (io) io.disconnect();
    var p = panels.filter(function(x){ return !x.hidden; })[0]; if (!p) return;
    var links = [].slice.call(p.querySelectorAll('.side-nav a'));
    if (!links.length || !('IntersectionObserver' in window)) return;
    io = new IntersectionObserver(function(ents){
      ents.forEach(function(en){
        if (en.isIntersecting) {
          links.forEach(function(l){ l.classList.toggle('on', l.getAttribute('href') === '#' + en.target.id); });
        }
      });
    }, { rootMargin: '-35% 0px -60% 0px' });
    links.forEach(function(l){ var t = document.getElementById(l.getAttribute('href').slice(1)); if (t) io.observe(t); });
  }

  /* ---------- copiar ---------- */
  document.querySelectorAll('.code').forEach(function(box){
    var btn = box.querySelector('.copy');
    btn.addEventListener('click', function(){
      var pre = [].slice.call(box.querySelectorAll('pre')).filter(function(p){ return !p.hidden; })[0];
      var txt = pre.innerText;
      var done = function(){ btn.classList.add('ok'); setTimeout(function(){ btn.classList.remove('ok'); }, 1400); };
      if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt).then(done, function(){ fallback(txt); done(); });
      else { fallback(txt); done(); }
    });
    box.querySelectorAll('.seg button').forEach(function(b){
      b.addEventListener('click', function(){
        box.querySelectorAll('.seg button').forEach(function(x){ x.classList.toggle('on', x === b); });
        box.querySelectorAll('pre').forEach(function(p){ p.hidden = p.dataset.pane !== b.dataset.show; });
      });
    });
  });
  function fallback(t){ var a = document.createElement('textarea'); a.value = t; document.body.appendChild(a); a.select(); try { document.execCommand('copy'); } catch(e){} a.remove(); }

  /* ---------- checklists ---------- */
  var boxes = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('input[data-ck]'));
  function updateCounts(){
    var by = {};
    boxes.forEach(function(b){ var f = b.dataset.ck.split('-')[0]; by[f] = by[f] || [0,0]; by[f][1]++; if (b.checked) by[f][0]++; });
    Object.keys(by).forEach(function(f){
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-count="'+f+'"]').forEach(function(n){ n.textContent = by[f][0] + '/' + by[f][1]; });
      var bar = document.querySelector('[data-bar="'+f+'"]'); if (bar) bar.style.width = (100 * by[f][0] / by[f][1]) + '%';
    });
  }
  boxes.forEach(function(b){
    b.checked = store.get('fv-cm-ck-' + b.dataset.ck) === '1';
    b.addEventListener('change', function(){ if (b.checked) store.set('fv-cm-ck-' + b.dataset.ck, '1'); else store.del('fv-cm-ck-' + b.dataset.ck); updateCounts(); });
  });
  var reset = document.getElementById('reset-ck');
  if (reset) reset.addEventListener('click', function(){ boxes.forEach(function(b){ b.checked = false; store.del('fv-cm-ck-' + b.dataset.ck); }); updateCounts(); });
  updateCounts();

  /* ---------- ruta en Empezar ---------- */
  var route = document.querySelector('.route');
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-route]').forEach(function(b){
    b.addEventListener('click', function(){
      var on = b.getAttribute('aria-pressed') !== 'true';
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-route]').forEach(function(x){ x.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false'); });
      route.classList.toggle('filter', on);
      if (on) b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
      route.querySelectorAll('.route-g').forEach(function(g){ g.classList.toggle('hit', on && g.dataset.routes.split(' ').indexOf(b.dataset.route) > -1); });
    });
  });

  /* ---------- búsqueda ---------- */
  function textIn(el, l){
    var c = el.cloneNode(true);
    c.querySelectorAll('.l-' + (l === 'es' ? 'en' : 'es')).forEach(function(x){ x.remove(); });
    c.querySelectorAll('li,td,th,p,pre,h3').forEach(function(x){ x.append(' '); });
    return c.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  }
  var index = [];
  function buildIndex(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(function(panel){
      panel.querySelectorAll('.panel-main > .card').forEach(function(card){
        var flow = card.dataset.flow || '';
        var head = card.querySelector('h2') || card.querySelector('.eyebrow');
        var body = card.querySelector('.flow-body') || card;
        // trocear por h3
        var chunks = [], cur = { id: card.id, el: [] , h: null };
        [].slice.call(body.children).forEach(function(ch){
          if (ch.tagName === 'H3') { chunks.push(cur); cur = { id: ch.id, el: [], h: ch }; }
          else if (ch !== head && !ch.classList.contains('eyebrow')) cur.el.push(ch);
        });
        chunks.push(cur);
        chunks.forEach(function(c){
          if (!c.id) return;
          var e = { id: c.id, flow: flow, tab: panel.dataset.tab, es: {}, en: {} };
          ['es','en'].forEach(function(l){
            e[l].title = (head ? textIn(head, l) : '') + (c.h ? ' · ' + textIn(c.h, l) : '');
            e[l].body = c.el.map(function(x){ return textIn(x, l); }).join(' ');
          });
          index.push(e);
        });
      });
    });
  }
  buildIndex();
  function norm(s){ return s.toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, ''); }
  function esc(s){ return s.replace(/[&<>"]/g, function(c){ return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]; }); }
  function searchOpen(){ return !S.hidden; }
  function openSearch(){ S.hidden = false; Q.value = ''; runSearch(); setTimeout(function(){ Q.focus(); }, 10); }
  function closeSearch(){ S.hidden = true; }
  function runSearch(){
    var l = root.lang, q = norm(Q.value.trim()), out = [];
    var terms = q.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    if (!terms.length) { R.innerHTML = '<div class="res-empty">' + (l === 'es' ? 'Escribe para buscar en los 16 flujos.' : 'Type to search the 16 flows.') + '</div>'; return; }
    index.forEach(function(e){
      var t = norm(e[l].title), b = norm(e[l].body), score = 0, ok = true;
      terms.forEach(function(term){ var it = t.indexOf(term) > -1, ib = b.indexOf(term) > -1; if (!it && !ib) ok = false; score += (it ? 10 : 0) + (ib ? 1 : 0); });
      if (ok) out.push({ e: e, score: score });
    });
    out.sort(function(a,b){ return b.score - a.score; });
    out = out.slice(0, 30); sel = 0;
    if (!out.length) { R.innerHTML = '<div class="res-empty">' + (l === 'es' ? 'Sin resultados. Prueba con el nombre de un campo o un endpoint.' : 'No results. Try a field or endpoint name.') + '</div>'; return; }
    R.innerHTML = out.map(function(o, i){
      var e = o.e, body = e[l].body, nb = norm(body), p = nb.indexOf(terms[0]);
      var start = Math.max(0, p - 50), snip = (start > 0 ? '…' : '') + body.slice(start, start + 150);
      var sn = esc(snip);
      terms.forEach(function(term){
        var re = new RegExp('(' + term.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&') + ')', 'ig');
        sn = sn.replace(re, '<mark>$1</mark>');
      });
      var num = e.flow ? '<span class="num">' + ('0' + e.flow).slice(-2) + '</span>' : '';
      return '<a class="res' + (i === 0 ? ' on' : '') + '" href="#' + e.id + '"><div class="res-t">' + num + esc(e[l].title) + '</div><div class="res-s">' + sn + '</div></a>';
    }).join('');
  }
  Q.addEventListener('input', runSearch);
  Q.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
    var items = R.querySelectorAll('.res'); if (!items.length) return;
    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
      e.preventDefault(); items[sel].classList.remove('on');
      sel = (sel + (e.key === 'ArrowDown' ? 1 : items.length - 1)) % items.length;
      items[sel].classList.add('on'); items[sel].scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'});
    } else if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); items[sel].click(); }
  });
  document.getElementById('open-search').addEventListener('click', openSearch);
  S.addEventListener('click', function(e){ if (e.target === S) closeSearch(); });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
    if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'k') { e.preventDefault(); searchOpen() ? closeSearch() : openSearch(); }
    else if (e.key === 'Escape' && searchOpen()) closeSearch();
    else if (e.key === '/' && !searchOpen() && !/INPUT|TEXTAREA/.test(document.activeElement.tagName)) { e.preventDefault(); openSearch(); }
  });

  goHash(location.hash, false);
})();

</script>
